Generate a JSON-LD ontology schema for Oracle EBS Reporting Intelligence
including these entities:
- OracleModule (AP, AR, GL, PO, HRMS, INV, iExpense, SCM, Cash Management, Journals, GL)
- EBSReport (reportId, reportName, module, sql, columns)
- OracleTable (tableName, module, multiOrgFlag, primaryKey, indexes)
- BusinessRule (ruleId, ruleName, category, severity, description)
- SQLPattern (patternId, patternName, type, templateSQL)
Include relationships:
- module_has_report (OracleModule → EBSReport)
- report_uses_table (EBSReport → OracleTable)
- table_has_rule (OracleTable → BusinessRule)
- pattern_applies_to_table (SQLPattern → OracleTable)
- rule_applies_to_report (BusinessRule → EBSReport)
Save as: ica-schema/ebs-intelligence-ontology.jsonld
Generate a comprehensive JSON-LD ontology schema for 
Oracle EBS Enterprise Intelligence and Learning Platform.
FILE: ica-schema/ebs-complete-ontology.jsonld
Base namespace: http://ibm.com/oracle-ebs#
Prefix: ebs
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION A — FUNCTIONAL DOMAIN ENTITIES
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Entity 1: OracleModule
Attributes:
- moduleId: string (identity key)
- moduleCode: string
  Values: AP|AR|GL|CM|PO|SCM|INV|OM|HRMS|PAY|BEN|
          iEXP|FA|CE|WIP|BOM|MRP|CRP|OE|QA|WMS|
          PA|GMS|CST|XLA|FND|WF|AME|OAF|APEX|ADF|
          SOA|REDWOOD
- moduleName: string
- moduleCategory: 
  FINANCIAL|SUPPLY_CHAIN|HUMAN_RESOURCES|
  MANUFACTURING|TECHNOLOGY|INTEGRATION
- businessProcess: 
  P2P|O2C|H2R|R2R|MFG|PLAN_TO_PRODUCE
- releaseVersion: R12.1|R12.2
- licenseType: CORE|OPTIONAL|ADD_ON
- description: string
- keyResponsibilities: list of string
- commonSetupSteps: list of string
- technicalSchema: string (APPS/AP/AR/GL/HR/etc)
Entity 2: BusinessProcess
Attributes:
- processId: string (identity key)
- processCode: P2P|O2C|H2R|R2R|MFG|PLAN_TO_PRODUCE
- processName: string
- fullName: string
- description: string
- startEvent: string
- endEvent: string
- primaryModules: list of moduleCode
- secondaryModules: list of moduleCode
- keyMilestones: list of string
- keyDocuments: list of string
- keyTables: list of string
- integrationPoints: list of string
- typicalPainPoints: list of string
- kpiMetrics: list of string
Sample instances to include:
Instance P2P:
processCode: P2P
fullName: Procure-to-Pay
startEvent: Purchase Requisition Creation
endEvent: Supplier Payment Cleared
primaryModules: [PO, AP, INV]
secondaryModules: [GL, CM, PA]

  PR Created,

  PO Approved,
  Goods Receipt,

  Invoice Matched,
  Invoice Approved,
  Payment Processed,
  GL Entry Posted
]

  Payment
]
keyTables: [
  po_requisition_headers_all,
  po_headers_all,
  rcv_transactions,
  ap_invoices_all,
  ap_checks_all
]

fullName: Order-to-Cash
startEvent: Sales Order Creation
endEvent: Cash Receipt Applied

  Sales Order Created,
  Order Booked,

  Benefits Enrolled,

  Termination Processed,
  Final Pay Processed
]

SECTION B — RICE COMPONENT ENTITIES
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Entity 3: RICEComponent (base entity)
Attributes:
- componentId: string (identity key)
- componentName: string
- componentType: 
  REPORT|INTERFACE|CONVERSION|EXTENSION|
  FORM|WORKFLOW|AME|OAF|SOA|APEX|ADF|REDWOOD
- riceCategory: R|I|C|E
- module: string
- businessProcess: string
- complexity: LOW|MEDIUM|HIGH|VERY_HIGH
- estimatedDays: number
- customizationType: 
  STANDARD|CUSTOMIZATION|EXTENSION|NEW_DEVELOPMENT
- status: ACTIVE|DEPRECATED|UNDER_DEVELOPMENT|RETIRED
- technicalStack: list of string
- relatedTables: list of string
- relatedPackages: list of string
Entity 4: EBSReport
Parent: RICEComponent (riceCategory: R)
Attributes:
- reportId: string (identity key)
- reportName: string
- reportType: 
  RDF|BIP|XML_PUBLISHER|OAF_REPORT|
  DISCOVERER|ANALYTICS|PLSQL_REPORT
- concurrentProgramName: string
- reportShortName: string
- applicationShortName: string
- executableName: string
- executableMethod: 
  ORACLE_REPORTS|JAVA_CONCURRENT_PROGRAM|
  PL_SQL_STORED_PROCEDURE|HOST|PERL|SQL_PLUS
- outputFormat: PDF|EXCEL|HTML|CSV|XML|TEXT
- templateFile: string (for BIP)
- dataDefinition: string (for BIP)
- xmlDataSource: string
- businessPurpose: string
- primarySQLQuery: string
- parameters: list of parameterName
- keyTables: list of string
- orgIdRequired: boolean
- multiOrgEnabled: boolean
- performanceNotes: string
- schedulingNotes: string
- requiredResponsibility: string
Sample instances:
RDF Report Example:
reportName: Invoice Register
reportType: RDF
module: AP
concurrentProgramName: APXINREG
primarySQLQuery: |
  SELECT aia.invoice_num,
         aia.invoice_date,
         aia.invoice_amount,
         aia.amount_paid,
         aia.invoice_currency_code,
         pv.vendor_name,
         pvs.vendor_site_code,
         aia.description,
         aia.payment_status_flag,
         aia.approval_status
  FROM   ap_invoices_all aia,
         po_vendors pv,
         po_vendor_sites_all pvs
  WHERE  aia.vendor_id = pv.vendor_id
  AND    aia.vendor_site_id = pvs.vendor_site_id
    AND    aia.org_id = :p_org_id
  AND    aia.invoice_date BETWEEN :p_start_date AND :p_end_date
  ORDER BY pv.vendor_name, aia.invoice_num
BIP Report Example:
reportName: Payment Register
reportType: BIP
module: AP
concurrentProgramName: APXPAWKB
dataDefinition: AP_PAYMENT_REGISTER_DD
templateFile: AP_PAYMENT_REGISTER_TEMPLATE.rtf
primarySQLQuery: |
  SELECT aca.check_number,
         aca.check_date,
         aca.amount,
         aca.currency_code,
         aca.status_lookup_code,
         pv.vendor_name,
         pvs.vendor_site_code,
         aba.bank_account_name,
         aba.bank_account_num,
         aca.void_date,
         aca.cleared_date
  FROM   ap_checks_all aca,
         po_vendors pv,
         po_vendor_sites_all pvs,
         ap_bank_accounts_all aba
  WHERE  aca.vendor_id = pv.vendor_id
  AND    aca.vendor_site_id = pvs.vendor_site_id
  AND    aca.bank_account_id = aba.bank_account_id
  AND    aca.org_id = :p_org_id
  AND    aca.check_date BETWEEN :p_start_date AND :p_end_date
XML Publisher Report Example:
reportName: Trial Balance Report
reportType: XML_PUBLISHER
module: GL
concurrentProgramName: GLXTRBAL
dataDefinition: GL_TRIAL_BALANCE_DD
templateFile: GL_TRIAL_BALANCE.rtf
primarySQLQuery: |
  SELECT gcc.segment1,
         gcc.segment2,
         gcc.segment3,
         gcc.segment4,
         gcc.segment5,
         gb.period_name,
         gb.begin_balance_dr,
         gb.begin_balance_cr,
         gb.period_net_dr,
         gb.period_net_cr,
         (gb.begin_balance_dr - gb.begin_balance_cr +
          gb.period_net_dr - gb.period_net_cr) AS ending_balance
  FROM   gl_balances gb,
         gl_code_combinations gcc,
         gl_ledgers gl
  WHERE  gb.code_combination_id = gcc.code_combination_id
  AND    gb.ledger_id = gl.ledger_id
  AND    gb.ledger_id = :p_ledger_id
  AND    gb.period_name = :p_period_name
  AND    gb.actual_flag = 'A'
  AND    gcc.summary_flag = 'N'
  AND    gcc.enabled_flag = 'Y'

Entity 5: EBSInterface
Parent: RICEComponent (riceCategory: I)
Attributes:
- interfaceId: string (identity key)
- interfaceName: string
- interfaceDirection: INBOUND|OUTBOUND|BIDIRECTIONAL
- interfaceMethod: 
  FILE_BASED|API_BASED|SOA_COMPOSITE|
  DATABASE_LINK|MIDDLEWARE|OPEN_INTERFACE|
  BUSINESS_EVENT|REST_API|SOAP_WS
- dataFormat: CSV|XML|JSON|FIXED_WIDTH|EDI|IDOC|EXCEL
- frequency: REALTIME|SCHEDULED|ON_DEMAND|EVENT_DRIVEN
- sourceSystem: string
- targetSystem: string
- stagingTable: string
- openInterfaceTable: string
- validationProgram: string
- importProgram: string
- errorTable: string
- apiPackage: string
- apiProcedure: string
- samplePayload: string
- volumePerRun: string
- transformationRequired: boolean
- encryptionRequired: boolean
- acknowledgmentRequired: boolean
Sample Inbound Interface Instances:
AP Invoice Inbound Interface:
interfaceName: AP Invoice Open Interface
interfaceDirection: INBOUND
interfaceMethod: OPEN_INTERFACE
openInterfaceTable: ap_invoices_interface
stagingTable: xxcust_ap_inv_stg
importProgram: Payables Open Interface Import
apiPackage: AP_IMPORT_INVOICES_PKG
sampleSQL: |
  -- Stage inbound invoice data
  INSERT INTO ap_invoices_interface (
    invoice_id,
    invoice_num,
    invoice_type_lookup_code,
    invoice_date,
    vendor_id,
    vendor_site_id,
    invoice_amount,
    invoice_currency_code,
    description,
    org_id,
    source,
    group_id,
    status
  ) VALUES (
    ap_invoices_interface_s.NEXTVAL,
    :p_invoice_num,
    'STANDARD',
    :p_invoice_date,
    :p_vendor_id,
    :p_vendor_site_id,
    :p_amount,
    'USD',
    :p_description,
    :p_org_id,
    'XXCUST_ERP_INTERFACE',
    :p_group_id,
    'NEW'
  );
  -- Corresponding lines
  INSERT INTO ap_invoice_lines_interface (
    invoice_id,
    line_number,
    line_type_lookup_code,
    amount,
    accounting_date,
    dist_code_combination_id,
    org_id
  ) VALUES (
    :p_invoice_id,
    1,
    'ITEM',
    :p_amount,
    SYSDATE,
    :p_ccid,
    :p_org_id
  );
  -- Run import program
  -- Concurrent: Payables Open Interface Import
  -- Parameters: Source = XXCUST_ERP_INTERFACE
  --             Group = :p_group_id
  --             Org = :p_org_id

Employee Inbound Interface (HRMS):
interfaceName: HR Employee Open Interface
interfaceDirection: INBOUND
openInterfaceTable: per_all_people_f
apiPackage: HR_PERSON_API
apiProcedure: create_person
sampleSQL: |
    DECLARE
    l_person_id          NUMBER;
    l_assignment_id      NUMBER;
    l_object_version_number NUMBER;
    l_asg_ovn            NUMBER;
    l_per_effective_start_date DATE;
    l_per_effective_end_date   DATE;
    l_full_name          VARCHAR2(240);
    l_per_comment_id     NUMBER;
    l_assignment_sequence NUMBER;
    l_assignment_number  VARCHAR2(30);
    l_name_combination_warning BOOLEAN;
    l_assign_payroll_warning   BOOLEAN;
    l_orig_hire_warning        BOOLEAN;
  BEGIN
    hr_person_api.create_person(
      p_effective_date              => :p_hire_date,
      p_business_group_id           => :p_bg_id,
      p_last_name                   => :p_last_name,
      p_first_name                  => :p_first_name,
      p_middle_names                => :p_middle_name,
      p_date_of_birth               => :p_dob,
      p_national_identifier         => :p_ssn,
      p_sex                         => :p_gender,
      p_email_address               => :p_email,
      p_employee_number             => :p_emp_number,
      p_person_id                   => l_person_id,
      p_object_version_number       => l_object_version_number,
      p_effective_start_date        => l_per_effective_start_date,
      p_effective_end_date          => l_per_effective_end_date,
      p_full_name                   => l_full_name,
      p_comment_id                  => l_per_comment_id,
      p_name_combination_warning    => l_name_combination_warning,
      p_assign_payroll_warning      => l_assign_payroll_warning,
      p_orig_hire_warning           => l_orig_hire_warning
    );
    COMMIT;
    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('Person created: ' || l_person_id);
  EXCEPTION
    WHEN OTHERS THEN
      ROLLBACK;
      DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('Error: ' || SQLERRM);
  END;

SECTION D — CONVERSION ENTITIES

Entity 6: EBSConversion
Parent: RICEComponent (riceCategory: C)
Attributes:
- conversionId: string (identity key)
- conversionName: string
- conversionType: 
  INITIAL_LOAD|DELTA_LOAD|HISTORICAL|
  CUTOVER|OPEN_BALANCES|MASTER_DATA
- dataCategory:
  MASTER_DATA|TRANSACTIONAL|OPEN_ITEMS|
  HISTORICAL_BALANCES|CONFIGURATION
- module: string
- sourceSystem: string
- stagingTable: string
- targetTable: string
- targetAPI: string
- validationQuery: string
- reconciliationQuery: string
- cleansingRules: list of string
- transformationLogic: string
- volumeEstimate: string
- cutoverSequence: number
- dependsOn: list of conversionId
- rollbackStrategy: string
Sample Conversion Instances:
Supplier Master Conversion:
conversionName: Supplier Master Data Conversion
conversionType: INITIAL_LOAD
dataCategory: MASTER_DATA
module: AP
stagingTable: xxcust_suppliers_stg
targetTable: po_vendors
targetAPI: AP_VENDOR_PUB.CREATE_VENDOR
cutoverSequence: 1
validationQuery: |
  -- Validate staged suppliers before conversion
  SELECT COUNT(*) total_records,
         SUM(CASE WHEN vendor_name IS NULL 
                  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) missing_name,
         SUM(CASE WHEN vendor_type_lookup_code 
                  NOT IN ('VENDOR','EMPLOYEE','TEMPORARY')
                  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) invalid_type,
         SUM(CASE WHEN tax_registration_num IS NULL
                  AND vendor_type_lookup_code = 'VENDOR'
                  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) missing_tax_reg,
         SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'ERROR' 
                  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) error_records
  FROM   xxcust_suppliers_stg
  WHERE  batch_id = :p_batch_id
conversionSQL: |
  DECLARE
    l_vendor_id        NUMBER;
    l_vendor_rec       ap_vendor_pub_pkg.r_vendor_rec_type;
    l_return_status    VARCHAR2(10);
    l_msg_count        NUMBER;
    l_msg_data         VARCHAR2(2000);
  BEGIN
    FOR rec IN (
      SELECT * FROM xxcust_suppliers_stg
      WHERE  batch_id = :p_batch_id
      AND    status = 'VALIDATED'
    ) LOOP
      l_vendor_rec.vendor_name           := rec.vendor_name;
      l_vendor_rec.vendor_type_lookup_code := rec.vendor_type;
      l_vendor_rec.tax_registration_num  := rec.tax_reg_num;
      l_vendor_rec.payment_currency_code := rec.currency_code;
      l_vendor_rec.pay_group_lookup_code := rec.pay_group;
      l_vendor_rec.payment_priority      := 99;
      l_vendor_rec.terms_id              := rec.terms_id;
      ap_vendor_pub_pkg.create_vendor(
        p_api_version   => 1.0,
        p_vendor_rec    => l_vendor_rec,
        x_return_status => l_return_status,
        x_msg_count     => l_msg_count,
        x_msg_data      => l_msg_data,
        x_vendor_id     => l_vendor_id
      );
      IF l_return_status = 'S' THEN
        UPDATE xxcust_suppliers_stg
        SET    status = 'PROCESSED',
               target_vendor_id = l_vendor_id,
               processed_date = SYSDATE
        WHERE  CURRENT OF cur;
      ELSE
        UPDATE xxcust_suppliers_stg
        SET    status = 'ERROR',
               error_message = l_msg_data
        WHERE  CURRENT OF cur;
      END IF;
    END LOOP;
    COMMIT;
  END;
reconciliationQuery: |
  SELECT s.total_staged,
         p.total_converted,
         (s.total_staged - p.total_converted) AS variance
  FROM   (SELECT COUNT(*) total_staged 
          FROM   xxcust_suppliers_stg 
          WHERE  batch_id = :p_batch_id) s,
         (SELECT COUNT(*) total_converted 
          FROM   xxcust_suppliers_stg 
          WHERE  batch_id = :p_batch_id
          AND    status = 'PROCESSED') p
GL Opening Balances Conversion:
conversionName: GL Opening Balances Conversion
conversionType: OPEN_BALANCES
dataCategory: HISTORICAL_BALANCES
module: GL
stagingTable: xxcust_gl_balances_stg
targetTable: gl_interface
targetAPI: Journal Import
cutoverSequence: 5
conversionSQL: |
  -- Load opening balances via GL Interface
  INSERT INTO gl_interface (
    status,
    set_of_books_id,
    accounting_date,
    currency_code,
    date_created,
    created_by,
    actual_flag,
        user_je_category_name,
    user_je_source_name,
    segment1,
    segment2,
    segment3,
    segment4,
    segment5,
    entered_dr,
    entered_cr,
    accounted_dr,
    accounted_cr,
    reference1,
    reference4,
    group_id
  )
  SELECT 'NEW',
         :p_sob_id,
         :p_cutover_date,
         stg.currency_code,
         SYSDATE,
         fnd_global.user_id,
         'A',
         'Opening Balance',
         'XXCUST Conversion',
         stg.segment1,
         stg.segment2,
         stg.segment3,
         stg.segment4,
         stg.segment5,
         CASE WHEN stg.balance_amount > 0 
              THEN stg.balance_amount ELSE 0 END,
         CASE WHEN stg.balance_amount < 0 
              THEN ABS(stg.balance_amount) ELSE 0 END,
         CASE WHEN stg.balance_amount > 0 
              THEN stg.balance_amount ELSE 0 END,
         CASE WHEN stg.balance_amount < 0 
              THEN ABS(stg.balance_amount) ELSE 0 END,
         'OPENING BALANCE CONVERSION',
         stg.account_description,
         :p_group_id
  FROM   xxcust_gl_balances_stg stg
  WHERE  stg.batch_id = :p_batch_id
  AND    stg.status = 'VALIDATED';
  -- Validate debit = credit before Journal Import
  SELECT SUM(entered_dr) total_dr,
         SUM(entered_cr) total_cr,
         SUM(entered_dr) - SUM(entered_cr) AS variance
  FROM   gl_interface
  WHERE  group_id = :p_group_id
  AND    status = 'NEW';
  -- Run Journal Import concurrent program
  -- Parameters:
  --   Source: XXCUST Conversion
  --   Group ID: :p_group_id
  --   Set of Books: :p_sob_id
  --   Post Errors to Suspense: No
AP Open Items Conversion:
conversionName: AP Open Invoices Conversion
conversionType: OPEN_ITEMS
dataCategory: TRANSACTIONAL
module: AP
stagingTable: xxcust_ap_open_inv_stg
targetTable: ap_invoices_interface
cutoverSequence: 6
dependsOn: [Supplier Master Conversion, 
            Supplier Sites Conversion]
conversionSQL: |
  -- Step 1: Load to AP Interface tables
  INSERT INTO ap_invoices_interface (
    invoice_id,
    invoice_num,
    invoice_type_lookup_code,
    invoice_date,
    vendor_id,
    vendor_site_id,
    invoice_amount,
    invoice_currency_code,
    exchange_rate_type,
    exchange_date,
    description,
    gl_date,
    terms_id,
    pay_group_lookup_code,
    org_id,
    source,
    group_id,
    status
  )
  SELECT ap_invoices_interface_s.NEXTVAL,
         stg.invoice_number,
         DECODE(stg.invoice_type,
                'CREDIT','CREDIT',
                'DEBIT','DEBIT',
                'STANDARD'),
         stg.invoice_date,
         pv.vendor_id,
         pvs.vendor_site_id,
         stg.invoice_amount,
         stg.currency_code,
         'Corporate',
         stg.invoice_date,
         'CONVERSION: ' || stg.invoice_number,
         :p_cutover_date,
         apt.term_id,
         stg.pay_group,
         stg.org_id,
         'XXCUST_CONVERSION',
         :p_group_id,
         'NEW'
  FROM   xxcust_ap_open_inv_stg stg,
         po_vendors pv,
         po_vendor_sites_all pvs,
         ap_terms apt
  WHERE  stg.vendor_num = pv.segment1
  AND    stg.vendor_site_code = pvs.vendor_site_code
  AND    pvs.vendor_id = pv.vendor_id
  AND    pvs.org_id = stg.org_id
  AND    stg.payment_terms = apt.name
  AND    stg.batch_id = :p_batch_id
  AND    stg.status = 'VALIDATED';

Entity 7: EBSExtension
Parent: RICEComponent (riceCategory: E)
Attributes:
- extensionId: string (identity key)

  DATABASE_TRIGGER|CUSTOM_PACKAGE|

  ADF_EXTENSION|REDWOOD_EXTENSION|
  WORKFLOW_CUSTOMIZATION|AME_RULE|
  CUSTOM_CONCURRENT|REPORT_CUSTOMIZATION|
  CUSTOM_API|BUSINESS_EVENT
- module: string
- baseObject: string
- extensionObject: string
- triggerPoint:
  BEFORE_INSERT|AFTER_INSERT|
  BEFORE_UPDATE|AFTER_UPDATE|
  BEFORE_DELETE|AFTER_DELETE|
  ON_SUBMIT|ON_APPROVE|ON_VALIDATE
- purposeDescription: string
- technicalApproach: string
- sampleCode: string
- testingApproach: string
- upgradeConsiderations: string
Sample Extension Instances:
Database Trigger Extension:
extensionName: AP Invoice Validation Trigger
extensionType: DATABASE_TRIGGER
module: AP
baseObject: AP_INVOICES_ALL
triggerPoint: BEFORE_INSERT
sampleCode: |
  CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER xxcust_ap_inv_biu_trg
  BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON ap_invoices_all
  FOR EACH ROW
  DECLARE
    l_vendor_hold    VARCHAR2(1);
    l_inv_count      NUMBER;
    l_max_amount     NUMBER := 100000;
  BEGIN
    -- Check vendor hold status
    BEGIN
      SELECT 'Y'
      INTO   l_vendor_hold
      FROM   po_vendors
      WHERE  vendor_id = :NEW.vendor_id
      AND    hold_flag = 'Y';
      RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
        -20001,
        'Vendor is on payment hold. Invoice cannot be created.'
      );
    EXCEPTION
      WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
        l_vendor_hold := 'N';
    END;
    -- Check duplicate invoice
    BEGIN
      SELECT COUNT(*)
      INTO   l_inv_count
      FROM   ap_invoices_all
      WHERE  vendor_id = :NEW.vendor_id
      AND    invoice_num = :NEW.invoice_num
      AND    org_id = :NEW.org_id
      AND    invoice_id != NVL(:NEW.invoice_id, -1);
      IF l_inv_count > 0 THEN
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
          -20002,
          'Duplicate invoice number for this vendor.'
        );
      END IF;
    END;
    -- Enforce amount limit
    IF :NEW.invoice_amount > l_max_amount THEN
      -- Log for approval escalation
      INSERT INTO xxcust_high_value_inv_log (
              invoice_id,
        vendor_id,
        invoice_num,
        invoice_amount,
        created_by,
        creation_date,
        status
      ) VALUES (
        :NEW.invoice_id,
        :NEW.vendor_id,
        :NEW.invoice_num,
        :NEW.invoice_amount,
        :NEW.created_by,
        SYSDATE,
        'PENDING_ESCALATION'
      );
    END IF;
    -- Auto-populate custom attribute
    :NEW.attribute1 := 'XXCUST_' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'YYYYMMDD');
    :NEW.attribute2 := SYS_CONTEXT('USERENV','SESSION_USER');
  EXCEPTION
    WHEN OTHERS THEN
      RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
        -20099,
        'XXCUST Invoice Trigger Error: ' || SQLERRM
      );
  END xxcust_ap_inv_biu_trg;
  /
Custom Package Extension:
extensionName: XXCUST_AP_UTILS_PKG
extensionType: CUSTOM_PACKAGE
module: AP
sampleCode: |
  CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE xxcust_ap_utils_pkg AS
  /*
  || Package  : XXCUST_AP_UTILS_PKG
  || Purpose  : Custom AP Utility Functions
  || Author   : IBM Consulting
  || Created  : 2025
  */
    -- Get vendor details
    FUNCTION get_vendor_name(
      p_vendor_id IN NUMBER
    ) RETURN VARCHAR2;
    -- Get payment terms name
    FUNCTION get_terms_name(
      p_terms_id IN NUMBER
    ) RETURN VARCHAR2;
    -- Validate invoice for payment
    FUNCTION validate_invoice_for_payment(
      p_invoice_id IN NUMBER,
      p_org_id     IN NUMBER,
      p_error_msg  OUT VARCHAR2
    ) RETURN BOOLEAN;
    -- Get invoice outstanding amount
    FUNCTION get_outstanding_amount(
      p_invoice_id IN NUMBER
    ) RETURN NUMBER;
    -- Generate payment reference
    FUNCTION generate_payment_ref(
      p_vendor_id  IN NUMBER,
      p_org_id     IN NUMBER
    ) RETURN VARCHAR2;
    -- Log custom audit trail
    PROCEDURE log_audit_trail(
      p_table_name   IN VARCHAR2,
      p_record_id    IN NUMBER,
      p_action       IN VARCHAR2,
      p_old_value    IN VARCHAR2,
      p_new_value    IN VARCHAR2,
      p_changed_by   IN NUMBER
    );
  END xxcust_ap_utils_pkg;
  /
  CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY xxcust_ap_utils_pkg AS
    FUNCTION get_vendor_name(
      p_vendor_id IN NUMBER
    ) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
      l_vendor_name VARCHAR2(240);
    BEGIN
      SELECT vendor_name
      INTO   l_vendor_name
      FROM   po_vendors
      WHERE  vendor_id = p_vendor_id;
      RETURN l_vendor_name;
    EXCEPTION
      WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
        RETURN 'UNKNOWN VENDOR';
      WHEN OTHERS THEN
        RETURN 'ERROR: ' || SQLERRM;
    END get_vendor_name;
    FUNCTION validate_invoice_for_payment(
      p_invoice_id IN NUMBER,
      p_org_id     IN NUMBER,
      p_error_msg  OUT VARCHAR2
    ) RETURN BOOLEAN IS
      l_hold_count    NUMBER;
      l_inv_status    VARCHAR2(25);
      l_amount_paid   NUMBER;
      l_inv_amount    NUMBER;
    BEGIN
      -- Check holds
      SELECT COUNT(*)
      INTO   l_hold_count
      FROM   ap_holds_all
      WHERE  invoice_id = p_invoice_id
      AND    release_lookup_code IS NULL;
      IF l_hold_count > 0 THEN
        p_error_msg := 'Invoice has ' || l_hold_count 
                       || ' unreleased holds';
        RETURN FALSE;
      END IF;
      -- Check approval status
      SELECT approval_status,
             invoice_amount,
             amount_paid
      INTO   l_inv_status,
             l_inv_amount,
             l_amount_paid
      FROM   ap_invoices_all
      WHERE  invoice_id = p_invoice_id
      AND    org_id = p_org_id;
      IF l_inv_status NOT IN ('APPROVED','NEVER APPROVED') THEN
        p_error_msg := 'Invoice approval status: ' || l_inv_status;
        RETURN FALSE;
      END IF;
      -- Check outstanding amount
      IF (l_inv_amount - NVL(l_amount_paid,0)) <= 0 THEN
        p_error_msg := 'Invoice fully paid';
        RETURN FALSE;
      END IF;
      p_error_msg := NULL;
      RETURN TRUE;
    EXCEPTION
      WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
        p_error_msg := 'Invoice not found';
        RETURN FALSE;
      WHEN OTHERS THEN
        p_error_msg := 'Validation error: ' || SQLERRM;
        RETURN FALSE;
    END validate_invoice_for_payment;
    PROCEDURE log_audit_trail(
      p_table_name   IN VARCHAR2,
      p_record_id    IN NUMBER,
      p_action       IN VARCHAR2,
      p_old_value    IN VARCHAR2,
      p_new_value    IN VARCHAR2,
      p_changed_by   IN NUMBER
    ) IS
      PRAGMA AUTONOMOUS_TRANSACTION;
    BEGIN
      INSERT INTO xxcust_audit_trail (
        audit_id,
        table_name,
        record_id,
        action_type,
        old_value,
        new_value,
        changed_by,
        changed_date
      ) VALUES (
        xxcust_audit_trail_s.NEXTVAL,
        p_table_name,
        p_record_id,
        p_action,
        p_old_value,
        p_new_value,
        p_changed_by,
        SYSDATE
      );
      COMMIT;
    EXCEPTION
      WHEN OTHERS THEN
        ROLLBACK;
    END log_audit_trail;
  END xxcust_ap_utils_pkg;
  /

AP Invoice AME Configuration:
ameName: AP Invoice Approval
transactionType: APINVOICE
module: AP
approvalType: SUPERVISORY
businessPurpose: |
  Controls approval routing for AP invoices
  based on invoice amount, vendor type,
  and cost center ownership
AME Conditions for AP Invoice:
Condition 1:
  conditionName: Invoice Amount GT 10000
  attributeName: INVOICE_AMOUNT
  operator: GREATER_THAN
  conditionValue: 10000
Condition 2:
  conditionName: Invoice Amount GT 50000
  attributeName: INVOICE_AMOUNT
  operator: GREATER_THAN
  conditionValue: 50000
Condition 3:
  conditionName: Invoice Amount GT 100000
  attributeName: INVOICE_AMOUNT
  operator: GREATER_THAN
  conditionValue: 100000
Condition 4:
  conditionName: Vendor Type is Employee
  attributeName: VENDOR_TYPE
  operator: EQUALS
  conditionValue: EMPLOYEE
Condition 5:
  conditionName: Invoice Type is Credit Memo
  attributeName: INVOICE_TYPE
  operator: EQUALS
  conditionValue: CREDIT
AME Rules for AP Invoice:
Rule 1:
  ruleName: Standard Invoice Approval
  ruleType: AUTHORIZATION
  priority: 50
  conditions: [Invoice Amount GT 10000]
  actionType: REQUIRE_APPROVAL
  approverType: SUPERVISOR
  approverValue: DIRECT_SUPERVISOR
  description: |
    Invoices over $10,000 require
    direct supervisor approval
Rule 2:
  ruleName: Senior Manager Approval
  ruleType: AUTHORIZATION
  priority: 40
  conditions: [Invoice Amount GT 50000]
  actionType: REQUIRE_APPROVAL
  approverType: JOB_LEVEL
  approverValue: SENIOR_MANAGER
  description: |
    Invoices over $50,000 require
    senior manager approval
Rule 3:
  ruleName: Executive Approval Required
  ruleType: AUTHORIZATION
  priority: 30
  conditions: [Invoice Amount GT 100000]
  actionType: REQUIRE_APPROVAL
  approverType: POSITION
  approverValue: CFO_POSITION
  description: |
    Invoices over $100,000 require
    CFO or delegate approval
Rule 4:
  ruleName: Employee Expense Exception
  ruleType: EXCEPTION
  priority: 10
  conditions: [Vendor Type is Employee]
  actionType: SKIP_APPROVER
  approverType: SUPERVISOR
  approverValue: SKIP_TO_HR_MANAGER
  description: |
    Employee expense invoices route
    to HR Manager instead of supervisor

Sample Workflow Instances:
AP Invoice Approval Workflow:
workflowName: AP Invoice Approval Workflow
itemType: APINVOICE
processName: AP_INVOICE_APPROVAL
module: AP
businessPurpose: |
  Routes AP invoices through approval chain
  based on AME rules. Handles notifications,
  reminders, escalations, and rejections.
triggerEvent: AP Invoice Validation
diagnosticQueries: |
  -- Check workflow status for invoice
  SELECT wf.item_key,
         wf.item_type,
         wf.begin_date,
         wf.end_date,
         wf.activity_status,
         wf.activity_result,
         wa.display_name AS activity_name,
         wf.assigned_user
  FROM   wf_item_activity_statuses_v wf,
         wf_activities_vl wa
  WHERE  wf.item_type = 'APINVOICE'
  AND    wf.activity_status IN ('NOTIFIED','ERROR','ACTIVE')
  AND    wf.process_activity = wa.instance_id
  ORDER BY wf.begin_date DESC;
   -- Find stuck workflow notifications
  SELECT wn.notification_id,
         wn.status,
         wn.sent_date,
         wn.due_date,
         wn.recipient_role,
         wn.subject,
         wn.mail_status,
         wr.display_name AS recipient_name,
         wr.email_address
  FROM   wf_notifications wn,
         wf_roles wr
  WHERE  wn.recipient_role = wr.name
  AND    wn.status = 'OPEN'
  AND    wn.item_type = 'APINVOICE'
  AND    wn.sent_date < SYSDATE - 7
  ORDER BY wn.sent_date;
  -- Workflow error diagnostics
  SELECT we.item_type,
         we.item_key,
         we.activity_label,
         we.error_name,
         we.error_message,
         we.error_stack,
         we.error_date
  FROM   wf_item_activity_statuses wias,
         wf_errors we
  WHERE  wias.item_type = we.item_type
  AND    wias.item_key = we.item_key
  AND    wias.activity_status = 'ERROR'
  AND    wias.item_type = 'APINVOICE'
  ORDER BY we.error_date DESC;
  -- Reassign stuck notification
  BEGIN
    wf_notification.transfer(
      nid   => :p_notification_id,
      new_role => :p_new_approver_role
    );
    COMMIT;
  END;
  -- Cancel and restart workflow
  BEGIN
    wf_engine.abortprocess(
      itemtype => 'APINVOICE',
      itemkey  => :p_item_key,
      process  => 'AP_INVOICE_APPROVAL'
    );
    -- Restart
    ap_workflow_pkg.start_workflow(
      p_invoice_id => :p_invoice_id
    );
    COMMIT;
  END;
  -- Check workflow item attributes
  SELECT wia.name,
         wia.text_value,
         wia.number_value,
         wia.date_value
  FROM   wf_item_attribute_values wia
  WHERE  wia.item_type = 'APINVOICE'
  AND    wia.item_key = :p_item_key;
PO Approval Workflow:
workflowName: PO Approval Workflow
itemType: POAPPRV
processName: PO_APPROVAL
module: PO
diagnosticQueries: |
  -- Check PO workflow status
  SELECT pha.segment1 AS po_number,
         pha.authorization_status,
         pha.approved_flag,
         wias.activity_status,
         wias.activity_result,
         wa.display_name AS current_activity,
         wias.assigned_user AS current_approver,
         wias.begin_date
  FROM   po_headers_all pha,
         wf_item_activity_statuses wias,
         wf_activities_vl wa
  WHERE  wias.item_type = 'POAPPRV'
  AND    wias.item_key = TO_CHAR(pha.po_header_id)
  AND    wias.process_activity = wa.instance_id
  AND    wias.activity_status IN ('NOTIFIED','ACTIVE','ERROR')
  AND    pha.segment1 = :p_po_number;
  -- PO approval history
  SELECT paa.action_code,
         paa.action_date,
         paa.employee_id,
         ppf.full_name AS approver_name,
         paa.note,
         paa.object_type_code
  FROM   po_action_history paa,
         per_all_people_f ppf
  WHERE  paa.object_id = :p_po_header_id
  AND    paa.object_type_code = 'PO'
  AND    paa.employee_id = ppf.person_id
  AND    SYSDATE BETWEEN ppf.effective_start_date
         AND ppf.effective_end_date
  ORDER BY paa.sequence_num;
HR New Hire Workflow:
workflowName: HR New Hire Notification
itemType: HRSSA
processName: HR_HIRE_PROCESS
module: HRMS
diagnosticQueries: |
  -- Check HR workflow status
  SELECT wias.item_key,
         wias.activity_status,
         wa.display_name AS activity_name,
         wias.assigned_user,
         wias.begin_date,
         wias.end_date
  FROM   wf_item_activity_statuses wias,
         wf_activities_vl wa
  WHERE  wias.item_type = 'HRSSA'
  AND    wias.process_activity = wa.instance_id
  AND    wias.activity_status NOT IN ('COMPLETE')
  ORDER BY wias.begin_date DESC;

  XXCUSTInvoiceEntryPageCO
basePagePath: |

sampleCode: |
  package xxcust.oracle.apps.ap.invoice.webui;
  import oracle.apps.fnd.common.VersionInfo;
  import oracle.apps.fnd.framework.OAException;
  import oracle.apps.fnd.framework.webui.OAControllerImpl;
  import oracle.apps.fnd.framework.webui.OAPageContext;
  import oracle.apps.fnd.framework.webui.beans.OAWebBean;
  import oracle.apps.fnd.framework.webui.beans.message
         .OAMessageTextInputBean;
  import oracle.apps.fnd.framework.webui.beans.nav
         .OASubmitButtonBean;
  public class XXCUSTInvoiceEntryPageCO
    extends OAControllerImpl {
    public static final String RCS_ID =
      "$Header: XXCUSTInvoiceEntryPageCO.java 1.0$";
    public void processRequest(
      OAPageContext pageContext,
      OAWebBean webBean
    ) {
      super.processRequest(pageContext, webBean);
      // Custom initialization logic
      String userId = pageContext.getUserId();
      String orgId  = pageContext.getOrgId();
      // Hide/show fields based on responsibility
      OAMessageTextInputBean customField =
        (OAMessageTextInputBean)webBean.findChildRecursive(
          "CustomRefNum"
        );
           if (customField != null) {
        // Make field mandatory for specific org
        if ("101".equals(orgId)) {
          customField.setRequired(true);
          customField.setRendered(true);
        } else {
          customField.setRequired(false);
          customField.setRendered(false);
        }
      }
    }
    public void processFormRequest(
      OAPageContext pageContext,
      OAWebBean webBean
    ) {
      super.processFormRequest(pageContext, webBean);
      // Handle custom button click
      if (pageContext.getParameter("CustomValidate") != null) {
        // Get invoice amount
        OAMessageTextInputBean amountBean =
          (OAMessageTextInputBean)webBean.findChildRecursive(
            "InvoiceAmount"
          );
        String amountStr = pageContext.getParameter(
          "InvoiceAmount"
        );
        if (amountStr != null && !amountStr.isEmpty()) {
          double amount = Double.parseDouble(amountStr);
          // Custom validation
          if (amount > 1000000) {
            throw new OAException(
              "XXCUST",
              "XXCUST_INV_AMOUNT_EXCEEDS_LIMIT",
              OAException.ERROR
            );
          }
        }
        // Invoke AM method
        pageContext.getApplicationModule(webBean)
          .invokeMethod("validateCustomRules");
      }
    }
  }
OAF VO Extension Example:

  -- Extend InvoiceVO to add custom attributes
  -- File: XXCUSTInvoiceVORowImpl.java
  package xxcust.oracle.apps.ap.invoice.server;
  import oracle.apps.ap.invoice.server.InvoiceVORowImpl;
  import oracle.jbo.domain.Number;
  public class XXCUSTInvoiceVORowImpl
    extends InvoiceVORowImpl {
    // Custom attribute: Custom Reference Number
    public String getCustomRefNum() {
      return (String)getAttributeInternal(
        CUSTOMREFNUM
      );
    }
    public void setCustomRefNum(String value) {
      setAttributeInternal(CUSTOMREFNUM, value);
    }
    // Custom attribute: Risk Score
    public Number getRiskScore() {
      return (Number)getAttributeInternal(RISKSCORE);
    }
    // Override getter to add custom logic
    public String getVendorName() {
      String vendorName = super.getVendorName();
      // Append custom indicator if vendor on watch list
      if (isVendorOnWatchList(getVendorId())) {
        return vendorName + " [WATCH LIST]";
      }
      return vendorName;
    }
    private boolean isVendorOnWatchList(
      Number vendorId
    ) {
      // Custom logic to check watch list
      return false;
    }
  }

              2025-01-15
            </xxc:accountingDate>
            <xxc:segment1>01</xxc:segment1>
            <xxc:segment2>100</xxc:segment2>
            <xxc:segment3>7500</xxc:segment3>
          </xxc:line>
        </xxc:invoiceLines>
      </xxc:createInvoiceRequest>
    </soapenv:Body>
  </soapenv:Envelope>
BPEL Process Code:
sampleCode: |
  <!-- AP Invoice Creation BPEL Process -->
  <process name="APInvoiceComposite"
    targetNamespace=
      "http://xmlns.oracle.com/xxcust/APInvoice"
    xmlns="http://schemas.xmlsoap.org/ws/2003/03/business-process/"
    xmlns:bpelx=
      "http://schemas.oracle.com/bpel/extension"
    xmlns:ora=
      "http://schemas.oracle.com/xpath/extension">
    <!-- Variables -->
    <variables>
      <variable name="inputVariable"
        messageType="tns:requestMessage"/>
      <variable name="outputVariable"
        messageType="tns:responseMessage"/>
      <variable name="invoiceId"
        type="xsd:string"/>
    </variables>
    <!-- Main Sequence -->
    <sequence name="main">
      <!-- Receive input -->
      <receive name="receiveInput"
        partnerLink="client"
        portType="tns:APInvoiceComposite"
        operation="createInvoice"
        variable="inputVariable"
        createInstance="yes"/>
      <!-- Validate invoice -->
      <invoke name="validateInvoice"
        partnerLink="APValidationService"
        portType="val:APValidation"
        operation="validate"
        inputVariable="inputVariable"
        outputVariable="validationResult"/>
            <!-- Check validation result -->
      <switch name="checkValidation">
        <case condition="bpws:getVariableData(
          'validationResult','payload',
          '/ns:validateResponse/ns:status') = 'VALID'">
          <!-- Create invoice via API -->
          <invoke name="createAPInvoice"
            partnerLink="APInvoiceAPIService"
            portType="api:APInvoiceAPI"
            operation="createInvoice"
            inputVariable="inputVariable"
            outputVariable="createResult"/>
          <!-- Extract invoice ID -->
          <assign name="extractInvoiceId">
            <copy>
              <from variable="createResult"
                part="payload"
                query="/ns:createResponse/ns:invoiceId"/>
              <to variable="invoiceId"/>
            </copy>
          </assign>
          <!-- Send success notification -->
          <invoke name="sendSuccessNotification"
            partnerLink="NotificationService"
            portType="notif:Notification"
            operation="sendEmail"
            inputVariable="successNotification"/>
          <!-- Set success response -->
          <assign name="setSuccessResponse">
            <copy>
              <from expression="'SUCCESS'"/>
              <to variable="outputVariable"
                part="payload"
                query="/ns:response/ns:status"/>
            </copy>
            <copy>
              <from variable="invoiceId"/>
              <to variable="outputVariable"
                part="payload"
                query="/ns:response/ns:invoiceId"/>
            </copy>
          </assign>
        </case>
        <otherwise>
          <!-- Handle validation failure -->
          <assign name="setErrorResponse">
            <copy>
              <from expression="'VALIDATION_FAILED'"/>
              <to variable="outputVariable"
                part="payload"
                query="/ns:response/ns:status"/>
            </copy>
          </assign>
          <!-- Log error -->
          <invoke name="logValidationError"
            partnerLink="AuditService"
            portType="audit:AuditService"
            operation="logError"
            inputVariable="errorDetails"/>
        </otherwise>
      </switch>
      <!-- Reply to caller -->
      <reply name="replyOutput"
        partnerLink="client"
        portType="tns:APInvoiceComposite"
        operation="createInvoice"
        variable="outputVariable"/>
    </sequence>
  </process>
REST Service Extension:
serviceName: XXCUSTInvoiceRestService
serviceType: REST_SERVICE
module: AP
endpointURL: |
  https://ebs-server:8080/xxcust/
  rest/v1/invoices
sampleCode: |
  -- REST Service using Oracle REST Data Services (ORDS)
  -- or custom Java REST endpoint
  -- Option 1: PL/SQL REST Handler
  CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE xxcust_invoice_rest_handler(
    p_request_body  IN  CLOB,
    p_response_body OUT CLOB,
    p_status_code   OUT NUMBER
  ) AS
    l_invoice_json   JSON_OBJECT_T;
    l_invoice_id     NUMBER;
    l_vendor_id      NUMBER;
    l_vendor_site_id NUMBER;
    l_return_status  VARCHAR2(10);
    l_msg_data       VARCHAR2(2000);
  BEGIN
    -- Parse JSON request
    l_invoice_json := JSON_OBJECT_T.parse(p_request_body);
    -- Extract fields
    l_vendor_id := xxcust_ap_utils_pkg.get_vendor_id(
      l_invoice_json.get_string('vendorNum')
    );
    l_vendor_site_id := xxcust_ap_utils_pkg.get_vendor_site_id(
      l_vendor_id,
      l_invoice_json.get_string('vendorSiteCode'),
      l_invoice_json.get_number('orgId')
    );
    -- Create invoice
    INSERT INTO ap_invoices_interface (
      invoice_id,
      invoice_num,
      invoice_type_lookup_code,
      invoice_date,
      vendor_id,
      vendor_site_id,
      invoice_amount,
      invoice_currency_code,
      org_id,
      source,
      group_id,
      status
    ) VALUES (
      ap_invoices_interface_s.NEXTVAL,
      l_invoice_json.get_string('invoiceNum'),
      'STANDARD',
      TO_DATE(
        l_invoice_json.get_string('invoiceDate'),
        'YYYY-MM-DD'
      ),
      l_vendor_id,
      l_vendor_site_id,
      l_invoice_json.get_number('invoiceAmount'),
      l_invoice_json.get_string('currencyCode'),
      l_invoice_json.get_number('orgId'),
      'REST_API',
      SYS_GUID(),
      'NEW'
    )
    RETURNING invoice_id INTO l_invoice_id;
    COMMIT;
    -- Build JSON response
    p_response_body := JSON_OBJECT(
      'status'     VALUE 'SUCCESS',
      'invoiceId'  VALUE l_invoice_id,
      'message'    VALUE 'Invoice created successfully'
    );
    p_status_code := 200;
  EXCEPTION
    WHEN OTHERS THEN
      ROLLBACK;
      p_response_body := JSON_OBJECT(
        'status'  VALUE 'ERROR',
        'message' VALUE SQLERRM
      );
      p_status_code := 500;
  END xxcust_invoice_rest_handler;
  /

AP Invoice Approval Dashboard:
appName: AP Invoice Management
appAlias: AP_INV_MGMT
module: AP
authenticationScheme: EBS_SSO
businessPurpose: |
  APEX application for AP Invoice
  approval and monitoring dashboard
  integrated with Oracle EBS R12
sampleCode: |
  -- APEX Interactive Report SQL
  -- Page: Invoice Approval Dashboard
  SELECT
    aia.invoice_id,
    aia.invoice_num,
    aia.invoice_date,
    aia.invoice_amount,
    aia.invoice_currency_code,
    aia.approval_status,
    aia.payment_status_flag,
    pv.vendor_name,
    pvs.vendor_site_code,
    aia.description,
    aia.gl_date,
    apt.name AS payment_terms,
    -- Custom approval indicator
        CASE aia.approval_status
      WHEN 'APPROVED' THEN
        '<span style="color:green">
         ✓ Approved</span>'
      WHEN 'NEEDS REAPPROVAL' THEN
        '<span style="color:orange">
         ⚠ Needs Reapproval</span>'
      WHEN 'REJECTED' THEN
        '<span style="color:red">
         ✗ Rejected</span>'
      ELSE
        '<span style="color:blue">
         ⏳ Pending</span>'
    END AS approval_status_html,
    -- Days outstanding
    TRUNC(SYSDATE - aia.invoice_date) AS days_outstanding,
    -- Amount due
    (aia.invoice_amount - NVL(aia.amount_paid,0))
      AS amount_due,
    -- Overdue indicator
    CASE
      WHEN (aia.invoice_date + apt.terms_days) < SYSDATE
           AND aia.payment_status_flag != 'Y'
      THEN '<span style="color:red">OVERDUE</span>'
      ELSE '<span style="color:green">CURRENT</span>'
    END AS payment_status_html
  FROM
    ap_invoices_all       aia,
    po_vendors            pv,
    po_vendor_sites_all   pvs,
    ap_terms              apt
  WHERE
    aia.vendor_id         = pv.vendor_id
    AND aia.vendor_site_id    = pvs.vendor_site_id
    AND aia.terms_id          = apt.term_id
    AND aia.org_id            = :P0_ORG_ID
    AND aia.invoice_date BETWEEN
          TO_DATE(:P1_START_DATE,'DD-MON-YYYY')
          AND TO_DATE(:P1_END_DATE,'DD-MON-YYYY')
    AND (
          :P1_APPROVAL_STATUS IS NULL
          OR aia.approval_status = :P1_APPROVAL_STATUS
        )
  ORDER BY
    aia.invoice_date DESC
  -- APEX Process: Approve Invoice
  -- Page Process Type: PL/SQL
  BEGIN
    FOR rec IN (
      SELECT APEX_APPLICATION.G_F01(i) AS invoice_id
      FROM   DUAL
      CONNECT BY LEVEL <=
        APEX_APPLICATION.G_F01.COUNT
    ) LOOP
      UPDATE ap_invoices_all
      SET    approval_status     = 'APPROVED',
             approved_by         = :APP_USER,
             approved_amount     = invoice_amount,
             last_update_date    = SYSDATE,
             last_updated_by     = FND_GLOBAL.USER_ID
      WHERE  invoice_id = TO_NUMBER(rec.invoice_id)
      AND    org_id      = :P0_ORG_ID;
    END LOOP;
    COMMIT;
    APEX_APPLICATION.G_PRINT_SUCCESS_MESSAGE :=
      'Invoices approved successfully.';
  EXCEPTION
    WHEN OTHERS THEN
      ROLLBACK;
      RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
        -20001,
        'Approval failed: ' || SQLERRM
      );
  END;
  -- APEX Dynamic Action: Refresh on Filter Change
  -- Event: Change
  -- Selection Type: Item(s)
  -- Items: P1_APPROVAL_STATUS, P1_START_DATE, P1_END_DATE
  -- True Action: Refresh
  -- Affected Elements: Invoice_Report (Interactive Report)
  -- APEX Validation: Date Range
  -- Type: PL/SQL Expression
  BEGIN
    IF TO_DATE(:P1_START_DATE,'DD-MON-YYYY') >
       TO_DATE(:P1_END_DATE,'DD-MON-YYYY')
    THEN
      RETURN FALSE;
    END IF;
    RETURN TRUE;
  END;
  -- APEX Chart SQL: Invoice Amount by Month
  SELECT
    TO_CHAR(invoice_date,'MON-YYYY') AS month_label,
    SUM(invoice_amount)              AS total_amount,
    COUNT(*)                         AS invoice_count
  FROM
    ap_invoices_all
  WHERE
    org_id = :P0_ORG_ID
    AND invoice_date >= ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,-12)
  GROUP BY
    TO_CHAR(invoice_date,'MON-YYYY'),
    TRUNC(invoice_date,'MM')
  ORDER BY
    TRUNC(invoice_date,'MM')

ADFBusinessComponent sampleCode: |
  // Entity Object: APInvoiceEO
  // Base Table: AP_INVOICES_ALL
  package xxcust.oracle.apps.ap.model;
  import oracle.jbo.Key;
  import oracle.jbo.ValidationException;
  import oracle.jbo.server.EntityImpl;
  import oracle.jbo.server.EntityDefImpl;
  import java.math.BigDecimal;
  public class APInvoiceEOImpl extends EntityImpl {
    // Validate invoice amount
    public void setInvoiceAmount(BigDecimal amount) {
      if (amount == null || amount.compareTo(
            BigDecimal.ZERO) <= 0) {
        throw new ValidationException(
          "Invoice amount must be greater than zero"
        );
      }
      if (amount.compareTo(
            new BigDecimal("10000000")) > 0) {
        throw new ValidationException(
          "Invoice amount exceeds maximum limit"
        );
      }
      setAttributeInternal(INVOICEAMOUNT, amount);
    }
    // Validate date range
    public void setInvoiceDate(
      oracle.jbo.domain.Date invoiceDate
    ) {
      if (invoiceDate == null) {
        throw new ValidationException(
          "Invoice date is required"
        );
      }
      // Invoice date cannot be future dated
      if (invoiceDate.dateValue().after(
            new java.util.Date())) {
        throw new ValidationException(
          "Invoice date cannot be in the future"
        );
      }
      setAttributeInternal(INVOICEDATE, invoiceDate);
    }
        // Custom method: Check for duplicates
    public boolean isDuplicateInvoice() {
      String query =
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ap_invoices_all " +
        "WHERE vendor_id = :1 " +
        "AND invoice_num = :2 " +
        "AND org_id = :3 " +
        "AND invoice_id != :4";
      oracle.jbo.server.DBTransaction txn =
        getDBTransaction();
      java.sql.PreparedStatement stmt = null;
      java.sql.ResultSet rs = null;
      try {
        stmt = txn.createPreparedStatement(query, 1);
        stmt.setLong(1, ((BigDecimal)
          getVendorId()).longValue());
        stmt.setString(2, (String)getInvoiceNum());
        stmt.setLong(3, ((BigDecimal)
          getOrgId()).longValue());
        stmt.setLong(4, ((BigDecimal)
          getInvoiceId()).longValue());
        rs = stmt.executeQuery();
        if (rs.next()) {
          return rs.getInt(1) > 0;
        }
      } catch (java.sql.SQLException e) {
        throw new oracle.jbo.JboException(e);
      } finally {
        try {
          if (rs != null) rs.close();
          if (stmt != null) stmt.close();
        } catch (java.sql.SQLException e) {
          // ignore
        }
      }
      return false;
    }
    // Override doDML for audit trail
    protected void doDML(
      int operation,
      oracle.jbo.server.TransactionEvent e
    ) {
      super.doDML(operation, e);
      // Log audit trail
      if (operation == DML_INSERT ||
          operation == DML_UPDATE) {
        String action = (operation == DML_INSERT)
          ? "INSERT" : "UPDATE";
        // Call audit package
        oracle.jbo.server.DBTransaction txn =
          getDBTransaction();
        try {
          java.sql.CallableStatement cs =
            txn.createCallableStatement(
              "BEGIN xxcust_ap_utils_pkg.log_audit_trail(" +
              ":1,:2,:3,:4,:5,:6); END;", 1
            );
          cs.setString(1, "AP_INVOICES_ALL");
          cs.setLong(2, ((BigDecimal)
            getInvoiceId()).longValue());
          cs.setString(3, action);
          cs.setString(4, null);
          cs.setString(5, getInvoiceNum().toString());
          cs.setLong(6, ((BigDecimal)
            getCreatedBy()).longValue());
          cs.execute();
          cs.close();
        } catch (java.sql.SQLException ex) {
          throw new oracle.jbo.JboException(ex);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  // View Object SQL: APInvoiceSearchVO
  SELECT
    aia.invoice_id,
    aia.invoice_num,
    aia.invoice_date,
    aia.invoice_amount,
    aia.invoice_currency_code,
    aia.approval_status,
    aia.payment_status_flag,
    aia.description,
    aia.gl_date,
    aia.org_id,
    pv.vendor_name,
    pv.segment1 AS vendor_num,
    pvs.vendor_site_code,
    apt.name AS terms_name,
    gcc.concatenated_segments AS account_code,
    (aia.invoice_amount -
     NVL(aia.amount_paid,0)) AS amount_due
  FROM
    ap_invoices_all       aia,
    po_vendors            pv,
    po_vendor_sites_all   pvs,
    ap_terms              apt,
    gl_code_combinations_kfv gcc,
    ap_invoice_distributions_all aida
  WHERE
    aia.vendor_id       = pv.vendor_id
    AND aia.vendor_site_id  = pvs.vendor_site_id
    AND aia.terms_id        = apt.term_id
    AND aida.invoice_id     = aia.invoice_id
    AND aida.dist_code_combination_id
                            = gcc.code_combination_id
    AND aia.org_id          = :pOrgId
    AND aida.line_type_lookup_code = 'ITEM'
    AND aida.reversal_flag != 'Y'

  <!-- JavaScript ViewModel -->
  <!-- File: invoice-dashboard-page-viewModel.js -->
  define([
    'knockout',
    'ojs/ojcontext',
    'ojs/ojarraydataprovider',
    'ojs/ojbufferingdataprovider',
    'text!./invoice-dashboard-page.html'
  ], function(
    ko,
    Context,
    ArrayDataProvider,
    BufferingDataProvider
  ) {
    function InvoiceDashboardViewModel() {
      var self = this;
      // Observable properties
      self.totalInvoices    = ko.observable(0);
      self.pendingApproval  = ko.observable(0);
      self.overdueInvoices  = ko.observable(0);
      self.totalAmount      = ko.observable('$0');
      self.startDateValue   = ko.observable();
      self.endDateValue     = ko.observable();
      self.approvalStatusValue = ko.observable();
      self.noRowsSelected   = ko.observable(true);
      // Invoice table columns
      self.invoiceColumns = [
        {
          field: 'invoiceNum',
          headerText: 'Invoice Number',
          sortable: 'enabled'
        },
        {
          field: 'vendorName',
          headerText: 'Vendor Name',
          sortable: 'enabled'
        },
        {
          field: 'invoiceDate',
          headerText: 'Invoice Date',
          sortable: 'enabled',
          template: 'dateTemplate'
        },
        {
          field: 'invoiceAmount',
          headerText: 'Amount',
          sortable: 'enabled',
          className: 'oj-helper-text-align-end'
        },
        {
          field: 'approvalStatus',
          headerText: 'Status',
          template: 'statusTemplate'
        },
        {
          field: 'amountDue',
          headerText: 'Amount Due',
          sortable: 'enabled',
          className: 'oj-helper-text-align-end'
        }
      ];
      // Load invoices from REST API
      self.loadInvoices = function() {
        var url = '/xxcust/rest/v1/invoices?' +
          'startDate=' + self.startDateValue() +
          '&endDate=' + self.endDateValue() +
          '&approvalStatus=' +
          self.approvalStatusValue();
        fetch(url, {
          method: 'GET',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            'Authorization': 'Bearer ' +
              self.getAuthToken()
          }
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
          self.invoiceDataProvider(
            new ArrayDataProvider(
              data.invoices,
              { keyAttributes: 'invoiceId' }
            )
          );
          self.updateKPIs(data.summary);
        })
        .catch(error => {
          console.error('Error loading invoices:', error);
        });
      };
      // Update KPI metrics
      self.updateKPIs = function(summary) {
        self.totalInvoices(summary.totalCount);
        self.pendingApproval(summary.pendingCount);
        self.overdueInvoices(summary.overdueCount);
        self.totalAmount(
          new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', {
            style: 'currency',
            currency: 'USD'
          }).format(summary.totalAmount)
        );
      };
      // Approve selected invoices
      self.approveSelected = function() {
        var selectedKeys = [];
        // Get selected rows
        self.invoiceTable.getSelectedRowKeys()
          .then(function(keys) {
            keys.forEach(function(key) {
              selectedKeys.push(key);
            });
            // Call approval REST endpoint
            fetch('/xxcust/rest/v1/invoices/approve', {
              method: 'POST',
              headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'Authorization': 'Bearer ' +
                  self.getAuthToken()
              },
              body: JSON.stringify({
                invoiceIds: selectedKeys
              })
            })
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
              if (data.status === 'SUCCESS') {
                self.loadInvoices();
              }
            });
          });
      };
    }
    return InvoiceDashboardViewModel;
  });
