ModuleKnowledge Instance: AP Complete
Topic 1: AP Functional Setup
knowledgeId: AP-FUNC-001
moduleCode: AP
topicName: Accounts Payable Complete Functional Setup
topicCategory: SETUP
difficulty: INTERMEDIATE
learningObjectives: [
  Understand AP system options configuration,
  Configure payment terms,
  Set up supplier master,
  Configure payment methods,
  Understand multi-org setup for AP,
  Configure AP accounting options,
  Set up AP approval workflows,
  Understand financial options impact on AP
]
keyConceptsExplained: |
  ACCOUNTS PAYABLE SETUP SEQUENCE:
  1. FINANCIAL OPTIONS (Setup > Options > Financial)
     - Liability Account: Default for all invoices
     - Prepayment Account: For advance payments
     - Future Dated Payment Account
     - Discount Taken Account
     - Interest Account
     - Exchange Gain/Loss Accounts
  2. PAYABLES OPTIONS (Setup > Options > Payables)
     - Invoice Options:
       * Allow Online Validation
       * Allow Force Approval  
       * Require Validation before Payment
       * GL Date Basis (Invoice/System/Accounting)
     - Payment Options:
       * Allow Document Category Override
       * Always Take Discount
       * Exclude Tax from Discount Calculation
     - Expense Report Options:
       * Apply Advances
       * Expense Report Template
  3. PAYMENT TERMS (Setup > Payment > Terms)
     - Term Name and Description
     - Cutoff Day
     - Payment Lines:
       * Due Days / Due Date
       * Discount % and Days
     Examples:
       NET30: Due in 30 days
       2/10NET30: 2% discount if paid in 10 days
       IMMEDIATE: Due immediately
  4. SUPPLIER SETUP (Suppliers > Entry)
     - Supplier Header:
       * Supplier Name (must be unique)
       * Supplier Number (auto or manual)
       * Supplier Type: VENDOR/EMPLOYEE/TEMPORARY
       * Tax Registration Number
       * Tax Reporting Name
     - Supplier Sites:
       * Site Code and Address
       * Payment Method
       * Payment Terms
       * Bank Account
       * Invoice Currency
       * ORG_ID assignment (Multi-org)
  5. PAYMENT METHODS (Setup > Payment > Methods)
     - CHECK: Paper check
     - EFT: Electronic Funds Transfer
     - WIRE: Wire Transfer
     - CLEARING: Internal clearing
stepByStepGuide: |
  STEP 1: Navigate to
  Payables > Setup > Options > Financial Options
  Configure: Liability, Prepayment accounts
  STEP 2: Navigate to
  Payables > Setup > Options > Payables Options
  Configure: Invoice options, Payment options
  STEP 3: Navigate to
  Payables > Setup > Payment > Payment Terms
  Create terms used by your suppliers
  STEP 4: Navigate to
  Payables > Suppliers > Entry
  Create supplier master with sites
  STEP 5: Navigate to
  Payables > Setup > Banks
  Create bank and bank accounts
  STEP 6: Navigate to
  Payables > Setup > Payment > Payment Documents
  Set up check stock for each bank account
commonMistakes: [
  Not assigning supplier site to correct ORG_ID,
  Missing liability account in financial options,
  Payment terms mismatch between PO and invoice,
  Bank account not linked to payment document,
  Duplicate supplier names causing merge issues,
  Tax registration number format mismatch,
  GL date basis set incorrectly causing period issues
]
Topic 2: AP Invoice Processing
knowledgeId: AP-FUNC-002
moduleCode: AP
topicName: AP Invoice Processing End-to-End
topicCategory: FUNCTIONAL
difficulty: INTERMEDIATE
learningObjectives: [
  Understand invoice types in AP,
  Enter and validate standard invoices,
  Process credit memos and debit memos,
  Handle prepayments and advances,
  Understand matching types PO/Receipt,
  Process invoice holds and releases,
  Understand approval workflow,
  Post invoices to GL,
  Query and report on invoices
]
keyConceptsExplained: |
  INVOICE TYPES:
  1. STANDARD: Normal supplier invoice
  2. CREDIT MEMO: Supplier credit
  3. DEBIT MEMO: Company debit to supplier
  4. PREPAYMENT: Advance payment to supplier
  5. EXPENSE REPORT: Employee expense
  6. INTEREST: Late payment interest
  7. MIXED: Combination of positive/negative lines
  8. QUICKDEFAULT: Quick entry invoice
    MATCHING TYPES (continued):
  3. 4-WAY MATCH: Invoice matched to PO + Receipt + Inspection
     Additional: Accepted Qty <= Received Qty
     Used when: Quality inspection required
  INVOICE HOLDS:
  System Holds (Automatic):
  - AMOUNT RANGE: Invoice amount outside tolerance
  - CANT CLOSE PO: PO cannot be finally closed
  - CURR DIFF: Currency conversion issue
  - DIST ACCT INVALID: Distribution account invalid
  - DIST VARIANCE: Distributions don't equal invoice
  - DUPLICATE INVOICE: Duplicate detected
  - FUTURE PERIOD: GL date in future period
  - INVALID PO: PO not found or invalid
  - MATCHING REQUIRED: Matching rules require match
  - MAX AMOUNT: Exceeds maximum invoice amount
  - MAX RATE AMOUNT: Exchange rate tolerance exceeded
  - MAX QTY ORD: Quantity exceeds PO quantity
  - MAX QTY REC: Quantity exceeds receipt quantity
  - MAX SHIP AMOUNT: Shipment amount exceeded
  - NO RATE: Exchange rate not found
  - NATURAL ACCOUNT TAX: Tax account issue
  - ORG DOC SEQ: Document sequence issue
  - PAYMENT BASIS: Payment basis mismatch
  - PO NOT APPROVED: PO not in approved status
  - PRICE: Price outside tolerance
  - QTY ORD: Quantity ordered tolerance exceeded
  - QTY REC: Quantity received tolerance exceeded
  - REC EXCEPTION: Receipt exception exists
  - TAX DIFF: Tax amount difference
  - TAX VARIANCE: Tax variance tolerance exceeded
  - VENDOR: Supplier on payment hold
  Manual Holds:
  - Can be applied by AP clerks
  - Require manual release
  - Customizable hold names
  INVOICE APPROVAL STATUS:
  - NEVER APPROVED: Initial status
  - NEEDS REAPPROVAL: Changed after approval  
  - APPROVED: Ready for payment
  - CANCELLED: Invoice cancelled
  - UNPAID: Approved but not paid
  - AVAILABLE: Available for payment
stepByStepGuide: |
  INVOICE ENTRY STEPS:
  STEP 1: Navigate to
  Payables > Invoices > Entry > Invoices
  STEP 2: Enter Invoice Header
  - Supplier Name or Number
  - Supplier Site
  - Invoice Number (must be unique per supplier)
  - Invoice Date
  - Invoice Amount
  - Currency
  - Payment Terms
  - GL Date
  - Description
  STEP 3: Enter Invoice Lines
  Click Lines tab:
  - Line Type: ITEM/FREIGHT/TAX/MISCELLANEOUS
  - Amount
  - Description
  - Account (CCID)
  - Tax Code (if applicable)
  STEP 4: Match to PO (if required)
  Click Match button:
  - Search by PO Number
  - Select PO Lines or Shipments
  - Enter quantity and amount to match
  STEP 5: Validate Invoice
  Click Validate button or run:
  Payables > Invoice Validation concurrent program
  STEP 6: Handle Holds (if any)
  Navigate to Holds tab:
  - Review hold reasons
  - Resolve underlying issues
  - Release holds manually where allowed
  STEP 7: Approve Invoice
  Navigate to Actions > Approve
  Or workflow routes for approval
  STEP 8: Create Accounting
  Actions > Create Accounting
  Or run: Create Accounting concurrent program
  STEP 9: Transfer to GL
  Run: Payables Transfer to General Ledger
  Verify: Journal entries created in GL
sqlExamples: [
  Example 1: AP Invoice Query
  exampleName: Complete AP Invoice Detail Query
  difficultyLevel: INTERMEDIATE
  businessPurpose: |
    Fetch complete invoice details with
    vendor, site, payment status and
    distribution information
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      aia.invoice_id,
      aia.invoice_num,
      aia.invoice_type_lookup_code     AS invoice_type,
      aia.invoice_date,
      aia.invoice_amount,
      aia.amount_paid,
      (aia.invoice_amount -
       NVL(aia.amount_paid,0))         AS amount_due,
      aia.invoice_currency_code        AS currency,
      aia.payment_status_flag,
      aia.approval_status,
      aia.cancelled_date,
      aia.cancelled_amount,
      aia.gl_date,
      aia.description,
      pv.vendor_name,
      pv.segment1                      AS vendor_num,
      pvs.vendor_site_code,
      pvs.address_line1,
      pvs.city,
      pvs.country,
      apt.name                         AS payment_terms,
      aia.pay_group_lookup_code,
      aia.source,
      aia.org_id,
      haou.name                        AS operating_unit,
      -- Hold information
      (SELECT COUNT(*)
       FROM   ap_holds_all ah
       WHERE  ah.invoice_id = aia.invoice_id
       AND    ah.release_lookup_code IS NULL)
                                       AS hold_count,
      -- Distribution count
      (SELECT COUNT(*)
       FROM   ap_invoice_distributions_all aida
       WHERE  aida.invoice_id = aia.invoice_id
       AND    aida.reversal_flag != 'Y')
                                       AS dist_count,
      -- Payment information
      (SELECT LISTAGG(aca.check_number,',')
              WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY aca.check_number)
       FROM   ap_invoice_payments_all aipa,
              ap_checks_all aca
       WHERE  aipa.invoice_id = aia.invoice_id
       AND    aipa.check_id = aca.check_id)
                                       AS payment_numbers
    FROM
      ap_invoices_all         aia,
      po_vendors              pv,
      po_vendor_sites_all     pvs,
      ap_terms                apt,
      hr_all_organization_units haou
    WHERE
      aia.vendor_id         = pv.vendor_id
      AND aia.vendor_site_id    = pvs.vendor_site_id
      AND aia.terms_id          = apt.term_id
      AND aia.org_id            = haou.organization_id
      AND aia.org_id            = :p_org_id
      AND aia.invoice_date
          BETWEEN :p_start_date AND :p_end_date
      AND aia.cancelled_date    IS NULL
    ORDER BY
      pv.vendor_name,
      aia.invoice_date DESC
  Example 2: AP Aging Query
  exampleName: AP Supplier Aging Report
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Calculate AP aging buckets for
    outstanding supplier invoices
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      pv.vendor_name,
      pv.segment1                        AS vendor_num,
      pvs.vendor_site_code,
      SUM(aia.invoice_amount -
          NVL(aia.amount_paid,0))        AS total_outstanding,
      -- Current (not yet due)
      SUM(CASE
            WHEN (aia.invoice_date +
                  NVL(apt.terms_days,0)) >= TRUNC(SYSDATE)
            THEN (aia.invoice_amount -
                  NVL(aia.amount_paid,0))
            ELSE 0
          END)                           AS current_amount,
            -- 1-30 days overdue
      SUM(CASE
            WHEN (aia.invoice_date +
                  NVL(apt.terms_days,0))
                 BETWEEN TRUNC(SYSDATE) - 30
                 AND TRUNC(SYSDATE) - 1
            THEN (aia.invoice_amount -
                  NVL(aia.amount_paid,0))
            ELSE 0
          END)                           AS overdue_1_30,
      -- 31-60 days overdue
      SUM(CASE
            WHEN (aia.invoice_date +
                  NVL(apt.terms_days,0))
                 BETWEEN TRUNC(SYSDATE) - 60
                 AND TRUNC(SYSDATE) - 31
            THEN (aia.invoice_amount -
                  NVL(aia.amount_paid,0))
            ELSE 0
          END)                           AS overdue_31_60,
      -- 61-90 days overdue
      SUM(CASE
            WHEN (aia.invoice_date +
                  NVL(apt.terms_days,0))
                 BETWEEN TRUNC(SYSDATE) - 90
                 AND TRUNC(SYSDATE) - 61
            THEN (aia.invoice_amount -
                  NVL(aia.amount_paid,0))
            ELSE 0
          END)                           AS overdue_61_90,
      -- Over 90 days overdue
      SUM(CASE
            WHEN (aia.invoice_date +
                  NVL(apt.terms_days,0))
                 < TRUNC(SYSDATE) - 90
            THEN (aia.invoice_amount -
                  NVL(aia.amount_paid,0))
            ELSE 0
          END)                           AS overdue_over_90
    FROM
      ap_invoices_all       aia,
      po_vendors            pv,
      po_vendor_sites_all   pvs,
      ap_terms              apt
    WHERE
      aia.vendor_id         = pv.vendor_id
      AND aia.vendor_site_id    = pvs.vendor_site_id
      AND aia.terms_id          = apt.term_id
      AND aia.payment_status_flag != 'Y'
      AND aia.cancelled_date    IS NULL
      AND aia.org_id            = :p_org_id
    GROUP BY
      pv.vendor_name,
      pv.segment1,
      pvs.vendor_site_code
    HAVING
      SUM(aia.invoice_amount -
          NVL(aia.amount_paid,0)) > 0
    ORDER BY
      SUM(aia.invoice_amount -
          NVL(aia.amount_paid,0)) DESC
  Example 3: AP Payment Query
  exampleName: AP Payment Register with Clearing Status
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Complete payment register showing
    payment details, clearing status,
    bank reconciliation information
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      aca.check_id,
      aca.check_number,
      aca.check_date,
      aca.amount                       AS payment_amount,
      aca.currency_code,
      aca.status_lookup_code           AS payment_status,
      aca.cleared_date,
      aca.cleared_amount,
      aca.void_date,
      aca.void_reason,
      aca.stop_initiated_date,
      pv.vendor_name,
      pv.segment1                      AS vendor_num,
      pvs.vendor_site_code,
      aba.bank_account_name,
      aba.bank_account_num,
      ab.bank_name,
      -- Payment type
      aca.payment_type_flag,
      -- Associated invoices
      (SELECT LISTAGG(
                aia.invoice_num, ', ')
              WITHIN GROUP (
                ORDER BY aia.invoice_num)
       FROM   ap_invoice_payments_all aipa,
              ap_invoices_all aia
       WHERE  aipa.check_id = aca.check_id
       AND    aipa.invoice_id = aia.invoice_id)
                                       AS invoice_numbers,
      -- Total invoices paid
      (SELECT SUM(aipa.amount)
       FROM   ap_invoice_payments_all aipa
       WHERE  aipa.check_id = aca.check_id)
                                       AS total_invoice_amount,
      -- Discount taken
      (SELECT SUM(aipa.discount_taken)
       FROM   ap_invoice_payments_all aipa
       WHERE  aipa.check_id = aca.check_id)
                                       AS discount_taken,
      aca.org_id,
      haou.name                        AS operating_unit
    FROM
      ap_checks_all           aca,
      po_vendors              pv,
      po_vendor_sites_all     pvs,
      ap_bank_accounts_all    aba,
      ap_banks_all            ab,
      hr_all_organization_units haou
    WHERE
      aca.vendor_id         = pv.vendor_id
      AND aca.vendor_site_id    = pvs.vendor_site_id
      AND aca.bank_account_id   = aba.bank_account_id
      AND aba.bank_id           = ab.bank_id
      AND aca.org_id            = haou.organization_id
      AND aca.org_id            = :p_org_id
      AND aca.check_date
          BETWEEN :p_start_date AND :p_end_date
      AND aca.status_lookup_code
          NOT IN ('VOIDED','SPOILED','OVERFLOW')
    ORDER BY
      aca.check_date DESC,
      aca.check_number
  Example 4: AP Invoice Distribution Query
  exampleName: AP Invoice Distribution with GL Account
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Fetch invoice distributions with
    full GL account segments for
    reconciliation and audit
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      aia.invoice_num,
      aia.invoice_date,
      aia.invoice_amount,
      pv.vendor_name,
      aida.distribution_line_number,
      aida.line_type_lookup_code       AS dist_type,
      aida.amount                      AS dist_amount,
      aida.base_amount                 AS func_amount,
      aida.accounting_date,
      aida.posted_flag,
      aida.reversal_flag,
      aida.description                 AS dist_description,
      -- GL Account Segments
      gcc.segment1                     AS company,
      gcc.segment2                     AS cost_center,
      gcc.segment3                     AS account,
      gcc.segment4                     AS sub_account,
      gcc.segment5                     AS product,
      gcc.concatenated_segments        AS full_account,
      -- Project information (if PA enabled)
      aida.project_id,
      aida.task_id,
      aida.expenditure_type,
      aida.expenditure_item_date,
      -- Tax information
      aida.tax_code_id,
      aida.tax_recoverable_flag,
      -- Matching information
      aida.po_distribution_id,
      aida.rcv_transaction_id,
            -- Accounting entries (continued)
      (SELECT xal.entered_dr
       FROM   xla_ae_lines xal,
              xla_ae_headers xah,
              xla_events xe
       WHERE  xal.ae_header_id     = xah.ae_header_id
       AND    xah.event_id         = xe.event_id
       AND    xe.entity_id         = aida.invoice_id
       AND    xal.code_combination_id
                                   = aida.dist_code_combination_id
       AND    xah.gl_transfer_status_code = 'Y'
       AND    ROWNUM = 1)          AS xla_entered_dr,
      (SELECT xal.entered_cr
       FROM   xla_ae_lines xal,
              xla_ae_headers xah,
              xla_events xe
       WHERE  xal.ae_header_id     = xah.ae_header_id
       AND    xah.event_id         = xe.event_id
       AND    xe.entity_id         = aida.invoice_id
       AND    xal.code_combination_id
                                   = aida.dist_code_combination_id
       AND    xah.gl_transfer_status_code = 'Y'
       AND    ROWNUM = 1)          AS xla_entered_cr
    FROM
      ap_invoices_all              aia,
      ap_invoice_distributions_all aida,
      po_vendors                   pv,
      gl_code_combinations         gcc
    WHERE
      aia.invoice_id        = aida.invoice_id
      AND aia.vendor_id         = pv.vendor_id
      AND aida.dist_code_combination_id
                                = gcc.code_combination_id
      AND aida.reversal_flag    != 'Y'
      AND aia.org_id            = :p_org_id
      AND aia.invoice_date
          BETWEEN :p_start_date AND :p_end_date
    ORDER BY
      aia.invoice_num,
      aida.distribution_line_number
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION O — GENERAL LEDGER COMPLETE KNOWLEDGE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Topic 3: GL Functional Setup
knowledgeId: GL-FUNC-001
moduleCode: GL
topicName: General Ledger Complete Functional Setup
topicCategory: SETUP
difficulty: ADVANCED
learningObjectives: [
  Understand Chart of Accounts structure,
  Configure Ledger and Ledger Sets,
  Set up Accounting Calendar,
  Configure Currency and Conversion rates,
  Understand Subledger Accounting (SLA/XLA),
  Set up Journal Sources and Categories,
  Configure Period Close process,
  Understand Consolidation setup,
  Configure FSG Financial Reports,
  Set up GL Security Rules and Cross Validation Rules
]
keyConceptsExplained: |
  1. CHART OF ACCOUNTS (COA)
     Structure: Key Flexfield (KFF)
     Flexfield Code: GL#
     Typical Segments:
     Segment 1: Company/Entity (4 chars)
     Segment 2: Cost Center (4 chars)
     Segment 3: Account (6 chars)
     Segment 4: Sub-Account (4 chars)
     Segment 5: Product/Project (4 chars)
     Segment 6: Intercompany (4 chars)
     Account Types:
     A = Asset
     L = Liability
     O = Owner Equity
     R = Revenue
     E = Expense
     Natural Account Rules:
     - Assets: Debit normal balance
     - Liabilities: Credit normal balance
     - Equity: Credit normal balance
     - Revenue: Credit normal balance
     - Expenses: Debit normal balance
  2. LEDGER CONFIGURATION
     Primary Ledger:
     - Name
     - Currency (Functional Currency)
     - Calendar
     - Chart of Accounts
     - Subledger Accounting Method
     Secondary Ledger:
     - Used for reporting in different currency
     - Linked to Primary Ledger
     - Can have different COA
     Reporting Currency:
     - Subset of Secondary Ledger
     - Currency translation only
  3. ACCOUNTING CALENDAR
     - Period Types: Month/Quarter/Year
     - Period Names: JAN-25, FEB-25, etc.
     - Year Type: Calendar/Fiscal
     - Adjusting Periods: 
       Usually 1-2 per year for year-end
  4. JOURNAL SOURCES AND CATEGORIES
     Standard Sources:
     - Payables (from AP)
     - Receivables (from AR)
     - Assets (from FA)
     - Payroll (from PAY)
     - Manual (manual entries)
     - Spreadsheet (ADI/WEBADI)
     Standard Categories:
     - Purchase Invoices
     - Payments
     - Sales Invoices
     - Receipts
     - Depreciation
     - Revaluation
  5. SUBLEDGER ACCOUNTING (XLA)
     Flow:
     Subledger Transaction
     → Create Accounting (XLA)
     → XLA Accounting Entries (xla_ae_lines)
     → Transfer to GL (gl_je_lines)
     → GL Balances (gl_balances)
     Key Tables:
     xla_events
     xla_ae_headers
     xla_ae_lines
     xla_distribution_links
     gl_import_references
     gl_je_headers
     gl_je_lines
     gl_balances
  6. PERIOD CLOSE PROCESS
     Month-End Close Sequence:
     Step 1: Close AP Period
       - All invoices validated
       - All payments processed
       - AP Transfer to GL complete
     Step 2: Close AR Period
       - All invoices generated
       - All receipts applied
       - AR Transfer to GL complete
     Step 3: Close FA Period
       - Depreciation run complete
       - Additions/Disposals processed
       - FA Transfer to GL complete
     Step 4: Close Payroll Period
       - Payroll processed
       - Costing complete
       - Payroll Transfer to GL complete
     Step 5: GL Period Activities
       - Foreign Currency Revaluation
       - Allocation Journals
       - Intercompany Eliminations
       - FSG Reports generated
       - Trial Balance reviewed
       - Suspense account cleared
     Step 6: Close GL Period
       - Verify all journals posted
       - Run Trial Balance
       - Approve period closure
       - Close period in GL calendar
sqlExamples: [
  Example 5: GL Journal Query
  exampleName: GL Journal Lines with Full Account Detail
  difficultyLevel: INTERMEDIATE
  businessPurpose: |
    Query GL journal entries with full
    account segment details for
    audit and reconciliation
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      gjh.je_header_id,
      gjh.name                         AS journal_name,
      gjh.je_source,
      gjh.je_category,
      gjh.period_name,
      gjh.posted_date,
      gjh.status                       AS journal_status,
      gjh.running_total_dr,
      gjh.running_total_cr,
      gjh.description                  AS header_description,
      gjh.created_by,
      gjh.creation_date,
      gjl.je_line_num,
      gjl.entered_dr,
      gjl.entered_cr,
      gjl.accounted_dr,
      gjl.accounted_cr,
      gjl.description                  AS line_description,
      gjl.effective_date,
            -- Account segments (continued)
      gcc.segment1                     AS company,
      gcc.segment2                     AS cost_center,
      gcc.segment3                     AS account,
      gcc.segment4                     AS sub_account,
      gcc.segment5                     AS product,
      gcc.concatenated_segments        AS full_account,
      -- Account type
      fav.compiled_value_attributes    AS account_type,
      -- Ledger information
      gl.name                          AS ledger_name,
      gl.currency_code                 AS ledger_currency,
      -- Source document reference
      gjl.reference_1                  AS source_doc_1,
      gjl.reference_2                  AS source_doc_2,
      gjl.reference_3                  AS source_doc_3,
      gjl.reference_4                  AS source_doc_4,
      -- Subledger reference
      gir.reference_1                  AS sl_reference_1,
      gir.reference_2                  AS sl_reference_2
    FROM
      gl_je_headers              gjh,
      gl_je_lines                gjl,
      gl_code_combinations       gcc,
      gl_ledgers                 gl,
      fnd_flex_values            fav,
      fnd_flex_value_sets        favs,
      gl_import_references       gir
    WHERE
      gjh.je_header_id      = gjl.je_header_id
      AND gjl.code_combination_id
                                = gcc.code_combination_id
      AND gjh.ledger_id         = gl.ledger_id
      AND favs.flex_value_set_name
                                = 'XX_ACCOUNT_TYPE_VS'
      AND fav.flex_value_set_id = favs.flex_value_set_id
      AND fav.flex_value        = gcc.segment3
      AND gir.je_header_id(+)   = gjl.je_header_id
      AND gir.je_line_num(+)    = gjl.je_line_num
      AND gjh.status            = 'P'
      AND gjh.ledger_id         = :p_ledger_id
      AND gjh.period_name       = :p_period_name
      AND gjh.je_source         = NVL(:p_source,
                                      gjh.je_source)
    ORDER BY
      gjh.je_source,
      gjh.name,
      gjl.je_line_num
  Example 6: GL Trial Balance Query
  exampleName: GL Trial Balance with Opening and Closing
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Generate trial balance showing
    opening balance, period activity,
    and closing balance for all accounts
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      gcc.segment1                     AS company,
      gcc.segment2                     AS cost_center,
      gcc.segment3                     AS account,
      gcc.segment4                     AS sub_account,
      gcc.segment5                     AS product,
      gcc.concatenated_segments        AS full_account,
      fav.description                  AS account_description,
      fav.compiled_value_attributes    AS account_type,
      -- Opening Balance
      (gb.begin_balance_dr -
       gb.begin_balance_cr)            AS opening_balance,
      -- Period Activity
      gb.period_net_dr                 AS period_debit,
      gb.period_net_cr                 AS period_credit,
      (gb.period_net_dr -
       gb.period_net_cr)               AS net_activity,
      -- Closing Balance
      (gb.begin_balance_dr +
       gb.period_net_dr -
       gb.begin_balance_cr -
       gb.period_net_cr)               AS closing_balance,
      -- YTD Balances
      gb.project_to_date_dr            AS ytd_debit,
      gb.project_to_date_cr            AS ytd_credit,
      (gb.project_to_date_dr -
       gb.project_to_date_cr)          AS ytd_balance,
      -- Currency
      gb.currency_code,
      gb.translated_flag,
      gb.period_name,
      gl.name                          AS ledger_name
    FROM
      gl_balances                gb,
      gl_code_combinations       gcc,
      gl_ledgers                 gl,
      fnd_flex_values            fav,
      fnd_flex_value_sets        favs
    WHERE
      gb.code_combination_id    = gcc.code_combination_id
      AND gb.ledger_id              = gl.ledger_id
      AND favs.flex_value_set_name  = 'XX_NATURAL_ACCOUNT_VS'
      AND fav.flex_value_set_id     = favs.flex_value_set_id
      AND fav.flex_value            = gcc.segment3
      AND gcc.summary_flag          = 'N'
      AND gcc.enabled_flag          = 'Y'
      AND gb.actual_flag            = 'A'
      AND gb.currency_code          = :p_currency
      AND gb.ledger_id              = :p_ledger_id
      AND gb.period_name            = :p_period_name
      AND (gb.begin_balance_dr != 0
           OR gb.begin_balance_cr != 0
           OR gb.period_net_dr != 0
           OR gb.period_net_cr != 0)
    ORDER BY
      gcc.segment1,
      gcc.segment3,
      gcc.segment2
  Example 7: GL Budget vs Actual Query
  exampleName: GL Budget vs Actual Variance Analysis
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Compare actual GL balances against
    budget with variance analysis
    and percentage calculations
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      gcc.concatenated_segments        AS account,
      fav.description                  AS account_description,
      gb_act.period_name,
      -- Actual amounts
      (gb_act.period_net_dr -
       gb_act.period_net_cr)           AS actual_amount,
      -- Budget amounts
      (gb_bud.period_net_dr -
       gb_bud.period_net_cr)           AS budget_amount,
      -- Variance
      ((gb_act.period_net_dr -
        gb_act.period_net_cr) -
       (gb_bud.period_net_dr -
        gb_bud.period_net_cr))         AS variance_amount,
      -- Variance percentage
      CASE
        WHEN (gb_bud.period_net_dr -
              gb_bud.period_net_cr) = 0
        THEN NULL
        ELSE ROUND(
          ((gb_act.period_net_dr -
            gb_act.period_net_cr) -
           (gb_bud.period_net_dr -
            gb_bud.period_net_cr)) /
          ABS(gb_bud.period_net_dr -
              gb_bud.period_net_cr) * 100,
          2)
      END                              AS variance_pct,
            -- Favorable/Unfavorable indicator (continued)
        ELSE
          CASE
            WHEN (gb_act.period_net_dr -
                  gb_act.period_net_cr) >=
                 (gb_bud.period_net_dr -
                  gb_bud.period_net_cr)
            THEN 'FAVORABLE'
            ELSE 'UNFAVORABLE'
          END
      END                              AS variance_indicator,
      -- YTD Actual
      (gb_act.project_to_date_dr -
       gb_act.project_to_date_cr)      AS ytd_actual,
      -- YTD Budget
      (gb_bud.project_to_date_dr -
       gb_bud.project_to_date_cr)      AS ytd_budget,
      -- YTD Variance
      ((gb_act.project_to_date_dr -
        gb_act.project_to_date_cr) -
       (gb_bud.project_to_date_dr -
        gb_bud.project_to_date_cr))    AS ytd_variance
    FROM
      gl_balances                gb_act,
      gl_balances                gb_bud,
      gl_code_combinations       gcc,
      gl_ledgers                 gl,
      fnd_flex_values            fav,
      fnd_flex_value_sets        favs,
      gl_budget_versions         gbv
    WHERE
      gb_act.code_combination_id = gcc.code_combination_id
      AND gb_bud.code_combination_id
                                 = gcc.code_combination_id
      AND gb_act.ledger_id       = gl.ledger_id
      AND gb_bud.ledger_id       = gl.ledger_id
      AND gb_act.actual_flag     = 'A'
      AND gb_bud.actual_flag     = 'B'
      AND gb_bud.budget_version_id
                                 = gbv.budget_version_id
      AND gbv.budget_name        = :p_budget_name
      AND favs.flex_value_set_name
                                 = 'XX_NATURAL_ACCOUNT_VS'
      AND fav.flex_value_set_id  = favs.flex_value_set_id
      AND fav.flex_value         = gcc.segment3
      AND gcc.summary_flag       = 'N'
      AND gcc.enabled_flag       = 'Y'
      AND gb_act.currency_code   = :p_currency
      AND gb_act.ledger_id       = :p_ledger_id
      AND gb_act.period_name     = :p_period_name
      AND gb_bud.period_name     = :p_period_name
    ORDER BY
      gcc.segment3,
      gcc.segment1
  Example 8: GL Revaluation Query
  exampleName: GL Foreign Currency Revaluation
  difficultyLevel: EXPERT
  businessPurpose: |
    Query revaluation journals created
    during period end for foreign
    currency balance revaluation
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      gjh.name                         AS revaluation_journal,
      gjh.period_name,
      gjh.posted_date,
      gjh.running_total_dr             AS total_debit,
      gjh.running_total_cr             AS total_credit,
      gjh.currency_code                AS from_currency,
      gjh.currency_conversion_rate     AS exchange_rate,
      gjh.currency_conversion_date     AS rate_date,
      gjh.currency_conversion_type     AS rate_type,
      gjl.je_line_num,
      gjl.entered_dr,
      gjl.entered_cr,
      gjl.accounted_dr,
      gjl.accounted_cr,
      gcc.concatenated_segments        AS account,
      gcc.segment3                     AS natural_account,
      -- Gain or Loss indicator
      CASE
        WHEN gjl.accounted_dr > 0
        THEN 'UNREALIZED LOSS'
        ELSE 'UNREALIZED GAIN'
      END                              AS gain_loss_type,
      gl.name                          AS ledger_name,
      gl.currency_code                 AS functional_currency
    FROM
      gl_je_headers              gjh,
      gl_je_lines                gjl,
      gl_code_combinations       gcc,
      gl_ledgers                 gl
    WHERE
      gjh.je_header_id      = gjl.je_header_id
      AND gjl.code_combination_id
                                = gcc.code_combination_id
      AND gjh.ledger_id         = gl.ledger_id
      AND gjh.je_source         = 'Revaluation'
      AND gjh.je_category       = 'Revaluation'
      AND gjh.status            = 'P'
      AND gjh.ledger_id         = :p_ledger_id
      AND gjh.period_name       = :p_period_name
    ORDER BY
      gjh.name,
      gjl.je_line_num

SECTION P — ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE COMPLETE KNOWLEDGE

Topic 4: AR Functional Setup and Processing
knowledgeId: AR-FUNC-001
moduleCode: AR
topicName: Accounts Receivable Complete Functional Setup
topicCategory: SETUP
difficulty: INTERMEDIATE
learningObjectives: [
  Understand AR system options,
  Configure customer master,
  Set up transaction types,
  Configure receipt classes and methods,
  Understand AutoAccounting rules,
  Set up AutoInvoice,
  Configure dunning letters,
  Understand revenue recognition,
  Set up lockbox processing,
  Configure AR aging reports
]
keyConceptsExplained: |
  1. AR SYSTEM OPTIONS
     - Set of Books / Ledger
     - Accounting Method: Accrual/Cash
     - Unearned Revenue Account
     - Unbilled Receivable Account
     - AutoCash Rule Set
     - Realized Gains/Losses Account
     - Cross Currency Rate Type
  2. CUSTOMER MASTER SETUP
     Customer Header:
     - Customer Name (unique)
     - Customer Number (auto/manual)
     - Customer Type: ORGANIZATION/PERSON
     - Tax Registration Number
     - Tax Payer ID
     Customer Sites:
     - Bill-To Site: Invoicing address
     - Ship-To Site: Delivery address
     - Dunning Site: Collections contact
     - Statement Site: Statement delivery
     Customer Profile Class:
     - Credit Limit
     - Payment Terms
     - Statement Cycle
     - Dunning Letter Set
     - Interest Charge Flag
     - AutoReceipt: Yes/No
  3. TRANSACTION TYPES
     Invoice:
     - Class: INV
     - Sign: Positive
     - GL Account: Receivable
     Credit Memo:
     - Class: CM
     - Sign: Negative
     - Applied against: Invoice
     Debit Memo:
     - Class: DM
     - Sign: Positive
     - Used for: Adjustments/Charges
     Deposit:
     - Class: DEP
     - Advance payment from customer
     Guarantee:
     - Class: GUAR
     - Commitment to buy
  4. RECEIPT CLASSES AND METHODS
     Receipt Class:
     - Creation Method: Manual/Automatic/BR
     - Remittance: Yes/No
     - Clearance: By Matching/By Automatic/Direct
     Receipt Method:
     - Bank Account
     - Payment Method: CHECK/EFT/WIRE/CASH
     - Credit Card Processing
    5. AUTOACCOUNTING RULES (continued)
     Controls how AR automatically derives
     GL accounts for transactions
     Account Types Derived:
     - Revenue Account
     - Receivable Account
     - Freight Account
     - Tax Account
     - Unearned Revenue Account
     - Unbilled Receivable Account
     - AutoInvoice Clearing Account
     Segment Sources:
     - Constant: Fixed value always used
     - Transaction: From transaction header
     - Salesrep: From salesperson setup
     - Standard Lines: From item/line
     - Taxes: From tax code
     - Site: From customer site
  6. AUTOINVOICE SETUP
     Used to import invoices from:
     - Order Management (OM)
     - Projects (PA)
     - Custom Systems
     Key Tables:
     ra_interface_lines_all
     ra_interface_distributions_all
     ra_interface_salescredits_all
     Grouping Rules:
     - Controls which lines group together
     - Into single invoice
     Line Ordering Rules:
     - Controls line sequence within invoice
     Import Program:
     - AutoInvoice Master Program
     - AutoInvoice Import Program
  7. REVENUE RECOGNITION
     Methods:
     - Immediate: All revenue on invoice date
     - Rules-based: Spread over time
     Revenue Scheduling Rules:
     - Fixed Schedule: Specific dates
     - Variable: Based on event
     Revenue Contingencies:
     - Extended Payment Terms
     - Cancellation Right
     - Acceptance Clause
     - Refund Policy
  8. LOCKBOX PROCESSING
     Purpose: Automate cash application
     from bank statement file
     Process:
     Bank File (BAI/BAI2 format)
     → Transmission
     → Validation
     → QuickCash
     → AutoCash Rules Apply
     → Post QuickCash
     → Receipts Created
     → Applied to Invoices
  9. AR AGING BUCKETS
     Standard Aging Methods:
     - Aging - 4 Bucket
     - Aging - 7 Bucket
     Common Buckets:
     Current (0-30 days)
     31-60 days
     61-90 days
     91-120 days
     Over 120 days
stepByStepGuide: |
  AR INVOICE PROCESSING STEPS:
  STEP 1: Navigate to
  Receivables > Transactions > Transactions
  STEP 2: Enter Transaction Header
  - Transaction Type: Invoice/CM/DM
  - Transaction Date
  - GL Date
  - Currency
  - Bill-To Customer
  - Bill-To Site
  - Payment Terms
  - Salesperson
  STEP 3: Enter Transaction Lines
  - Item or Description
  - Quantity
  - Unit Price
  - Tax Code
  - Revenue Account (from AutoAccounting)
  STEP 4: Complete and Save
  - Complete transaction
  - Verify accounting entries
  STEP 5: Print Invoice
  - Print to send to customer
  STEP 6: Apply Receipt (when paid)
  Receivables > Receipts > Receipts
  - Enter receipt details
  - Apply to invoice
  STEP 7: Transfer to GL
  Run: General Ledger Transfer Program
  Verify: Journal entries in GL
  AR RECEIPT PROCESSING STEPS:
  STEP 1: Navigate to
  Receivables > Receipts > Receipts
  STEP 2: Enter Receipt Header
  - Receipt Type: Standard/Miscellaneous
  - Receipt Method
  - Receipt Number
  - Receipt Date
  - Currency
  - Amount
  - Customer Name
  STEP 3: Apply Receipt
  Click Apply:
  - Select invoice to apply
  - Enter application amount
  - Handle discounts if applicable
  STEP 4: Save and Complete
  - Verify application
  - Confirm status: Applied
sqlExamples: [
  Example 9: AR Invoice Query
  exampleName: AR Transaction Detail with Aging
  difficultyLevel: INTERMEDIATE
  businessPurpose: |
    Query AR transactions with customer
    details aging days and
    application status
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      rct.customer_trx_id,
      rct.trx_number                   AS invoice_number,
      rct.trx_date                     AS invoice_date,
      rct.due_date,
      rctt.name                        AS transaction_type,
      rctt.type                        AS trx_class,
      hca.account_number               AS customer_number,
      hp.party_name                    AS customer_name,
      hcsu.location                    AS site_code,
      rct.invoice_currency_code        AS currency,
      -- Invoice amounts
      rct.amount_due_original          AS original_amount,
      rct.amount_due_remaining         AS remaining_amount,
      (rct.amount_due_original -
       rct.amount_due_remaining)       AS amount_applied,
      -- Aging calculation
      TRUNC(SYSDATE) - TRUNC(rct.due_date)
                                       AS days_past_due,
      CASE
        WHEN TRUNC(SYSDATE) -
             TRUNC(rct.due_date) <= 0
        THEN 'CURRENT'
        WHEN TRUNC(SYSDATE) -
             TRUNC(rct.due_date) BETWEEN 1 AND 30
        THEN '1-30 DAYS'
        WHEN TRUNC(SYSDATE) -
             TRUNC(rct.due_date) BETWEEN 31 AND 60
        THEN '31-60 DAYS'
        WHEN TRUNC(SYSDATE) -
             TRUNC(rct.due_date) BETWEEN 61 AND 90
        THEN '61-90 DAYS'
        WHEN TRUNC(SYSDATE) -
             TRUNC(rct.due_date) BETWEEN 91 AND 120
        THEN '91-120 DAYS'
        ELSE 'OVER 120 DAYS'
      END                              AS aging_bucket,
      -- Payment status
      rct.status_trx                   AS trx_status,
      -- GL information
      rct.gl_date,
      -- Collector information
      arc.name                         AS collector_name,
      -- Credit information
      hcp.credit_hold                  AS on_credit_hold,
      hcp.credit_limit                 AS credit_limit,
      -- Balance check
      CASE
        WHEN hcp.credit_limit IS NOT NULL
        THEN ROUND(
          (rct.amount_due_remaining /
           hcp.credit_limit) * 100, 2)
        ELSE NULL
      END                              AS credit_utilization_pct
    FROM
      ra_customer_trx_all        rct,
      ra_cust_trx_types_all      rctt,
      hz_cust_accounts           hca,
      hz_parties                 hp,
      hz_cust_site_uses_all      hcsu,
      hz_cust_acct_sites_all     hcas,
      ar_collectors              arc,
      hz_customer_profiles       hcp
    WHERE
      rct.cust_trx_type_id      = rctt.cust_trx_type_id
      AND rct.bill_to_customer_id   = hca.cust_account_id
      AND hca.party_id              = hp.party_id
      AND rct.bill_to_site_use_id   = hcsu.site_use_id
      AND hcsu.cust_acct_site_id    = hcas.cust_acct_site_id
            AND hca.cust_account_id       = hcp.cust_account_id(+)
      AND hcp.collector_id          = arc.collector_id(+)
      AND rctt.org_id               = :p_org_id
      AND rct.org_id                = :p_org_id
      AND rct.complete_flag         = 'Y'
      AND rct.amount_due_remaining  > 0
      AND rct.status_trx            NOT IN ('CL','VOID')
    ORDER BY
      TRUNC(SYSDATE) - TRUNC(rct.due_date) DESC,
      rct.amount_due_remaining DESC
  Example 10: AR Receipt Application Query
  exampleName: AR Receipt Application Detail
  difficultyLevel: INTERMEDIATE
  businessPurpose: |
    Query receipt applications showing
    which invoices each receipt was
    applied to with discount details
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      acr.cash_receipt_id,
      acr.receipt_number,
      acr.receipt_date,
      acr.amount                       AS receipt_amount,
      acr.currency_code,
      acr.status                       AS receipt_status,
      hp.party_name                    AS customer_name,
      hca.account_number               AS customer_number,
      -- Application details
      ara.apply_date,
      ara.amount_applied,
      ara.discount_taken,
      ara.earned_discount_taken,
      ara.unearned_discount_taken,
      -- Applied-to invoice details
      rct.trx_number                   AS applied_invoice,
      rct.trx_date                     AS invoice_date,
      rct.amount_due_original          AS invoice_amount,
      rct.amount_due_remaining         AS invoice_balance,
      -- GL accounts
      gcc_rec.concatenated_segments    AS receivable_account,
      gcc_rev.concatenated_segments    AS revenue_account,
      -- Bank details
      aba.bank_account_name,
      aba.bank_account_num,
      ab.bank_name,
      -- Application type
      ara.application_type,
      ara.status                       AS application_status
    FROM
      ar_cash_receipts_all       acr,
      ar_receivable_applications_all ara,
      ra_customer_trx_all        rct,
      hz_cust_accounts           hca,
      hz_parties                 hp,
      ap_bank_accounts_all       aba,
      ap_banks_all               ab,
      gl_code_combinations       gcc_rec,
      gl_code_combinations       gcc_rev
    WHERE
      acr.cash_receipt_id       = ara.cash_receipt_id
      AND ara.applied_customer_trx_id
                                    = rct.customer_trx_id
      AND acr.pay_from_customer     = hca.cust_account_id
      AND hca.party_id              = hp.party_id
      AND acr.remit_bank_acct_use_id
                                    = aba.bank_account_id(+)
      AND aba.bank_id               = ab.bank_id(+)
      AND ara.code_combination_id   = gcc_rec.code_combination_id(+)
      AND ara.earned_discount_ccid  = gcc_rev.code_combination_id(+)
      AND ara.status                = 'APP'
      AND acr.org_id                = :p_org_id
      AND acr.receipt_date
          BETWEEN :p_start_date AND :p_end_date
    ORDER BY
      acr.receipt_date DESC,
      acr.receipt_number
  Example 11: AR AutoInvoice Interface Query
  exampleName: AR AutoInvoice Interface Errors
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Query AutoInvoice interface errors
    for troubleshooting failed invoice
    imports from feeder systems
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      ril.interface_line_id,
      ril.interface_line_context,
      ril.interface_line_attribute1    AS order_number,
      ril.interface_line_attribute2    AS line_number,
      ril.trx_date,
      ril.amount,
      ril.quantity,
      ril.unit_selling_price,
      ril.description,
      ril.currency_code,
      ril.orig_system_batch_name       AS batch_name,
      -- Customer info
      ril.orig_system_bill_customer_id,
      ril.orig_system_bill_address_id,
      -- Error information
      ril.interface_status,
      -- Get error messages
      (SELECT LISTAGG(
                rim.message_text, ' | ')
              WITHIN GROUP (
                ORDER BY rim.message_text)
       FROM   ra_interface_errors_all rim
       WHERE  rim.interface_id =
              ril.interface_line_id
       AND    rim.org_id = ril.org_id)
                                       AS error_messages,
      -- Source information
      ril.line_type,
      ril.sales_order,
      ril.sales_order_date,
      ril.sales_order_source,
      ril.memo_line_id,
      ril.inventory_item_id,
      ril.org_id,
      ril.creation_date,
      ril.last_update_date
    FROM
      ra_interface_lines_all     ril
    WHERE
      ril.interface_status  != 'P'
      AND ril.org_id            = :p_org_id
      AND ril.creation_date     >= TRUNC(SYSDATE) - 30
    ORDER BY
      ril.creation_date DESC
