SECTION Q — PROCURE TO PAY COMPLETE KNOWLEDGE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Topic 5: P2P End-to-End Process
knowledgeId: P2P-PROC-001
moduleCode: PO
topicName: Procure-to-Pay Complete Process
topicCategory: FUNCTIONAL
difficulty: ADVANCED
learningObjectives: [
  Understand complete P2P cycle,
  Create and process Purchase Requisitions,
  Convert PR to Purchase Order,
  Process PO approval workflow,
  Receive goods against PO,
  Match AP invoice to PO and Receipt,
  Process payment to supplier,
  Understand accounting entries at each step,
  Troubleshoot P2P exceptions,
  Query P2P status end to end
]
keyConceptsExplained: |
  P2P COMPLETE CYCLE:
  Step 1: PURCHASE REQUISITION (iProcurement/PO)
  ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  Who: Requester/Employee
  What: Request to purchase goods/services
  Key Fields:
  - Item Description
  - Quantity
  - Unit Price
  - Need-by Date
  - Deliver-to Location
  - Charge Account (GL)
  - Suggested Supplier (optional)
  Status Flow:
  INCOMPLETE → IN PROCESS → PRE-APPROVED
  → APPROVED → FINALLY CLOSED
  Key Tables:
  po_requisition_headers_all
  po_requisition_lines_all
  po_req_distributions_all
   Step 2: PURCHASE ORDER (continued)
  ────────────────────────────────────
  PO Types:
  - STANDARD: One-time purchase
  - BLANKET: Pre-negotiated terms,
             multiple releases
  - CONTRACT: Supplier agreement
             without items
  - PLANNED: Pre-authorized,
             multiple releases
  PO Status Flow:
  INCOMPLETE → IN PROCESS → PRE-APPROVED
  → APPROVED → CLOSED FOR RECEIVING
  → CLOSED FOR INVOICING → FINALLY CLOSED
  Key Fields:
  - PO Number (auto-generated)
  - Supplier and Site
  - Buyer
  - Currency
  - Payment Terms
  - Ship-To Location
  - Bill-To Location
  - Freight Terms
  - FOB Point
  PO Line Types:
  - Goods: Quantity-based
  - Services: Amount-based
  - Fixed Price Services: Lump sum
  - Rate-based: Time and materials
  Key Tables:
  po_headers_all
  po_lines_all
  po_line_locations_all (Shipments)
  po_distributions_all
  PO Accounting (Encumbrance):
  On PO Approval:
  DR: Encumbrance/Obligation Account
  CR: Budgetary Control Account
  (Only if encumbrance accounting enabled)
  Step 3: RECEIVING (PO Module)
  ────────────────────────────────────
  Who: Warehouse/Receiving Team
  What: Record physical receipt of goods
  Receipt Types:
  - STANDARD: Normal receipt
  - INSPECT: Requires inspection
  - DIRECT DELIVERY: Direct to requester
  - EXPENSE: Non-inventory items
  Receiving Actions:
  - RECEIVE: Record receipt
  - INSPECT: Approve/Reject quality
  - DELIVER: Move to final destination
  - RETURN TO VENDOR: Send back
  - CORRECT: Correct quantity
  Receiving Status:
  Expected → Received → Inspected
  → Delivered → Returned
  Key Tables:
  rcv_shipment_headers
  rcv_shipment_lines
  rcv_transactions
  rcv_receiving_sub_ledger
  Receiving Accounting:
  On Receipt:
  DR: Receiving Inspection Account
  CR: Accrual Account (AP Accrual)
  On Delivery:
  DR: Inventory/Expense Account
  CR: Receiving Inspection Account
  Step 4: AP INVOICE MATCHING
  ────────────────────────────────────
  Who: AP Team
  What: Match supplier invoice to
        PO and/or Receipt
  Matching Process:
  Enter Invoice Header
  → Click Match button
  → Search PO by number
  → Select PO lines/shipments
  → System validates:
    * Invoice Price vs PO Price
    * Invoice Qty vs PO Qty
    * Invoice Qty vs Receipt Qty
  → Apply tolerances
  → Create matched distributions
  Matching Tolerances:
  - Amount: ±10% or fixed amount
  - Quantity: ±5%
  - Price: ±0%
  Holds Placed if Exceeded:
  - QTY ORD: Qty exceeds PO qty
  - QTY REC: Qty exceeds receipt qty
  - PRICE: Price exceeds tolerance
  - AMOUNT: Amount exceeds tolerance
  Step 5: AP PAYMENT PROCESSING
  ────────────────────────────────────
  Who: AP Team/Treasury
  What: Issue payment to supplier
  Payment Process:
  Create Payment Batch
  → Select Invoices (by Pay Group/Org)
  → Format Payment (Check/EFT)
  → Confirm Payment Batch
  → Print Checks (if applicable)
  → Post Payment Batch
  → Bank Reconciliation
  Payment Accounting:
  On Payment:
  DR: Liability Account (AP Liability)
  CR: Cash/Bank Account
  On Clearing:
  DR: Cash Clearing Account
  CR: Bank Account (when cleared)
  Step 6: GL ACCOUNTING ENTRIES
  ────────────────────────────────────
  Complete P2P Accounting Flow:
  On PR Approval (if encumbrance):
  DR: Requisition Encumbrance
  CR: Budgetary Account
  On PO Approval (if encumbrance):
  DR: Obligation Encumbrance
  CR: Requisition Encumbrance
  On Receipt:
  DR: Receiving Inspection
  CR: AP Accrual Account
  On Invoice Validation:
  DR: Expense/Item Account
  CR: AP Liability Account
  On Invoice Matching:
  DR: AP Accrual Account
  CR: Receiving Inspection
  On Payment:
  DR: AP Liability Account
  CR: Cash Clearing Account
  On Bank Clearance:
  DR: Cash Clearing Account
  CR: Bank/Cash Account
sqlExamples: [
  Example 12: P2P Status Query
  exampleName: Complete P2P Transaction Status
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Query complete P2P status from
    requisition through payment
    for a given supplier or PO
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      -- Requisition details
      prh.segment1                     AS req_number,
      prh.creation_date                AS req_date,
      prh.authorization_status         AS req_status,
      prl.quantity                     AS req_quantity,
      prl.unit_price                   AS req_unit_price,
      (prl.quantity * prl.unit_price)  AS req_amount,
      -- PO details
      pha.segment1                     AS po_number,
      pha.creation_date                AS po_date,
      pha.authorization_status         AS po_status,
      pla.quantity                     AS po_quantity,
      pla.unit_price                   AS po_unit_price,
      plla.quantity_received           AS qty_received,
      plla.quantity_billed             AS qty_billed,
      -- Supplier details
      pv.vendor_name,
      pv.segment1                      AS vendor_num,
      pvs.vendor_site_code,
      -- Receipt details
      rsh.receipt_num,
      rsh.receipt_date,
      rt.quantity                      AS recv_quantity,
      rt.transaction_type              AS recv_type,
      -- Invoice details
      aia.invoice_num,
      aia.invoice_date,
      aia.invoice_amount,
      aia.approval_status              AS inv_approval_status,
      aia.payment_status_flag,
      -- Payment details
      aca.check_number,
      aca.check_date,
      aca.amount                       AS payment_amount,
      aca.status_lookup_code           AS payment_status,
      aca.cleared_date,
      -- GL Accounting
      gcc.concatenated_segments        AS charge_account,
      -- Operating unit
      haou.name                        AS operating_unit
    FROM
      po_requisition_headers_all prh,
      po_requisition_lines_all   prl,
      po_req_distributions_all   prd,
      po_headers_all             pha,
      po_lines_all               pla,
      po_line_locations_all      plla,
      po_distributions_all       pda,
      po_vendors                 pv,
      po_vendor_sites_all        pvs,
      rcv_shipment_headers       rsh,
      rcv_shipment_lines         rsl,
      rcv_transactions           rt,
      ap_invoices_all            aia,
      ap_invoice_distributions_all aida,
      ap_invoice_payments_all    aipa,
      ap_checks_all              aca,
            gl_code_combinations       gcc,
      hr_all_organization_units  haou
    WHERE
      -- Requisition to PO link
      prl.requisition_header_id = prh.requisition_header_id
      AND prd.requisition_line_id   = prl.requisition_line_id
      AND pda.req_distribution_id   = prd.distribution_id
      AND pda.po_header_id          = pha.po_header_id
      AND pda.po_line_id            = pla.po_line_id
      AND pda.line_location_id      = plla.line_location_id
      AND pda.po_distribution_id    = pda.po_distribution_id
      -- PO to Supplier
      AND pha.vendor_id             = pv.vendor_id
      AND pha.vendor_site_id        = pvs.vendor_site_id
      -- PO to Receipt
      AND rsl.po_header_id(+)       = pha.po_header_id
      AND rsl.po_line_id(+)         = pla.po_line_id
      AND rsh.shipment_header_id(+) = rsl.shipment_header_id
      AND rt.shipment_line_id(+)    = rsl.shipment_line_id
      AND rt.transaction_type(+)    = 'RECEIVE'
      -- PO to Invoice
      AND aida.po_distribution_id(+)= pda.po_distribution_id
      AND aia.invoice_id(+)         = aida.invoice_id
      -- Invoice to Payment
      AND aipa.invoice_id(+)        = aia.invoice_id
      AND aca.check_id(+)           = aipa.check_id
      -- GL Account
      AND gcc.code_combination_id   = pda.code_combination_id
      -- Operating Unit
      AND haou.organization_id      = pha.org_id
      AND pha.org_id                = :p_org_id
      AND pha.segment1              = NVL(:p_po_number,
                                         pha.segment1)
      AND pv.vendor_name            = NVL(:p_vendor_name,
                                         pv.vendor_name)
    ORDER BY
      prh.segment1,
      pha.segment1,
      aia.invoice_num
  Example 13: PO Accrual Reconciliation
  exampleName: PO Accrual Reconciliation Query
  difficultyLevel: EXPERT
  businessPurpose: |
    Identify PO accrual mismatches
    between receiving and AP invoicing
    for period end reconciliation
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      pha.segment1                     AS po_number,
      pla.line_num                     AS po_line,
      plla.shipment_num,
      pv.vendor_name,
      plla.quantity_received,
      plla.quantity_billed,
      (plla.quantity_received -
       plla.quantity_billed)           AS unbilled_qty,
      pla.unit_price,
      ((plla.quantity_received -
        plla.quantity_billed) *
       pla.unit_price)                 AS accrual_amount,
      -- Receipt accounting
      (SELECT SUM(rrs.amount)
       FROM   rcv_receiving_sub_ledger rrs,
              rcv_transactions rt
       WHERE  rt.shipment_line_id = plla.line_location_id
       AND    rrs.rcv_transaction_id = rt.transaction_id
       AND    rrs.accounting_event_id IS NOT NULL)
                                       AS recv_accrual_amount,
      -- AP Invoice amount
      (SELECT SUM(aida.amount)
       FROM   ap_invoice_distributions_all aida
       WHERE  aida.po_distribution_id IN (
                SELECT po_distribution_id
                FROM   po_distributions_all
                WHERE  line_location_id =
                       plla.line_location_id))
                                       AS invoiced_amount,
      -- Variance
      ((plla.quantity_received *
        pla.unit_price) -
       NVL((SELECT SUM(aida.amount)
            FROM   ap_invoice_distributions_all aida
            WHERE  aida.po_distribution_id IN (
                     SELECT po_distribution_id
                     FROM   po_distributions_all
                     WHERE  line_location_id =
                            plla.line_location_id)),0))
                                       AS accrual_variance,
      gcc.concatenated_segments        AS accrual_account,
      plla.closed_code                 AS shipment_status,
      pha.org_id
    FROM
      po_headers_all             pha,
      po_lines_all               pla,
      po_line_locations_all      plla,
      po_distributions_all       pda,
      po_vendors                 pv,
      gl_code_combinations       gcc
    WHERE
      pha.po_header_id          = pla.po_header_id
      AND pla.po_line_id            = plla.po_line_id
      AND plla.line_location_id     = pda.line_location_id
      AND pha.vendor_id             = pv.vendor_id
      AND pda.accrual_account_id    = gcc.code_combination_id
      AND plla.quantity_received    > plla.quantity_billed
      AND plla.closed_code          NOT IN (
            'FINALLY CLOSED',
            'CLOSED FOR INVOICING')
      AND pha.org_id                = :p_org_id
    ORDER BY
      ((plla.quantity_received -
        plla.quantity_billed) *
       pla.unit_price) DESC
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION R — ORDER TO CASH COMPLETE KNOWLEDGE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Topic 6: O2C End-to-End Process
knowledgeId: O2C-PROC-001
moduleCode: OM
topicName: Order-to-Cash Complete Process
topicCategory: FUNCTIONAL
difficulty: ADVANCED
learningObjectives: [
  Understand complete O2C cycle,
  Create and process Sales Orders,
  Manage order booking and scheduling,
  Process Pick Release and Shipping,
  Generate AR Invoice via AutoInvoice,
  Apply customer receipts,
  Understand accounting at each step,
  Handle order exceptions and holds,
  Query O2C status end to end,
  Understand OM and AR integration
]
keyConceptsExplained: |
  O2C COMPLETE CYCLE:
  Step 1: SALES ORDER CREATION (OM)
  ─────────────────────────────────
  Who: Order Entry Clerk/Customer
  What: Customer purchase order entry
  Order Types:
  - Standard Orders: Normal sales
  - Return Orders: Customer returns (RMA)
  - Internal Orders: Inter-org transfers
  - Drop Shipment: Ship direct from supplier
  - Back-to-Back: Trigger PO on order
  Order Header Fields:
  - Order Number (auto-generated)
  - Order Type
  - Customer Name and Bill-To
  - Ship-To Address
  - Order Date
  - Request Date
  - Payment Terms
  - Price List
  - Salesperson
  - Currency
  Order Line Fields:
  - Item Number
  - Ordered Quantity
  - Unit of Measure
  - Unit Selling Price
  - Schedule Ship Date
  - Ship-From Warehouse
  - Line Type
  Status Flow:
  ENTERED → BOOKED → AWAITING SHIPPING
  → PICKED → SHIPPED → CLOSED
    Key Tables:
  oe_order_headers_all
  oe_order_lines_all
  oe_price_adjustments
  oe_order_holds_all
  oe_hold_sources_all
  Step 2: ORDER BOOKING (OM)
  ─────────────────────────────────
  Who: Order Entry Clerk
  What: Confirm order is complete
        and ready for fulfillment
  Booking Checks:
  - Price list assigned
  - Payment terms defined
  - Ship-to address valid
  - Items available
  - Credit check passed
  - All required fields complete
  On Booking:
  - Order status: BOOKED
  - Lines become eligible for scheduling
  - Demand placed on inventory
  - Credit reservation made
  Credit Check Types:
  - NONE: No credit check
  - UNLIMITED: Always passes
  - CREDIT CHECK: Check credit limit
  - COLLATERAL: Against collateral
  Step 3: ORDER SCHEDULING (OM/MRP)
  ─────────────────────────────────
  Who: System/Planner
  What: Determine fulfillment dates
  Scheduling Actions:
  - Schedule: Set ship/arrival dates
  - Reserve: Reserve inventory
  - AutoSchedule: Automatic scheduling
  ATP (Available to Promise):
  - Checks inventory availability
  - Considers supply/demand
  - Returns earliest ship date
  Step 4: PICK RELEASE (OM/WMS)
  ─────────────────────────────────
  Who: Warehouse Manager
  What: Release order lines for picking
  Pick Release Criteria:
  - Departure Date
  - Ship-From Organization
  - Scheduled Ship Date
  - Customer
  - Order Number
  - Item
  Pick Release Process:
  Release Sales Orders
  → Move Orders Created
  → Pick Slips Generated
  → Pickers Assigned
  → Items Picked
  → Pick Confirmed
  Key Tables:
  wsh_delivery_details
  wsh_delivery_assignments
  wsh_new_deliveries
  mtl_material_transactions
  Step 5: SHIPPING (OM/WSH)
  ─────────────────────────────────
  Who: Shipping Clerk
  What: Ship goods to customer
  Shipping Process:
  Pack Items
  → Ship Confirm
  → Delivery Closed
  → Interface to OM
  → OM Line Status: SHIPPED
  Ship Confirm Actions:
  - Actual Departure Date
  - Waybill Number
  - Freight Carrier
  - Weight and Volume
  Inventory Accounting on Ship:
  DR: Cost of Goods Sold (COGS)
  CR: Inventory Valuation Account
  Step 6: AUTOINVOICE (AR)
  ─────────────────────────────────
  Who: System (Automatic)
  What: Generate AR Invoice from
        shipped order
  AutoInvoice Flow:
  OM Ships Order
  → Workflow: OM-AR Interface
  → ra_interface_lines_all populated
  → AutoInvoice Import Program runs
  → ra_customer_trx_all created
  → Invoice sent to customer
  Revenue Recognition:
  DR: Accounts Receivable
  CR: Revenue Account
  Step 7: RECEIPT APPLICATION (AR)
  ─────────────────────────────────
  Who: AR Team
  What: Apply customer payment
        to invoice
  Receipt Methods:
  - Manual: Enter check/wire details
  - Lockbox: Automated bank file
  - AutoReceipt: Automatic creation
  Application Process:
  Receive Payment
  → Create Receipt
  → Apply to Invoice
  → Close Invoice
  Cash Application Accounting:
  DR: Bank/Cash Account
  CR: Accounts Receivable
  Step 8: PERIOD CLOSE (GL)
  ─────────────────────────────────
  Who: Finance Team
  What: Transfer all O2C accounting
        to General Ledger
  Transfer Programs:
  - OM: No direct transfer (via AR)
  - AR: Receivables Transfer to GL
  - GL: Journal Import and Posting
  O2C Complete Accounting Summary:
  ─────────────────────────────────
  On Shipment:
  DR: COGS Account
  CR: Inventory Valuation
  On Invoice:
  DR: Accounts Receivable
  CR: Revenue Account
  CR: Tax Liability (if applicable)
  On Receipt:
  DR: Cash/Bank Account
  CR: Accounts Receivable
  On Discount:
  DR: Discount Expense
  CR: Accounts Receivable
sqlExamples: [
  Example 14: O2C Status Query
  exampleName: Complete O2C Transaction Status
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Query complete O2C status from
    sales order through cash receipt
    for a given customer or order
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      -- Sales Order details
      ooh.order_number,
      ooh.ordered_date,
      ooh.flow_status_code             AS order_status,
      ooh.order_type_id,
      ott.name                         AS order_type,
      -- Customer details
      hp.party_name                    AS customer_name,
      hca.account_number               AS customer_number,
      -- Order Line details
      ool.line_number,
      ool.ordered_item,
      ool.ordered_quantity,
      ool.shipped_quantity,
      ool.invoiced_quantity,
      ool.unit_selling_price,
      (ool.ordered_quantity *
       ool.unit_selling_price)         AS line_amount,
      ool.flow_status_code             AS line_status,
      ool.schedule_ship_date,
      ool.actual_shipment_date,
      -- Delivery details
      wnd.name                         AS delivery_name,
      wnd.status_code                  AS delivery_status,
      wdd.tracking_number,
      wdd.weight,
      -- Invoice details
      rct.trx_number                   AS invoice_number,
      rct.trx_date                     AS invoice_date,
      rct.amount_due_original          AS invoice_amount,
      rct.amount_due_remaining         AS balance_due,
      rct.status_trx                   AS invoice_status,
      -- Receipt details
      acr.receipt_number,
      acr.receipt_date,
      acr.amount                       AS receipt_amount,
      acr.status                       AS receipt_status,
      -- AR Application
      ara.apply_date,
      ara.amount_applied,
      ara.discount_taken,
      -- GL Account
      gcc.concatenated_segments        AS revenue_account,
      -- Operating unit
      haou.name                        AS operating_unit
    FROM
      oe_order_headers_all       ooh,
      oe_order_lines_all         ool,
      oe_transaction_types_tl    ott,
      hz_cust_accounts           hca,
      hz_parties                 hp,
      wsh_delivery_details       wdd,
      wsh_delivery_assignments   wda,
      wsh_new_deliveries         wnd,
      ra_customer_trx_all        rct,
      ra_customer_trx_lines_all  rctl,
      ar_cash_receipts_all       acr,
      ar_receivable_applications_all ara,
      gl_code_combinations       gcc,
      hr_all_organization_units  haou
    WHERE
      ooh.header_id             = ool.header_id
      AND ooh.order_type_id         = ott.transaction_type_id
      AND ott.language              = USERENV('LANG')
      AND ooh.sold_to_org_id        = hca.cust_account_id
            AND hca.party_id              = hp.party_id
      -- Order to Delivery
      AND wdd.source_header_id(+)   = ooh.header_id
      AND wdd.source_line_id(+)     = ool.line_id
      AND wda.delivery_detail_id(+) = wdd.delivery_detail_id
      AND wnd.delivery_id(+)        = wda.delivery_id
      -- Order to Invoice (via AutoInvoice reference)
      AND rctl.interface_line_attribute1(+)
                                    = TO_CHAR(ooh.order_number)
      AND rctl.interface_line_context(+)
                                    = 'ORDER ENTRY'
      AND rct.customer_trx_id(+)    = rctl.customer_trx_id
      -- Invoice to Receipt
      AND ara.applied_customer_trx_id(+)
                                    = rct.customer_trx_id
      AND ara.status(+)             = 'APP'
      AND acr.cash_receipt_id(+)    = ara.cash_receipt_id
      -- GL Account from line
      AND gcc.code_combination_id(+)
                                    = rctl.revenue_ccid
      -- Operating unit
      AND haou.organization_id      = ooh.org_id
      AND ooh.org_id                = :p_org_id
      AND ooh.order_number          = NVL(
                                        :p_order_number,
                                        ooh.order_number)
      AND hp.party_name             = NVL(
                                        :p_customer_name,
                                        hp.party_name)
    ORDER BY
      ooh.order_number,
      ool.line_number
  Example 15: OM Order Holds Query
  exampleName: Sales Order Holds Analysis
  difficultyLevel: INTERMEDIATE
  businessPurpose: |
    Query all active order holds
    with hold reason and
    resolution details
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      ooh.order_number,
      ooh.ordered_date,
      ooh.flow_status_code             AS order_status,
      hp.party_name                    AS customer_name,
      hca.account_number               AS customer_number,
      -- Hold details
      oha.hold_source_id,
      ohd.name                         AS hold_name,
      ohd.description                  AS hold_description,
      ohd.type_code                    AS hold_type,
      oha.hold_date,
      oha.hold_comment,
      oha.released_flag,
      oha.release_date,
      oha.release_reason_code,
      oha.release_comment,
      -- Hold applied by
      fu_apply.user_name               AS applied_by,
      -- Released by
      fu_release.user_name             AS released_by,
      -- Order amount
      (SELECT SUM(ool2.ordered_quantity *
                  ool2.unit_selling_price)
       FROM   oe_order_lines_all ool2
       WHERE  ool2.header_id = ooh.header_id
       AND    ool2.cancelled_flag = 'N')
                                       AS order_value,
      -- Days on hold
      TRUNC(NVL(oha.release_date,
                SYSDATE)) -
      TRUNC(oha.hold_date)             AS days_on_hold,
      haou.name                        AS operating_unit
    FROM
      oe_order_headers_all       ooh,
      oe_order_holds_all         oha,
      oe_hold_sources_all        ohs,
      oe_hold_definitions        ohd,
      hz_cust_accounts           hca,
      hz_parties                 hp,
      fnd_user                   fu_apply,
      fnd_user                   fu_release,
      hr_all_organization_units  haou
    WHERE
      ooh.header_id             = oha.header_id
      AND oha.hold_source_id        = ohs.hold_source_id
      AND ohs.hold_id               = ohd.hold_id
      AND ooh.sold_to_org_id        = hca.cust_account_id
      AND hca.party_id              = hp.party_id
      AND oha.created_by            = fu_apply.user_id
      AND oha.last_updated_by       = fu_release.user_id(+)
      AND haou.organization_id      = ooh.org_id
      AND oha.released_flag         = NVL(
                                        :p_released_flag,
                                        oha.released_flag)
      AND ooh.org_id                = :p_org_id
    ORDER BY
      oha.hold_date DESC,
      ooh.order_number
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION S — HRMS COMPLETE KNOWLEDGE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Topic 7: HRMS Complete Functional and Technical
knowledgeId: HRMS-FUNC-001
moduleCode: HRMS
topicName: HRMS Complete Setup and Processing
topicCategory: FUNCTIONAL
difficulty: ADVANCED
learningObjectives: [
  Understand HRMS enterprise structure,
  Configure Business Groups,
  Set up Organizations and Locations,
  Configure Jobs and Positions,
  Set up Grade and Pay structures,
  Process employee hiring,
  Manage assignments and transfers,
  Process terminations,
  Understand DateTrack mechanism,
  Query HRMS data correctly with DateTrack
]
keyConceptsExplained: |
  1. HRMS ENTERPRISE STRUCTURE
  ─────────────────────────────
  Business Group (Top Level)
  └── Legal Entity
      └── Operating Unit
          └── HR Organization
              └── Department
                  └── Location
  Key Configuration:
  - Business Group: Top-level HR entity
  - GRE/Legal Entity: Tax reporting entity
  - HR Organization: Department/Division
  - Location: Physical work location
  - Payroll: Pay group definition
  2. DATETRACK MECHANISM
  ─────────────────────────────
  CRITICAL CONCEPT:
  Most HRMS tables are DateTracked.
  This means ALL changes are preserved
  as history - no data is overwritten.
  DateTrack Columns:
  effective_start_date
  effective_end_date
  DateTrack Modes:
  - CORRECTION: Fix current record
  - UPDATE: Insert new record from today
  - UPDATE_OVERRIDE: Override future changes
  - UPDATE_CHANGE_INSERT: Complex history
  CRITICAL SQL RULE:
  ALWAYS include date filter:
  WHERE SYSDATE BETWEEN
        effective_start_date
        AND effective_end_date
  OR for specific date:
  WHERE :p_effective_date BETWEEN
        effective_start_date
        AND effective_end_date
  WITHOUT this filter:
  - Multiple rows returned per employee
  - Incorrect results
  - Performance issues
  3. EMPLOYEE MASTER DATA
  ─────────────────────────────
  Key Tables:
  per_all_people_f          (DateTracked)
  per_all_assignments_f     (DateTracked)
  per_phones                (DateTracked)
  per_addresses             (DateTracked)
  per_periods_of_service    (Not DateTracked)
  per_person_types          (Reference)
  Person Types:
  - Employee (EMP)
  - Applicant (APL)
  - Contact (CON)
  - External (EXT)
  - Contingent Worker (CWK)
    4. ASSIGNMENT STRUCTURE (continued)
  ─────────────────────────────────
  Assignment contains:
  - Organization (Department)
  - Job
  - Position (optional)
  - Grade
  - Location
  - Payroll
  - Employment Category:
    FULL_TIME|PART_TIME|
    TEMP|CONTRACT
  - Pay Basis:
    ANNUAL|MONTHLY|HOURLY|PERIOD
  - Assignment Status:
    ACTIVE_ASSIGN|SUSPEND_ASSIGN|
    TERM_ASSIGN|END
  Assignment Number:
  - Unique identifier per assignment
  - Employee can have multiple assignments
  - One PRIMARY assignment per employee
  - PRIMARY_FLAG = 'Y' for primary
  5. JOB AND POSITION STRUCTURE
  ─────────────────────────────
  Job:
  - Generic role (e.g., Manager, Analyst)
  - Shared across departments
  - Has Job Group
  - Has Valid Grades
  Position:
  - Specific instance of Job
  - Belongs to specific Organization
  - Has headcount
  - Has FTE (Full Time Equivalent)
  - Position Hierarchy possible
  Grade:
  - Pay range definition
  - Grade Steps possible
  - Grade Rate: Salary ranges
  - Grade Scale: Step-based pay
  6. HIRE PROCESS
  ─────────────────────────────
  New Hire Steps:
  Step 1: Create Person Record
    Navigation: People > Enter and Maintain
    Enter: Name, DOB, Gender, NI/SSN
  Step 2: Add Employment Information
    - Start Date (Hire Date)
    - Person Type: Employee
    - Legal Employer
  Step 3: Create Assignment
    - Organization
    - Job/Position
    - Grade
    - Location
    - Payroll
    - Pay Basis
  Step 4: Enter Pay
    - Salary Administration
    - Salary Basis
    - Pay Amount
    - Effective Date
  Step 5: Add Personal Details
    - Address
    - Phone Numbers
    - Emergency Contacts
    - Bank Account (for payroll)
  Step 6: Benefits Enrollment
    - Benefits Group
    - Plan Selection
    - Dependent Information
  API: hr_employee_api.create_employee
  7. TRANSFER PROCESS
  ─────────────────────────────
  Transfer Steps:
  Step 1: Open Assignment
    - Navigate to Assignment window
    - Enter effective date of transfer
  Step 2: Change Assignment Details
    DateTrack Mode: UPDATE
    - New Organization
    - New Job/Position (if changed)
    - New Location
    - New Grade (if changed)
    - New Supervisor
  Step 3: Verify Cost Allocation
    - Update cost center in assignment
    - Update GL account segments
  API: hr_assignment_api.update_emp_asg
  8. TERMINATION PROCESS
  ─────────────────────────────
  Termination Steps:
  Step 1: Navigate to
    People > Enter and Maintain
    Terminate button
  Step 2: Enter Termination Details
    - Termination Date
    - Termination Reason:
      RESIGNATION|RETIREMENT|
      REDUNDANCY|DISMISSAL|
      END_OF_CONTRACT|DECEASED
    - Final Process Date
    - Last Standard Process Date
    - Notified Termination Date
  Step 3: Process Final Pay
    - Final payroll run
    - Outstanding leave payout
    - Final expense processing
  Step 4: Update Benefits
    - Terminate benefit enrollments
    - COBRA/continuation if applicable
  Step 5: System Updates
    - Assignment status: TERM_ASSIGN
    - Period of service: actual_termination_date
  API: hr_ex_employee_api.actual_termination_emp
sqlExamples: [
  Example 16: HRMS Employee Query
  exampleName: Complete Employee Details with DateTrack
  difficultyLevel: INTERMEDIATE
  businessPurpose: |
    Query complete employee details
    including assignment, job, grade,
    location and supervisor using
    correct DateTrack filter
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      papf.employee_number,
      papf.full_name,
      papf.first_name,
      papf.last_name,
      papf.middle_names,
      papf.date_of_birth,
      papf.sex                         AS gender,
      papf.marital_status,
      papf.national_identifier         AS ssn_ni,
      papf.email_address,
      papf.effective_start_date        AS person_start_date,
      papf.effective_end_date          AS person_end_date,
      -- Assignment details
      paaf.assignment_number,
      paaf.assignment_sequence,
      paaf.primary_flag,
      paaf.assignment_status_type_id,
      past.user_status                 AS assignment_status,
      paaf.employment_category,
      paaf.effective_start_date        AS asg_start_date,
      paaf.effective_end_date          AS asg_end_date,
      -- Organization
      haou.name                        AS department,
      haou.organization_id,
      -- Job
      pj.name                          AS job_name,
      -- Position
      pap.name                         AS position_name,
      -- Grade
      pg.name                          AS grade_name,
      -- Location
      hl.location_code,
      hl.description                   AS location_name,
      hl.town_or_city                  AS city,
      hl.country,
      -- Supervisor
      papf_sup.full_name               AS supervisor_name,
      papf_sup.employee_number         AS supervisor_emp_num,
      -- Payroll
      ppf.payroll_name,
      -- Business group
      pbg.name                         AS business_group,
      -- Period of service
      ppos.date_start                  AS hire_date,
      ppos.actual_termination_date,
      ppos.leaving_reason,
      -- Service length
      ROUND(
        MONTHS_BETWEEN(
          NVL(ppos.actual_termination_date, SYSDATE),
          ppos.date_start
        ) / 12, 2)                     AS years_of_service
    FROM
      per_all_people_f           papf,
      per_all_assignments_f      paaf,
      per_assignment_status_types past,
      hr_all_organization_units  haou,
      per_jobs                   pj,
      hr_all_positions_f         pap,
      per_grades                 pg,
      hr_locations_all           hl,
      per_all_people_f           papf_sup,
      pay_payrolls_f             ppf,
      per_business_groups        pbg,
      per_periods_of_service     ppos
    WHERE
      -- DateTrack filter - CRITICAL
      SYSDATE BETWEEN papf.effective_start_date
              AND papf.effective_end_date
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN paaf.effective_start_date
              AND paaf.effective_end_date
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN pap.effective_start_date(+)
              AND pap.effective_end_date(+)
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN ppf.effective_start_date(+)
              AND ppf.effective_end_date(+)
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN papf_sup.effective_start_date(+)
              AND papf_sup.effective_end_date(+)
      -- Joins
      AND paaf.person_id            = papf.person_id
      AND paaf.assignment_status_type_id
                                    = past.assignment_status_type_id
      AND paaf.organization_id      = haou.organization_id
            AND paaf.job_id               = pj.job_id(+)
      AND paaf.position_id          = pap.position_id(+)
      AND paaf.grade_id             = pg.grade_id(+)
      AND paaf.location_id          = hl.location_id(+)
      AND paaf.supervisor_id        = papf_sup.person_id(+)
      AND paaf.payroll_id           = ppf.payroll_id(+)
      AND papf.business_group_id    = pbg.business_group_id
      AND ppos.person_id            = papf.person_id
      AND ppos.date_start           = (
            SELECT MAX(ppos2.date_start)
            FROM   per_periods_of_service ppos2
            WHERE  ppos2.person_id = papf.person_id)
      -- Primary assignment only
      AND paaf.primary_flag         = 'Y'
      -- Active employees only
      AND past.per_system_status    = 'ACTIVE_ASSIGN'
      -- Business group filter
      AND papf.business_group_id    = :p_bg_id
      -- Optional filters
      AND haou.name                 = NVL(
                                        :p_department,
                                        haou.name)
      AND papf.employee_number      = NVL(
                                        :p_emp_number,
                                        papf.employee_number)
    ORDER BY
      haou.name,
      papf.last_name,
      papf.first_name
  Example 17: HRMS Headcount Query
  exampleName: Department Headcount Analysis
  difficultyLevel: INTERMEDIATE
  businessPurpose: |
    Query headcount by department
    with gender split, employment
    category and average service
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      haou.name                        AS department,
      COUNT(*)                         AS total_headcount,
      -- Gender split
      SUM(CASE WHEN papf.sex = 'M'
               THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)      AS male_count,
      SUM(CASE WHEN papf.sex = 'F'
               THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)      AS female_count,
      -- Employment category
      SUM(CASE WHEN paaf.employment_category
               = 'FR' THEN 1 ELSE 0
          END)                         AS full_time,
      SUM(CASE WHEN paaf.employment_category
               = 'PR' THEN 1 ELSE 0
          END)                         AS part_time,
      -- Average service years
      ROUND(AVG(
        MONTHS_BETWEEN(
          SYSDATE,
          ppos.date_start
        ) / 12), 2)                    AS avg_service_years,
      -- New hires this year
      SUM(CASE WHEN ppos.date_start
               >= TRUNC(SYSDATE,'YYYY')
               THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)      AS new_hires_ytd,
      -- Terminations this year
      SUM(CASE WHEN ppos.actual_termination_date
               >= TRUNC(SYSDATE,'YYYY')
               AND ppos.actual_termination_date
               <= SYSDATE
               THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)      AS terminations_ytd,
      -- Average age
      ROUND(AVG(
        MONTHS_BETWEEN(
          SYSDATE,
          papf.date_of_birth
        ) / 12), 1)                    AS avg_age
    FROM
      per_all_people_f           papf,
      per_all_assignments_f      paaf,
      per_assignment_status_types past,
      hr_all_organization_units  haou,
      per_periods_of_service     ppos
    WHERE
      SYSDATE BETWEEN papf.effective_start_date
              AND papf.effective_end_date
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN paaf.effective_start_date
              AND paaf.effective_end_date
      AND paaf.person_id            = papf.person_id
      AND paaf.assignment_status_type_id
                                    = past.assignment_status_type_id
      AND paaf.organization_id      = haou.organization_id
      AND ppos.person_id            = papf.person_id
      AND ppos.date_start           = (
            SELECT MAX(ppos2.date_start)
            FROM   per_periods_of_service ppos2
            WHERE  ppos2.person_id = papf.person_id)
      AND paaf.primary_flag         = 'Y'
      AND past.per_system_status    = 'ACTIVE_ASSIGN'
      AND papf.business_group_id    = :p_bg_id
      AND ppos.actual_termination_date IS NULL
    GROUP BY
      haou.name
    ORDER BY
      COUNT(*) DESC
  Example 18: HRMS Supervisor Hierarchy Query
  exampleName: Employee Supervisor Hierarchy
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Query complete supervisor hierarchy
    using hierarchical SQL connect by
    for org chart visualization
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      LEVEL                            AS hierarchy_level,
      LPAD(' ', (LEVEL-1)*4) ||
      papf.full_name                   AS employee_name,
      papf.employee_number,
      haou.name                        AS department,
      pj.name                          AS job_title,
      papf_sup.full_name               AS supervisor_name,
      papf_sup.employee_number         AS supervisor_emp_num,
      -- Hierarchy path
      SYS_CONNECT_BY_PATH(
        papf.employee_number, '/'
      )                                AS hierarchy_path,
      -- Direct reports count
      (SELECT COUNT(*)
       FROM   per_all_assignments_f paaf2,
              per_all_people_f papf2,
              per_assignment_status_types past2
       WHERE  paaf2.supervisor_id = papf.person_id
       AND    papf2.person_id = paaf2.person_id
       AND    paaf2.assignment_status_type_id
                                    = past2.assignment_status_type_id
       AND    past2.per_system_status = 'ACTIVE_ASSIGN'
       AND    paaf2.primary_flag = 'Y'
       AND    SYSDATE BETWEEN paaf2.effective_start_date
              AND paaf2.effective_end_date
       AND    SYSDATE BETWEEN papf2.effective_start_date
              AND papf2.effective_end_date)
                                       AS direct_reports_count
    FROM
      per_all_people_f           papf,
      per_all_assignments_f      paaf,
      per_assignment_status_types past,
      hr_all_organization_units  haou,
      per_jobs                   pj,
      per_all_people_f           papf_sup
    WHERE
      SYSDATE BETWEEN papf.effective_start_date
              AND papf.effective_end_date
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN paaf.effective_start_date
              AND paaf.effective_end_date
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN papf_sup.effective_start_date(+)
              AND papf_sup.effective_end_date(+)
      AND paaf.person_id            = papf.person_id
      AND paaf.assignment_status_type_id
                                    = past.assignment_status_type_id
      AND paaf.organization_id      = haou.organization_id
            AND paaf.job_id               = pj.job_id(+)
      AND paaf.supervisor_id        = papf_sup.person_id(+)
      AND paaf.primary_flag         = 'Y'
      AND past.per_system_status    = 'ACTIVE_ASSIGN'
      AND papf.business_group_id    = :p_bg_id
    CONNECT BY PRIOR
      papf.person_id                = paaf.supervisor_id
    START WITH
      paaf.supervisor_id            IS NULL
      OR papf.employee_number       = :p_start_emp_number
    ORDER SIBLINGS BY
      papf.last_name,
      papf.first_name

SECTION T — PAYROLL COMPLETE KNOWLEDGE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Topic 8: Payroll Complete Functional and Technical
knowledgeId: PAY-FUNC-001
moduleCode: PAY
topicName: Oracle Payroll Complete Setup and Processing
topicCategory: FUNCTIONAL
difficulty: EXPERT
learningObjectives: [
  Understand Payroll structure and setup,
  Configure payroll elements and classifications,
  Set up element links and entry values,
  Understand payroll processing cycle,
  Configure tax setup and withholding,
  Process payroll runs and retro-pays,
  Understand costing and GL accounting,
  Query payroll results and balances,
  Troubleshoot payroll processing errors,
  Understand fast formulas and their usage
]
keyConceptsExplained: |
  1. PAYROLL STRUCTURE
  ─────────────────────────────
  Payroll Definition:
  - Payroll Name
  - Period Type:
    WEEKLY|BIWEEKLY|SEMI_MONTHLY|
    MONTHLY|QUARTERLY|ANNUALLY
  - First Period End Date
  - Number of Years
  - Cut-off Date Offset
  - Pay Date Offset
  - Direct Deposit Cut-off
  Payroll Calendar:
  - Auto-generated periods
  - Period dates drive processing
  - Cannot overlap periods
  2. ELEMENT STRUCTURE
  ─────────────────────────────
  Element Classification:
  - Earnings:
    REGULAR_EARNINGS|SUPPLEMENTAL_EARNINGS|
    IMPUTED_EARNINGS|PRE_TAX_DEDUCTIONS
  - Deductions:
    INVOLUNTARY_DEDUCTIONS|
    VOLUNTARY_DEDUCTIONS|
    EMPLOYER_CHARGES
  - Information Elements:
    INFORMATION|EMPLOYER_TAXES
  Element Processing Types:
  - RECURRING: Process every period
  - NONRECURRING: Process once
  Element Input Values:
  - Pay Value: Always present
  - Hours: For hourly elements
  - Rate: Hourly rate
  - Multiple: For adjustments
  - From Date/To Date: For retro
  Element Links:
  - Standard: All eligible employees
  - Non-standard: Specific criteria
  Link Criteria:
  - Payroll
  - Employment Category
  - Grade
  - Organization
  - Job
  - Location
  Element Entries:
  - Created from links (standard)
  - Manual entry (non-standard)
  - BatchElement API for bulk
  3. FAST FORMULAS
  ─────────────────────────────
  Purpose:
  - Custom payroll calculations
  - Skip rules
  - Validation rules
  - Assignment attributes
  Formula Types:
  - Oracle Payroll: Core calculations
  - QuickPay: On-demand formulas
  - Accrual: Leave accrual
  - Proration: Partial period calc
  - Absence: Leave management
  Sample Fast Formula:
  /* XXCUST_BONUS_FORMULA */
  DEFAULT FOR SALARY_ANNUAL_SALARY IS 0
  DEFAULT FOR ASG_EMPLOYMENT_CATEGORY IS 'FR'
  l_annual_salary = SALARY_ANNUAL_SALARY
  l_bonus_pct = 0
  IF ASG_EMPLOYMENT_CATEGORY = 'FR' THEN
    IF l_annual_salary >= 100000 THEN
      l_bonus_pct = 15
    ELSE IF l_annual_salary >= 50000 THEN
      l_bonus_pct = 10
    ELSE
      l_bonus_pct = 5
    END IF
  ELSE
    l_bonus_pct = 3
  END IF
  l_bonus_amount = l_annual_salary * l_bonus_pct / 100
  RETURN l_bonus_amount
  4. PAYROLL PROCESSING CYCLE
  ─────────────────────────────
  Monthly Payroll Process:
  Step 1: PRE-PAYROLL ACTIVITIES
  - Verify employee master data
  - Enter/update element entries
  - Process timecards (if applicable)
  - Enter bonuses and adjustments
  - Verify tax information
  - Run HR/Payroll audit reports
  Step 2: PAYROLL RUN
  Process: Run Payroll
  - Processes all elements
  - Calculates taxes
  - Applies deductions
  - Creates payroll action
  Status: MARK FOR RETRY → 
          PROCESSING → 
          COMPLETE
  Step 3: PRE-PAYMENT VERIFICATION
  - Run Gross to Net Summary
  - Verify tax calculations
  - Check for exceptions
  - Run Payroll Register
  - Verify deductions
  - Balance verification
  Step 4: PRE-PAYMENTS
  Process: Pre-Payments
  - Assigns payment methods
  - Creates EFT/check batches
  - Allocates to bank accounts
  Step 5: DIRECT DEPOSIT / CHECKS
  - EFT: Generate NACHA file
  - Checks: Generate check file
  - Wire: Process wire transfers
  Step 6: COSTING
  Process: Costing
  - Calculates GL distributions
  - Uses costing setup
  - Creates costing results
  Costing Types:
  STANDARD: Fixed account
  DISTRIBUTED: Spread across accounts
  DYNAMIC_DISTRIBUTED: Formula-based
  MANUAL: Manually specified
  Step 7: TRANSFER TO GL
  Process: Transfer to GL
  - Creates journal entries
  - Posts to GL interface
  - GL Journal Import runs
  Payroll GL Accounting:
  On Payroll Run:
  DR: Salary Expense Account
  DR: Benefits Expense Account
  CR: Payroll Liability Account
  On Payment:
  DR: Payroll Liability Account
  CR: Bank/Cash Account
  On Employer Tax:
  DR: Employer Tax Expense
  CR: Tax Liability Account
  Step 8: POST-PAYROLL
  - Payslip generation
  - Year-end reporting
  - W2/P60 processing
  - Audit trail reports
  5. RETROACTIVE PROCESSING
  ─────────────────────────────
  Retro Pay Types:
  - RetroCosting: GL restatement
  - RetroPay: Employee payment adjustment
  Retro Scenarios:
  - Salary change backdated
  - Grade change backdated
  - Missed payment correction
  - Benefit change backdated
  Retro Process:
  Identify Retro Changes
  → Create Retro Notifications
  → Process RetroPay Run
  → Verify Retro Results
  → Process Normal Payroll
  → Retro included in paycheck
    6. BALANCE ARCHITECTURE (continued)
  ─────────────────────────────────
  Balance Dimensions (continued):
  - _PAYMENTS: Payment run balance
  - _PER_RUN: Current run, person
  - _PER_YTD: Year-to-date, person
  - _GRE_RUN: Current run, GRE
  - _GRE_YTD: Year-to-date, GRE
  - _REP_ITD: Inception-to-date, report
  Balance Feeds:
  - Elements feed into balances
  - Add/Subtract based on classification
  - Multiple elements can feed one balance
  Key Balance Names:
  - GROSS_EARNINGS: Total gross pay
  - NET_ASG_RUN: Net payment this run
  - REGULAR_EARNINGS: Base salary
  - OVERTIME_EARNINGS: Overtime pay
  - FEDERAL_INCOME_TAX: FIT withheld
  - STATE_INCOME_TAX: SIT withheld
  - SOCIAL_SECURITY_EE: SS employee
  - SOCIAL_SECURITY_ER: SS employer
  - MEDICARE_EE: Medicare employee
  - MEDICARE_ER: Medicare employer
  - VOLUNTARY_DEDUCTIONS: Total vol deductions
  - INVOLUNTARY_DEDUCTIONS: Total involuntary
sqlExamples: [
  Example 19: Payroll Results Query
  exampleName: Payroll Run Results with Element Details
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Query complete payroll run results
    showing all element results for
    each employee in a payroll period
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      ppf.payroll_name,
      ppa.date_earned,
      ppa.effective_date               AS pay_date,
      ppa.action_type,
      ppa.action_status,
      papf.employee_number,
      papf.full_name,
      haou.name                        AS department,
      -- Element details
      pet.element_name,
      pec.classification_name,
      -- Input values
      (SELECT pivf.name
       FROM   pay_input_values_f pivf
       WHERE  pivf.input_value_id =
              prv.input_value_id
       AND    ppa.effective_date
              BETWEEN pivf.effective_start_date
              AND pivf.effective_end_date)
                                       AS input_value_name,
      prv.result_value,
      -- Run balance
      (SELECT SUM(TO_NUMBER(prv2.result_value))
       FROM   pay_run_values_v prv2,
              pay_run_results prr2,
              pay_assignment_actions paa2
       WHERE  prr2.run_result_id = prv2.run_result_id
       AND    paa2.assignment_action_id =
              prr2.assignment_action_id
       AND    paa2.payroll_action_id =
              ppa.payroll_action_id
       AND    prv2.element_type_id =
              prr.element_type_id
       AND    paa2.assignment_id =
              paa.assignment_id)
                                       AS element_total,
      -- Costing info
      pcd.segment1                     AS cost_center,
      pcd.segment2                     AS account,
      pcd.debit_or_credit,
      pcd.value                        AS costing_amount
    FROM
      pay_payroll_actions        ppa,
      pay_assignment_actions     paa,
      pay_run_results            prr,
      pay_run_values_v           prv,
      pay_element_types_f        pet,
      pay_element_classifications pec,
      per_all_assignments_f      paaf,
      per_all_people_f           papf,
      pay_payrolls_f             ppf,
      hr_all_organization_units  haou,
      pay_costs                  pcd
    WHERE
      ppa.payroll_action_id     = paa.payroll_action_id
      AND paa.assignment_action_id  = prr.assignment_action_id
      AND prr.run_result_id         = prv.run_result_id
      AND prr.element_type_id       = pet.element_type_id
      AND pet.classification_id     = pec.classification_id
      AND paa.assignment_id         = paaf.assignment_id
      AND paaf.person_id            = papf.person_id
      AND paaf.payroll_id           = ppf.payroll_id
      AND paaf.organization_id      = haou.organization_id
      AND paa.assignment_action_id  = pcd.assignment_action_id(+)
      -- DateTrack filters
      AND ppa.effective_date
          BETWEEN pet.effective_start_date
          AND pet.effective_end_date
      AND ppa.effective_date
          BETWEEN paaf.effective_start_date
          AND paaf.effective_end_date
      AND ppa.effective_date
          BETWEEN papf.effective_start_date
          AND papf.effective_end_date
      AND ppa.effective_date
          BETWEEN ppf.effective_start_date
          AND ppf.effective_end_date
      -- Filters
      AND ppa.action_type           = 'R'
      AND ppa.action_status         = 'C'
      AND ppa.payroll_id            = :p_payroll_id
      AND ppa.date_earned
          BETWEEN :p_start_date AND :p_end_date
      AND papf.employee_number      = NVL(
                                        :p_emp_number,
                                        papf.employee_number)
    ORDER BY
      papf.employee_number,
      pet.element_name
  Example 20: Payroll Balance Query
  exampleName: Employee YTD Balance Summary
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Query year-to-date payroll balances
    for all key earnings, deductions
    and tax balances per employee
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      papf.employee_number,
      papf.full_name,
      haou.name                        AS department,
      ppf.payroll_name,
      -- Gross earnings
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        'GROSS_EARNINGS',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS gross_earnings_ytd,
      -- Regular earnings
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        'REGULAR_EARNINGS',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS regular_earnings_ytd,
      -- Overtime
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        'OVERTIME_EARNINGS',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS overtime_ytd,
      -- Net pay
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        'NET_ASG_RUN',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS net_pay_ytd,
      -- Federal Income Tax
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        'FEDERAL_INCOME_TAX',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS federal_tax_ytd,
      -- State Income Tax
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        'STATE_INCOME_TAX',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS state_tax_ytd,
            -- Social Security Employee
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        'SOCIAL_SECURITY_EE',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS ss_ee_ytd,
      -- Social Security Employer
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        'SOCIAL_SECURITY_ER',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS ss_er_ytd,
      -- Medicare Employee
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        'MEDICARE_EE',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS medicare_ee_ytd,
      -- Medicare Employer
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        'MEDICARE_ER',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS medicare_er_ytd,
      -- Voluntary Deductions
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        'VOLUNTARY_DEDUCTIONS',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS vol_deductions_ytd,
      -- Involuntary Deductions
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        'INVOLUNTARY_DEDUCTIONS',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS invol_deductions_ytd,
      -- 401K Employee
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        '401K_DEDUCTIONS',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS retirement_401k_ytd,
      -- Health Insurance
      pay_balance_pkg.get_value(
        paa.assignment_action_id,
        'HEALTH_INSURANCE_EE',
        '_ASG_YTD'
      )                                AS health_ins_ee_ytd,
      -- Run date
      ppa.date_earned,
      ppa.effective_date               AS pay_date
    FROM
      pay_payroll_actions        ppa,
      pay_assignment_actions     paa,
      per_all_assignments_f      paaf,
      per_all_people_f           papf,
      pay_payrolls_f             ppf,
      hr_all_organization_units  haou
    WHERE
      ppa.payroll_action_id     = paa.payroll_action_id
      AND paa.assignment_id         = paaf.assignment_id
      AND paaf.person_id            = papf.person_id
      AND paaf.payroll_id           = ppf.payroll_id
      AND paaf.organization_id      = haou.organization_id
      -- DateTrack filters
      AND ppa.effective_date
          BETWEEN paaf.effective_start_date
          AND paaf.effective_end_date
      AND ppa.effective_date
          BETWEEN papf.effective_start_date
          AND papf.effective_end_date
      AND ppa.effective_date
          BETWEEN ppf.effective_start_date
          AND ppf.effective_end_date
      -- Latest run only
      AND ppa.payroll_action_id     = (
            SELECT MAX(ppa2.payroll_action_id)
            FROM   pay_payroll_actions ppa2,
                   pay_assignment_actions paa2
            WHERE  ppa2.payroll_action_id =
                   paa2.payroll_action_id
            AND    paa2.assignment_id =
                   paa.assignment_id
            AND    ppa2.action_type = 'R'
            AND    ppa2.action_status = 'C'
            AND    EXTRACT(YEAR FROM ppa2.date_earned)
                   = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM SYSDATE))
      AND ppa.action_type           = 'R'
      AND ppa.action_status         = 'C'
      AND ppf.payroll_id            = :p_payroll_id
      AND papf.employee_number      = NVL(
                                        :p_emp_number,
                                        papf.employee_number)
    ORDER BY
      haou.name,
      papf.employee_number
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION U — BENEFITS COMPLETE KNOWLEDGE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Topic 9: Oracle Benefits Complete Knowledge
knowledgeId: BEN-FUNC-001
moduleCode: BEN
topicName: Oracle Benefits Complete Setup and Processing
topicCategory: FUNCTIONAL
difficulty: EXPERT
learningObjectives: [
  Understand Benefits program structure,
  Configure benefit programs and plans,
  Set up eligibility profiles,
  Configure life events,
  Process open enrollment,
  Manage dependent and beneficiary data,
  Process COBRA administration,
  Understand benefits costing,
  Query benefits enrollment data,
  Troubleshoot enrollment issues
]
keyConceptsExplained: |
  1. BENEFITS HIERARCHY
  ─────────────────────────────
  Program
  └── Plan Type
      └── Plan
          └── Option
              └── Coverage
                  └── Enrollment
  Example:
  Program: US Benefits 2025
  └── Plan Type: Medical
      └── Plan: Blue Cross PPO
          └── Option: Employee + Family
              └── Coverage: $500 Deductible
                  └── Enrollment: John Smith
  2. PLAN TYPES
  ─────────────────────────────
  Medical:
  - HMO, PPO, HDHP, EPO
  - Employee, Employee+Spouse,
    Employee+Children, Family
  Dental:
  - Basic, Enhanced, Orthodontia
  Vision:
  - Basic, Enhanced
  Life Insurance:
  - Basic Life (1x salary)
  - Supplemental Life (multiple)
  - AD&D (Accidental Death)
  Disability:
  - Short-Term Disability (STD)
  - Long-Term Disability (LTD)
  Retirement:
  - 401(k) Traditional
  - 401(k) Roth
  - Pension
  Flexible Spending Accounts:
  - Health FSA
  - Dependent Care FSA
  - Health Savings Account (HSA)
  3. ELIGIBILITY PROFILES
  ─────────────────────────────
  Criteria Types:
  - Employment:
    Full-time/Part-time
    Regular/Temporary
    Minimum hours per week
  - Assignment:
    Grade, Job, Organization
    Location, Payroll
  - Personal:
    Age, Gender, Marital Status
    Citizenship, Disability
  - Service:
    Minimum service length
    Hire date criteria
    Rehire criteria
  4. LIFE EVENTS
  ─────────────────────────────
  Life Event Types:
  - New Hire: Triggers initial enrollment
  - Open Enrollment: Annual enrollment
  - Marriage/Divorce: Family status change
  - Birth/Adoption: New dependent
  - Loss of Coverage: COBRA trigger
  - Termination: Coverage termination
  - Age-In/Age-Out: Age-based eligibility
  Life Event Processing:
  Life Event Occurs
  → Triggering Event Detected
  → Enrollment Window Opens
  → Employee Makes Elections
  → Elections Processed
  → Coverage Effective
    5. ENROLLMENT PROCESS (continued)
  ─────────────────────────────────
  Open Enrollment Process:
  Step 4: Employee self-service enrollment
          via SSHR or Benefits module
  Step 5: Dependent verification
  Step 6: Beneficiary designation
  Step 7: Evidence of Insurability (EOI)
          for supplemental life
  Step 8: Elections finalized
  Step 9: Enrollment processed
  Step 10: Payroll deductions updated
  Step 11: Coverage certificates generated
  Enrollment Methods:
  - Self-Service (iRecruitment/SSHR)
  - HR Administrator entry
  - Batch enrollment via API
  - Passive enrollment (defaults)
  6. COBRA ADMINISTRATION
  ─────────────────────────────
  COBRA Qualifying Events:
  - Termination of employment
  - Reduction in hours
  - Death of covered employee
  - Divorce or legal separation
  - Dependent child aging out
  - Medicare entitlement
  COBRA Process:
  Qualifying Event Occurs
  → Notice of Qualifying Event
  → 14-day notice to administrator
  → 44-day COBRA notice to participant
  → Election period: 60 days
  → Coverage retroactive to event date
  → Premium payment: 102% of cost
  COBRA Duration:
  - Termination/Reduction: 18 months
  - Death/Divorce/Dependent: 36 months
  - Medicare: 36 months
  7. BENEFITS COSTING
  ─────────────────────────────
  Deduction Costing:
  - Employee deductions → payroll element
  - Employer contributions → benefit cost
  - Both feed GL through payroll costing
  GL Accounting:
  Employee Deduction:
  DR: Salary/Wages (net reduction)
  CR: Benefits Payable Account
  Employer Contribution:
  DR: Benefits Expense Account
  CR: Benefits Payable Account
  On Payment to Carrier:
  DR: Benefits Payable Account
  CR: Bank/Cash Account
sqlExamples: [
  Example 21: Benefits Enrollment Query
  exampleName: Employee Benefits Enrollment Details
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Query complete benefits enrollment
    for all employees showing plan
    elections, coverage and costs
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      papf.employee_number,
      papf.full_name,
      haou.name                        AS department,
      -- Program details
      bpr.name                         AS program_name,
      -- Plan type
      bpt.name                         AS plan_type,
      -- Plan details
      bp.name                          AS plan_name,
      -- Option details
      bopt.name                        AS option_name,
      -- Enrollment details
      ler.name                         AS life_event,
      bbe.effective_start_date         AS coverage_start,
      bbe.effective_end_date           AS coverage_end,
      bbe.enrt_cvg_strt_dt             AS coverage_effective,
      bbe.enrt_cvg_thru_dt             AS coverage_end_date,
      -- Coverage amount
      bbe.bnft_amt                     AS coverage_amount,
      -- Employee cost
      bbe.prtt_enrt_rslt_id,
      (SELECT SUM(bec.val)
       FROM   ben_enrt_rt bec
       WHERE  bec.prtt_enrt_rslt_id =
              bbe.prtt_enrt_rslt_id
       AND    bec.enrt_rt_ctfn_typ_cd = 'EE')
                                       AS employee_cost,
      -- Employer cost
      (SELECT SUM(bec2.val)
       FROM   ben_enrt_rt bec2
       WHERE  bec2.prtt_enrt_rslt_id =
              bbe.prtt_enrt_rslt_id
       AND    bec2.enrt_rt_ctfn_typ_cd = 'ER')
                                       AS employer_cost,
      -- Dependents enrolled
      (SELECT COUNT(*)
       FROM   ben_prtt_enrt_dpnt bped
       WHERE  bped.prtt_enrt_rslt_id =
              bbe.prtt_enrt_rslt_id
       AND    bped.dpnt_inelig_flag = 'N')
                                       AS dependent_count,
      -- Beneficiaries
      (SELECT COUNT(*)
       FROM   ben_per_bnfts_bal bpbb
       WHERE  bpbb.person_id =
              papf.person_id)
                                       AS beneficiary_count,
      -- Waived flag
      bbe.bnft_typ_cd,
      CASE bbe.bnft_typ_cd
        WHEN 'WAIV' THEN 'WAIVED'
        ELSE 'ENROLLED'
      END                              AS enrollment_status
    FROM
      ben_prtt_enrt_rslt_f       bbe,
      ben_pgm_f                  bpr,
      ben_pl_typ_f               bpt,
      ben_pl_f                   bp,
      ben_opt_f                  bopt,
      ben_ler_f                  ler,
      per_all_people_f           papf,
      per_all_assignments_f      paaf,
      hr_all_organization_units  haou
    WHERE
      bbe.pgm_id                = bpr.pgm_id
      AND bbe.pl_typ_id             = bpt.pl_typ_id
      AND bbe.pl_id                 = bp.pl_id
      AND bbe.opt_id                = bopt.opt_id(+)
      AND bbe.ler_id                = ler.ler_id(+)
      AND bbe.person_id             = papf.person_id
      AND paaf.person_id            = papf.person_id
      AND paaf.organization_id      = haou.organization_id
      AND paaf.primary_flag         = 'Y'
      -- DateTrack filters
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN bbe.effective_start_date
              AND bbe.effective_end_date
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN bpr.effective_start_date
              AND bpr.effective_end_date
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN bpt.effective_start_date
              AND bpt.effective_end_date
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN bp.effective_start_date
              AND bp.effective_end_date
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN bopt.effective_start_date(+)
              AND bopt.effective_end_date(+)
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN ler.effective_start_date(+)
              AND ler.effective_end_date(+)
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN papf.effective_start_date
              AND papf.effective_end_date
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN paaf.effective_start_date
              AND paaf.effective_end_date
      AND bbe.business_group_id     = :p_bg_id
      AND bbe.prtt_enrt_rslt_stat_cd = 'ACTIVE'
    ORDER BY
      haou.name,
      papf.employee_number,
      bpr.name,
      bpt.name
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION V — SCM AND INVENTORY COMPLETE KNOWLEDGE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Topic 10: SCM and Inventory (continued)
learningObjectives: [
  Understand Inventory organization structure,
  Configure inventory parameters,
  Set up item master and categories,
  Understand subinventories and locators,
  Process inventory transactions,
  Understand costing methods,
  Configure min-max and reorder planning,
  Process cycle counts and physical inventory,
  Understand inter-org transfers,
  Query inventory balances and transactions
]
keyConceptsExplained: |
  1. INVENTORY ORGANIZATION STRUCTURE
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Item Master Organization:
  - Top-level org that owns item definitions
  - All child orgs inherit item master
  - Only one per business group
  Inventory Organization:
  - Physical warehouse or plant
  - Has its own parameters
  - Has subinventories
  - Has locators (optional)
  - Has costing method
  Organization Hierarchy:
  Item Master Org
  └── Inventory Org 1 (Main Warehouse)
      └── Subinventory: STORES
          └── Locator: A-01-01
      └── Subinventory: FINISHED_GOODS
          └── Locator: B-01-01
  └── Inventory Org 2 (Regional DC)
  └── Inventory Org 3 (Manufacturing)
  2. INVENTORY PARAMETERS
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Key Parameters:
  - Costing Method:
    STANDARD|AVERAGE|FIFO|LIFO|LAYER
  - Default Cost Group
  - Negative Balance: Allow/Disallow
  - Serial Number Control:
    NO_CONTROL|PREDEFINED|AT_RECEIPT|
    AT_SALES_ORDER|DYNAMIC_ENTRY
  - Lot Control:
    NO_LOT_CONTROL|FULL_LOT_CONTROL
  - Locator Control:
    NO_LOCATOR|PRESPECIFIED|DYNAMIC
  3. ITEM MASTER SETUP
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Item Attributes:
  - General:
    Item Number, Description
    Item Type, Category
    UOM, Conversions
  - Inventory:
    Inventory Item: Yes/No
    Stock Enabled: Yes/No
    Transactable: Yes/No
    Reservable: Yes/No
    Lot Control, Serial Control
    Shelf Life, Cycle Count Enabled
  - Costing:
    Costing Enabled: Yes/No
    Include in Rollup: Yes/No
    Standard Cost (if standard costing)
  - Purchasing:
    Purchased: Yes/No
    Purchasable: Yes/No
    Unit of Issue
    List Price
    Outside Processing: Yes/No
  - Order Management:
    Customer Ordered: Yes/No
    Shippable: Yes/No
    Invoiceable: Yes/No
    Revenue: Yes/No
  - Bills of Material:
    BOM Allowed: Yes/No
    BOM Item Type:
    STANDARD|MODEL|OPTION_CLASS|PLANNING
  Item Categories:
  - Category Set: Groups of categories
  - Category: Classification code
  - Default Category Set per functional area
  4. INVENTORY TRANSACTIONS
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Transaction Types:
  Receipts:
  - PO Receipt: Receive against PO
  - RTV: Return to Vendor
  - Customer Return: RMA receipt
  - Inter-Org Receipt: From other org
  - Miscellaneous Receipt: Manual receipt
  Issues:
  - Sales Order Issue: Ship to customer
  - WIP Issue: To manufacturing
  - Inter-Org Transfer: To other org
  - Miscellaneous Issue: Manual issue
  Transfers:
  - Subinventory Transfer: Within org
  - Locator Transfer: Within subinventory
  - Inter-Org Transfer: Between orgs
  Adjustments:
  - Cycle Count Adjustment
  - Physical Inventory Adjustment
  - Cost Update (standard costing)
  Transaction Accounting:
  PO Receipt to Inventory:
  DR: Inventory Valuation
  CR: Receiving Inspection Account
  Delivery to Inventory:
  DR: Inventory Valuation (at PO price)
  CR: Receiving Inspection
  Sales Order Issue:
  DR: COGS Account
  CR: Inventory Valuation
  Miscellaneous Issue:
  DR: Expense Account
  CR: Inventory Valuation
  5. COSTING METHODS
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Standard Costing:
  - Predetermined cost per item
  - Variances tracked:
    * Purchase Price Variance (PPV)
    * Invoice Price Variance (IPV)
    * WIP Variances
  - Cost update required for changes
  Average Costing:
  - Running average of actual costs
  - No standard cost variances
  - Automatically updated on receipt
  FIFO Costing:
  - First In First Out
  - Maintains cost layers
  - Layer cost consumed in order
  LIFO Costing:
  - Last In First Out
  - Maintains cost layers
  6. CYCLE COUNT PROCESS
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Step 1: Define Cycle Count
  - Name and description
  - Organization
  - Items to count
  - ABC classification
  Step 2: Generate Count Requests
  - System selects items by ABC class
  - A items: Monthly
  - B items: Quarterly
  - C items: Annually
  Step 3: Print Count Sheets
  - Blank sheets (no quantities shown)
  - Or show on-hand quantities
  Step 4: Physical Count
  - Count items in warehouse
  - Record actual quantities
  Step 5: Enter Count Results
  - Enter counted quantities
  - System shows variance
  Step 6: Approve Adjustments
  - Auto-approve within tolerance
  - Manual approval outside tolerance
  Step 7: Post Adjustments
  - Create inventory transactions
  - Update on-hand quantities
  - Create accounting entries
  7. MIN-MAX PLANNING
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Parameters per Item/Subinventory:
  - Min Quantity: Reorder trigger
  - Max Quantity: Target quantity
  - Multiple: Order in multiples of
  - Fixed Lot Size: Fixed order qty
  Process:
  Run Min-Max Planning Report
  → System checks current on-hand
  → If on-hand < Min Quantity:
    Create Purchase Requisition OR
    Create WIP Job OR
    Create Move Order
  → Quantity to order:
    Max Quantity - Current On-hand
sqlExamples: [
  Example 22: Inventory Balance Query
  exampleName: Current Inventory On-Hand Balances
  difficultyLevel: INTERMEDIATE
  businessPurpose: |
    Query current on-hand inventory
    balances with lot and locator
    details and unit costs
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      mp.organization_code,
      haou.name                        AS org_name,
      msib.segment1                    AS item_number,
      msib.description                 AS item_description,
      msib.primary_uom_code            AS uom,
      msib.item_type,
      -- Category
      mic.category_id,
      (SELECT mc.segment1
       FROM   mtl_categories mc
       WHERE  mc.category_id =
              mic.category_id)         AS category,
      -- Subinventory
      moq.subinventory_code,
      -- Locator
      milk.concatenated_segments       AS locator,
      -- Lot number
      moq.lot_number,
      -- Serial number (if controlled)
      msn.serial_number,
	        -- On-hand quantities
      moq.primary_transaction_quantity AS on_hand_qty,
      moq.secondary_transaction_quantity AS secondary_qty,
      -- Unit cost
      cic.item_cost                    AS unit_cost,
      -- Total value
      (moq.primary_transaction_quantity *
       cic.item_cost)                  AS total_value,
      -- Cost details
      cic.material_cost,
      cic.material_overhead_cost,
      cic.resource_cost,
      cic.outside_processing_cost,
      cic.overhead_cost,
      -- Lot expiry
      mln.expiration_date              AS lot_expiry_date,
      TRUNC(mln.expiration_date) -
      TRUNC(SYSDATE)                   AS days_to_expiry,
      -- Reorder information
      msc_min.minimum_quantity         AS min_qty,
      msc_min.maximum_quantity         AS max_qty,
      -- Last transaction
      (SELECT MAX(mmt.transaction_date)
       FROM   mtl_material_transactions mmt
       WHERE  mmt.inventory_item_id =
              moq.inventory_item_id
       AND    mmt.organization_id =
              moq.organization_id
       AND    mmt.subinventory_code =
              moq.subinventory_code)   AS last_txn_date,
      -- ABC class
      mca.abc_class_name,
      -- Cycle count enabled
      msib.cycle_count_enabled_flag
    FROM
      mtl_onhand_quantities_detail moq,
      mtl_system_items_b           msib,
      mtl_parameters               mp,
      hr_all_organization_units    haou,
      mtl_item_locations_kfv       milk,
      mtl_lot_numbers              mln,
      mtl_serial_numbers           msn,
      cst_item_costs               cic,
      mtl_item_categories          mic,
      mtl_abc_assgn_group_classes  mca,
      mtl_secondary_inventories    msi,
      mtl_item_sub_inventories     msc_min
    WHERE
      moq.inventory_item_id     = msib.inventory_item_id
      AND moq.organization_id       = msib.organization_id
      AND moq.organization_id       = mp.organization_id
      AND haou.organization_id      = moq.organization_id
      AND moq.locator_id            = milk.inventory_location_id(+)
      AND moq.lot_number            = mln.lot_number(+)
      AND moq.inventory_item_id     = mln.inventory_item_id(+)
      AND moq.organization_id       = mln.organization_id(+)
      AND moq.serial_number         = msn.serial_number(+)
      AND moq.inventory_item_id     = msn.inventory_item_id(+)
      AND cic.inventory_item_id     = moq.inventory_item_id
      AND cic.organization_id       = moq.organization_id
      AND cic.cost_type_id          = 1
      AND mic.inventory_item_id     = msib.inventory_item_id
      AND mic.organization_id       = msib.organization_id
      AND mic.category_set_id       = :p_category_set_id
      AND mca.inventory_item_id(+)  = msib.inventory_item_id
      AND mca.organization_id(+)    = msib.organization_id
      AND msi.organization_id       = moq.organization_id
      AND msi.secondary_inventory_name
                                    = moq.subinventory_code
      AND msc_min.inventory_item_id(+)
                                    = msib.inventory_item_id
      AND msc_min.organization_id(+)= msib.organization_id
      AND msc_min.secondary_inventory(+)
                                    = moq.subinventory_code
      AND moq.organization_id       = :p_org_id
      AND msib.segment1             = NVL(
                                        :p_item_number,
                                        msib.segment1)
      AND moq.subinventory_code     = NVL(
                                        :p_subinventory,
                                        moq.subinventory_code)
      AND moq.primary_transaction_quantity > 0
    ORDER BY
      msib.segment1,
      moq.subinventory_code,
      milk.concatenated_segments
  Example 23: Inventory Transaction History
  exampleName: Item Transaction History with Accounting
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Query complete transaction history
    for an inventory item showing
    all movements with accounting
    entries and source documents
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      mmt.transaction_id,
      mmt.transaction_date,
      mmt.transaction_type_id,
      mtt.transaction_type_name,
      mmt.transaction_action_id,
      mmt.transaction_source_type_id,
      mtst.transaction_source_type_name,
      -- Item details
      msib.segment1                    AS item_number,
      msib.description,
      mmt.subinventory_code,
      milk.concatenated_segments       AS locator,
      mmt.lot_number,
      mmt.serial_number,
      -- Quantity
      mmt.primary_quantity,
      mmt.transaction_uom,
      mmt.transaction_quantity,
      -- Cost
      mmt.actual_cost,
      mmt.transaction_cost,
      (mmt.primary_quantity *
       mmt.actual_cost)                AS transaction_value,
      -- Source document
      mmt.transaction_source_id,
      mmt.transaction_source_name,
      mmt.source_code,
      mmt.source_line_id,
      -- For PO receipts
      CASE mmt.transaction_source_type_id
        WHEN 1 THEN
          (SELECT pha.segment1
           FROM   po_headers_all pha
           WHERE  pha.po_header_id =
                  mmt.transaction_source_id)
        ELSE NULL
      END                              AS po_number,
      -- For Sales Orders
      CASE mmt.transaction_source_type_id
        WHEN 2 THEN
          (SELECT TO_CHAR(ooh.order_number)
           FROM   oe_order_headers_all ooh
           WHERE  ooh.header_id =
                  mmt.transaction_source_id)
        ELSE NULL
      END                              AS so_number,
      -- For WIP Jobs
      CASE mmt.transaction_source_type_id
        WHEN 5 THEN
          (SELECT wdj.wip_entity_id
           FROM   wip_discrete_jobs wdj
           WHERE  wdj.wip_entity_id =
                  mmt.transaction_source_id)
        ELSE NULL
      END                              AS wip_job_id,
      -- Accounting entries
      mta.accounting_line_type,
      mta.base_transaction_value,
      mta.contra_set_id,
      gcc.concatenated_segments        AS gl_account,
      -- Transfer details
      mmt.transfer_organization_id,
      mmt.transfer_subinventory,
      -- Reference
      mmt.reason_id,
      mtr.reason_name,
      mmt.transaction_reference,
      -- Created by
      fu.user_name                     AS created_by,
      mmt.creation_date
    FROM
          FROM
      mtl_material_transactions      mmt,
      mtl_transaction_types          mtt,
      mtl_txn_source_types           mtst,
      mtl_system_items_b             msib,
      mtl_item_locations_kfv         milk,
      mtl_transaction_accounts       mta,
      gl_code_combinations_kfv       gcc,
      mtl_transaction_reasons        mtr,
      fnd_user                       fu
    WHERE
      mmt.transaction_type_id       = mtt.transaction_type_id
      AND mmt.transaction_source_type_id
                                    = mtst.transaction_source_type_id
      AND mmt.inventory_item_id     = msib.inventory_item_id
      AND mmt.organization_id       = msib.organization_id
      AND mmt.locator_id            = milk.inventory_location_id(+)
      AND mta.transaction_id(+)     = mmt.transaction_id
      AND mta.reference_account     = gcc.code_combination_id(+)
      AND mmt.reason_id             = mtr.reason_id(+)
      AND mmt.created_by            = fu.user_id
      AND mmt.organization_id       = :p_org_id
      AND msib.segment1             = NVL(
                                        :p_item_number,
                                        msib.segment1)
      AND mmt.transaction_date
          BETWEEN :p_start_date AND :p_end_date
    ORDER BY
      mmt.transaction_date DESC,
      mmt.transaction_id
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION W — CASH MANAGEMENT COMPLETE KNOWLEDGE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Topic 11: Cash Management Complete Knowledge
knowledgeId: CM-FUNC-001
moduleCode: CM
topicName: Oracle Cash Management Complete Setup
topicCategory: FUNCTIONAL
difficulty: ADVANCED
learningObjectives: [
  Understand Cash Management purpose,
  Configure bank accounts and statements,
  Process bank statement import,
  Perform bank reconciliation,
  Understand cash forecasting,
  Configure cash pools and positions,
  Process intercompany settlements,
  Query cash management data,
  Troubleshoot reconciliation issues,
  Understand integration with AP and AR
]
keyConceptsExplained: |
  1. CASH MANAGEMENT PURPOSE
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Oracle Cash Management provides:
  - Bank account management
  - Bank statement import and processing
  - Automatic and manual reconciliation
  - Cash forecasting and positioning
  - Bank account transfers
  - Interest calculation
  2. BANK SETUP HIERARCHY
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Bank
  └── Bank Branch
      └── Bank Account
          └── Bank Account Uses
              └── AP Payment Document
              └── AR Receipt Method
  Key Setup Steps:
  Step 1: Create Bank
    - Bank Name
    - Bank Number (ABA Routing)
    - Country
    - Bank Type
  Step 2: Create Bank Branch
    - Branch Name
    - Branch Number
    - Address
    - BIC/SWIFT Code
  Step 3: Create Bank Account
    - Account Name
    - Account Number
    - Currency
    - Account Type: CHECKING|SAVINGS|MONEY_MARKET
    - GL Account (Cash Account)
    - Cash Clearing Account
    - Owner: Legal Entity
  Step 4: Assign to AP
    - Payment Document
    - Payment Method
    - Payment Format
  Step 5: Assign to AR
    - Receipt Class
    - Receipt Method
  3. BANK STATEMENT PROCESSING
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Bank Statement Sources:
  - Manual entry
  - File import (BAI/BAI2/SWIFT MT940)
  - Electronic Data Interchange (EDI)
  - Bank direct connection (API)
  Statement Import Process:
  Receive Bank File
  → Load to CE_STATEMENT_HEADERS_INT
  → Load to CE_STATEMENT_LINES_INT
  → Run Bank Statement Import program
  → Validate statement
  → CE_STATEMENT_HEADERS created
  → CE_STATEMENT_LINES created
  BAI2 File Format:
  Record 01: File Header
  Record 02: Group Header
  Record 03: Account Identifier
  Record 16: Transaction Detail
  Record 49: Account Trailer
  Record 98: Group Trailer
  Record 99: File Trailer
  4. BANK RECONCILIATION
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Reconciliation Types:
  Automatic Reconciliation:
  - System matches statement lines
    to system transactions
  - Matching Rules:
    * Amount match
