    * Reference number match
    * Date tolerance
    * Check number match
  Manual Reconciliation:
  - User manually matches
    statement lines to transactions
  - Used for exceptions
  Reconciliation Sources:
  AP Side:
  - Payments (ap_checks_all)
  - Cleared payments
  - Voided payments
  AR Side:
  - Receipts (ar_cash_receipts_all)
  - Cleared receipts
  - Reversed receipts
  GL Side:
  - Journal entries
  - Manual transactions
  Outstanding Items:
  - Unreconciled statement lines
  - Outstanding system transactions
  - Timing differences
  5. CASH FORECASTING
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Forecast Sources:
  - AP: Open invoices due
  - AR: Outstanding receivables
  - PO: Open purchase orders
  - OM: Open sales orders
  - GL: Budget entries
  - Treasury: Manual entries
  Forecast Periods:
  - Daily
  - Weekly
  - Monthly
  Cash Position Formula:
  Opening Balance
  + Expected Receipts (AR)
  - Expected Payments (AP)
  ± Other Cash Flows
  = Closing Cash Position
sqlExamples: [
  Example 24: Bank Reconciliation Status Query
  exampleName: Bank Statement Reconciliation Status
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Query bank statement reconciliation
    status showing reconciled and
    unreconciled transactions
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      csh.statement_number,
      csh.statement_date,
      csh.gl_date,
      cba.bank_account_name,
      cba.bank_account_num,
      ab.bank_name,
      -- Statement totals
      csh.control_begin_balance        AS opening_balance,
      csh.control_end_balance          AS closing_balance,
      csh.control_total_dr             AS total_debits,
      csh.control_total_cr             AS total_credits,
      -- Line details
      csl.line_number,
      csl.trx_date                     AS transaction_date,
      csl.amount                       AS statement_amount,
      csl.trx_type,
      csl.bank_trx_number,
      csl.description                  AS statement_description,
      csl.status                       AS reconciliation_status,
      -- Reconciled to AP Payment
      CASE csl.status
        WHEN 'RECONCILED' THEN
          (SELECT aca.check_number
           FROM   ce_reconciliations cr,
                  ap_checks_all aca
           WHERE  cr.statement_line_id =
                  csl.statement_line_id
           AND    aca.check_id =
                  cr.reference_id
           AND    cr.reference_type = 'PAYMENT'
           AND    ROWNUM = 1)
        ELSE NULL
      END                              AS ap_check_number,
            -- Reconciled to AR Receipt
      CASE csl.status
        WHEN 'RECONCILED' THEN
          (SELECT acr.receipt_number
           FROM   ce_reconciliations cr,
                  ar_cash_receipts_all acr
           WHERE  cr.statement_line_id =
                  csl.statement_line_id
           AND    acr.cash_receipt_id =
                  cr.reference_id
           AND    cr.reference_type = 'RECEIPT'
           AND    ROWNUM = 1)
        ELSE NULL
      END                              AS ar_receipt_number,
      -- Days unreconciled
      CASE csl.status
        WHEN 'UNRECONCILED' THEN
          TRUNC(SYSDATE) -
          TRUNC(csl.trx_date)
        ELSE NULL
      END                              AS days_unreconciled,
      -- GL account
      cba.asset_code_combination_id,
      gcc.concatenated_segments        AS cash_gl_account
    FROM
      ce_statement_headers           csh,
      ce_statement_lines             csl,
      ce_bank_accounts               cba,
      ce_bank_branches_v             ab,
      gl_code_combinations_kfv       gcc
    WHERE
      csh.statement_header_id       = csl.statement_header_id
      AND csh.bank_account_id           = cba.bank_account_id
      AND cba.bank_branch_id            = ab.branch_party_id
      AND cba.asset_code_combination_id = gcc.code_combination_id(+)
      AND csh.bank_account_id           = :p_bank_account_id
      AND csh.statement_date
          BETWEEN :p_start_date AND :p_end_date
      AND csl.status                    = NVL(
                                            :p_status,
                                            csl.status)
    ORDER BY
      csh.statement_date DESC,
      csl.line_number
  Example 25: Cash Position Query
  exampleName: Daily Cash Position and Forecast
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Query current cash position
    combining bank balances with
    expected AP payments and
    AR receipts for cash forecasting
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      cba.bank_account_name,
      cba.bank_account_num,
      cba.currency_code,
      ab.bank_name,
      -- Current bank balance
      (SELECT csh.control_end_balance
       FROM   ce_statement_headers csh
       WHERE  csh.bank_account_id =
              cba.bank_account_id
       AND    csh.statement_date = (
                SELECT MAX(csh2.statement_date)
                FROM   ce_statement_headers csh2
                WHERE  csh2.bank_account_id =
                       cba.bank_account_id))
                                       AS last_stmt_balance,
      -- Last statement date
      (SELECT MAX(csh.statement_date)
       FROM   ce_statement_headers csh
       WHERE  csh.bank_account_id =
              cba.bank_account_id)     AS last_stmt_date,
      -- Outstanding AP payments (uncleared)
      (SELECT NVL(SUM(aca.amount),0)
       FROM   ap_checks_all aca
       WHERE  aca.bank_account_id =
              cba.bank_account_id
       AND    aca.status_lookup_code = 'NEGOTIABLE'
       AND    aca.cleared_date IS NULL
       AND    aca.void_date IS NULL)   AS outstanding_ap_pmts,
      -- AP payments due next 7 days
      (SELECT NVL(SUM(
                aia.invoice_amount -
                NVL(aia.amount_paid,0)),0)
       FROM   ap_invoices_all aia,
              ap_payment_schedules_all apsa
       WHERE  aia.invoice_id = apsa.invoice_id
       AND    aia.payment_status_flag != 'Y'
       AND    aia.cancelled_date IS NULL
       AND    apsa.payment_status_flag != 'Y'
       AND    apsa.due_date
              BETWEEN TRUNC(SYSDATE)
              AND TRUNC(SYSDATE) + 7
       AND    aia.org_id IN (
                SELECT org_id
                FROM   ap_system_parameters_all
                WHERE  bank_account_id =
                       cba.bank_account_id))
                                       AS ap_due_7_days,
      -- AP payments due next 30 days
      (SELECT NVL(SUM(
                aia.invoice_amount -
                NVL(aia.amount_paid,0)),0)
       FROM   ap_invoices_all aia,
              ap_payment_schedules_all apsa
       WHERE  aia.invoice_id = apsa.invoice_id
       AND    aia.payment_status_flag != 'Y'
       AND    aia.cancelled_date IS NULL
       AND    apsa.payment_status_flag != 'Y'
       AND    apsa.due_date
              BETWEEN TRUNC(SYSDATE)
              AND TRUNC(SYSDATE) + 30
       AND    aia.org_id IN (
                SELECT org_id
                FROM   ap_system_parameters_all
                WHERE  bank_account_id =
                       cba.bank_account_id))
                                       AS ap_due_30_days,
      -- AR expected receipts next 7 days
      (SELECT NVL(SUM(rct.amount_due_remaining),0)
       FROM   ra_customer_trx_all rct,
              ar_payment_schedules_all apsa
       WHERE  rct.customer_trx_id = apsa.customer_trx_id
       AND    apsa.amount_due_remaining > 0
       AND    apsa.due_date
              BETWEEN TRUNC(SYSDATE)
              AND TRUNC(SYSDATE) + 7
       AND    rct.org_id IN (
                SELECT rr.org_id
                FROM   ar_receipt_methods rr,
                       ar_receipt_classes rc
                WHERE  rr.receipt_class_id =
                       rc.receipt_class_id))
                                       AS ar_expected_7_days,
      -- AR expected receipts next 30 days
      (SELECT NVL(SUM(rct.amount_due_remaining),0)
       FROM   ra_customer_trx_all rct,
              ar_payment_schedules_all apsa
       WHERE  rct.customer_trx_id = apsa.customer_trx_id
       AND    apsa.amount_due_remaining > 0
       AND    apsa.due_date
              BETWEEN TRUNC(SYSDATE)
              AND TRUNC(SYSDATE) + 30)
                                       AS ar_expected_30_days,
      -- Net cash position calculation
      (SELECT csh.control_end_balance
       FROM   ce_statement_headers csh
       WHERE  csh.bank_account_id =
              cba.bank_account_id
       AND    csh.statement_date = (
                SELECT MAX(csh2.statement_date)
                FROM   ce_statement_headers csh2
                WHERE  csh2.bank_account_id =
                       cba.bank_account_id)) -
      (SELECT NVL(SUM(aca.amount),0)
       FROM   ap_checks_all aca
       WHERE  aca.bank_account_id =
              cba.bank_account_id
             AND    aca.status_lookup_code = 'NEGOTIABLE'
       AND    aca.cleared_date IS NULL
       AND    aca.void_date IS NULL)   AS net_cash_position,
      -- Currency
      gcc.concatenated_segments        AS cash_account
    FROM
      ce_bank_accounts               cba,
      ce_bank_branches_v             ab,
      gl_code_combinations_kfv       gcc
    WHERE
      cba.bank_branch_id            = ab.branch_party_id
      AND cba.asset_code_combination_id
                                    = gcc.code_combination_id(+)
      AND cba.account_owner_org_id  = :p_org_id
      AND cba.bank_account_id       = NVL(
                                        :p_bank_account_id,
                                        cba.bank_account_id)
    ORDER BY
      cba.bank_account_name
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION X — iEXPENSE COMPLETE KNOWLEDGE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Topic 12: iExpense Complete Knowledge
knowledgeId: iEXP-FUNC-001
moduleCode: iEXP
topicName: Oracle iExpense Complete Setup and Processing
topicCategory: FUNCTIONAL
difficulty: INTERMEDIATE
learningObjectives: [
  Understand iExpense setup and configuration,
  Configure expense templates and categories,
  Set up expense policies and rules,
  Process expense report submission,
  Configure approval workflows,
  Understand corporate card integration,
  Process expense reimbursement,
  Query expense report data,
  Troubleshoot expense issues,
  Understand iExpense and AP integration
]
keyConceptsExplained: |
  1. iEXPENSE SETUP
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  System Options:
  - Enable Web Expenses: Yes/No
  - Expense Clearing Account
  - Default Payment Method
  - Receipts Required Amount
  - Notify Approver: Days before due
  - Maximum Attachments
  Expense Categories:
  - Airfare
  - Hotel
  - Meals
  - Car Rental
  - Ground Transportation
  - Entertainment
  - Communication
  - Miscellaneous
  Expense Templates:
  - Define required fields per category
  - Set amount limits
  - Require receipts above threshold
  - Define justification requirements
  2. EXPENSE POLICY CONFIGURATION
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Policy Types:
  Amount Limits:
  - Per Transaction: $500 hotel/night
  - Per Day: $75 meals/day
  - Per Trip: $5000 total
  Receipt Requirements:
  - Required above: $25 default
  - Original receipts vs copies
  - Electronic receipt acceptance
  Booking Requirements:
  - Preferred vendors only
  - Advance booking required
  - Manager pre-approval for travel
  Itemization Rules:
  - Hotel must be itemized:
    Room Rate, Taxes, Parking, Meals
  - Per diem vs actual expense
  3. EXPENSE REPORT PROCESSING
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Employee Submission Process:
  Step 1: Login to iExpense (SSHR)
  Step 2: Create Expense Report
    - Purpose/Description
    - Default Expense Account
    - Employee Cost Center
  Step 3: Add Expense Lines
    - Category
    - Date
    - Amount
    - Description
    - Merchant
    - Receipt attached
  Step 4: Add Attendees (Entertainment)
  Step 5: Split Expenses (if needed)
  Step 6: Submit for Approval
  Approval Routing:
  - Direct Supervisor
  - Cost Center Manager
  - Finance Reviewer
  - Based on AME rules
  AP Processing:
  Approved Expense Report
  → Expense Report Export Program
  → AP Invoice created
  → Payment processed
  → Reimbursement to employee
  4. CORPORATE CARD INTEGRATION
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Card Types:
  - Individual Liability: Employee pays
  - Corporate Liability: Company pays
  Integration Process:
  Card Transactions Feed
  → Credit Card Transaction Import
  → Transactions visible in iExpense
  → Employee assigns to expense report
  → Submit for approval
  → Payment to card company
  Card Programs:
  - Visa Corporate
  - MasterCard Corporate
  - American Express Corporate
  5. ADVANCES PROCESSING
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Cash Advance Process:
  Request Advance
  → Manager Approval
  → AP Prepayment created
  → Payment to employee
  → Employee submits expense report
  → Advance applied to expense report
  → Net amount reimbursed
  iExpense Integration with AP:
  - Expense reports create AP invoices
  - Payment method: EFT/Check
  - GL account from expense lines
  - Supplier = Employee
sqlExamples: [
  Example 26: iExpense Report Query
  exampleName: Expense Report Detail with Approval Status
  difficultyLevel: INTERMEDIATE
  businessPurpose: |
    Query expense reports with
    complete approval history
    and reimbursement status
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      aerh.report_header_id,
      aerh.report_number,
      aerh.report_date,
      aerh.description                 AS report_purpose,
      aerh.total                       AS report_total,
      aerh.currency_code,
      aerh.expense_status_code         AS report_status,
      aerh.reimbursement_status_code,
      -- Employee details
      papf.employee_number,
      papf.full_name                   AS employee_name,
      haou.name                        AS department,
      -- Approval details
      aerh.source                      AS submission_source,
      aerh.last_update_date            AS last_update,
      -- Manager approval
      papf_mgr.full_name               AS manager_name,
      -- AP Invoice linkage
      aia.invoice_num                  AS ap_invoice_num,
      aia.invoice_date                 AS ap_invoice_date,
      aia.approval_status              AS ap_approval_status,
      aia.payment_status_flag          AS payment_status,
      -- Payment details
      aca.check_number,
      aca.check_date                   AS payment_date,
      aca.amount                       AS payment_amount,
      -- Advance applied
      (SELECT NVL(SUM(aia2.invoice_amount),0)
       FROM   ap_invoices_all aia2
       WHERE  aia2.invoice_type_lookup_code = 'PREPAYMENT'
       AND    aia2.employee_id =
              papf.person_id
       AND    aia2.payment_status_flag = 'Y')
                                       AS advances_applied,
      -- Expense lines count
      (SELECT COUNT(*)
       FROM   ap_expense_report_lines_all aerl
       WHERE  aerl.report_header_id =
              aerh.report_header_id)   AS line_count,
      -- Policy violations
      (SELECT COUNT(*)
       FROM   ap_pol_violations_all apv
       WHERE  apv.report_header_id =
              aerh.report_header_id)   AS violation_count,
      -- Cost center
      gcc.concatenated_segments        AS default_account
        FROM
      ap_expense_report_headers_all  aerh,
      per_all_people_f               papf,
      per_all_people_f               papf_mgr,
      hr_all_organization_units      haou,
      per_all_assignments_f          paaf,
      ap_invoices_all                aia,
      ap_checks_all                  aca,
      ap_invoice_payments_all        aipa,
      gl_code_combinations_kfv       gcc
    WHERE
      aerh.employee_id              = papf.person_id
      AND paaf.person_id                = papf.person_id
      AND paaf.supervisor_id            = papf_mgr.person_id(+)
      AND paaf.organization_id          = haou.organization_id
      AND aerh.default_currency_code    = aerh.currency_code
      AND aia.employee_id(+)            = papf.person_id
      AND aia.invoice_type_lookup_code(+)
                                        = 'EXPENSE REPORT'
      AND aia.invoice_num(+)            = aerh.report_number
      AND aipa.invoice_id(+)            = aia.invoice_id
      AND aca.check_id(+)               = aipa.check_id
      AND aerh.default_expense_ccid     = gcc.code_combination_id(+)
      -- DateTrack filters
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN papf.effective_start_date
              AND papf.effective_end_date
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN papf_mgr.effective_start_date(+)
              AND papf_mgr.effective_end_date(+)
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN paaf.effective_start_date
              AND paaf.effective_end_date
      AND paaf.primary_flag             = 'Y'
      AND aerh.org_id                   = :p_org_id
      AND aerh.report_date
          BETWEEN :p_start_date AND :p_end_date
      AND aerh.expense_status_code      = NVL(
                                            :p_status,
                                            aerh.expense_status_code)
    ORDER BY
      aerh.report_date DESC,
      papf.employee_number
  Example 27: iExpense Policy Violations Query
  exampleName: Expense Policy Violations Analysis
  difficultyLevel: INTERMEDIATE
  businessPurpose: |
    Query expense policy violations
    to identify non-compliant
    expense reports for audit
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      aerh.report_number,
      aerh.report_date,
      papf.employee_number,
      papf.full_name                   AS employee_name,
      haou.name                        AS department,
      aerh.total                       AS report_total,
      aerh.expense_status_code,
      -- Violation details
      apv.violation_number,
      apv.violation_type,
      apv.violation_reason,
      apv.amount                       AS violation_amount,
      apv.limit_amount                 AS policy_limit,
      (apv.amount - apv.limit_amount)  AS over_limit_amount,
      -- Expense line details
      aerl.category_code,
      aerl.expense_date,
      aerl.amount                      AS line_amount,
      aerl.merchant_name,
      aerl.location,
      aerl.justification,
      -- Receipt status
      aerl.receipt_required_flag,
      aerl.receipt_missing_flag,
      aerl.receipt_verified_flag,
      -- Approver override
      apv.policy_shortpay_flag,
      apv.justification                AS violation_justification,
      apv.override_approver_id,
      papf_ovr.full_name               AS override_approver
    FROM
      ap_expense_report_headers_all  aerh,
      ap_pol_violations_all          apv,
      ap_expense_report_lines_all    aerl,
      per_all_people_f               papf,
      per_all_people_f               papf_ovr,
      hr_all_organization_units      haou,
      per_all_assignments_f          paaf
    WHERE
      aerh.report_header_id         = apv.report_header_id
      AND aerh.report_header_id         = aerl.report_header_id
      AND apv.distribution_line_number  = aerl.distribution_line_number
      AND aerh.employee_id              = papf.person_id
      AND apv.override_approver_id      = papf_ovr.person_id(+)
      AND paaf.person_id                = papf.person_id
      AND paaf.organization_id          = haou.organization_id
      AND paaf.primary_flag             = 'Y'
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN papf.effective_start_date
              AND papf.effective_end_date
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN papf_ovr.effective_start_date(+)
              AND papf_ovr.effective_end_date(+)
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN paaf.effective_start_date
              AND paaf.effective_end_date
      AND aerh.org_id                   = :p_org_id
      AND aerh.report_date
          BETWEEN :p_start_date AND :p_end_date
    ORDER BY
      apv.amount - apv.limit_amount DESC
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION Y — FIXED ASSETS COMPLETE KNOWLEDGE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Topic 13: Fixed Assets Complete Knowledge
knowledgeId: FA-FUNC-001
moduleCode: FA
topicName: Oracle Fixed Assets Complete Setup and Processing
topicCategory: FUNCTIONAL
difficulty: ADVANCED
learningObjectives: [
  Understand Fixed Assets setup,
  Configure asset books and categories,
  Add and retire assets,
  Process depreciation,
  Handle asset transfers and adjustments,
  Understand CIP assets,
  Process mass additions from AP,
  Query asset data,
  Troubleshoot depreciation issues,
  Understand FA and GL integration
]
keyConceptsExplained: |
  1. FIXED ASSETS SETUP
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Asset Book Types:
  - Corporate Book: GAAP depreciation
  - Tax Book: Tax depreciation
  - Budget Book: Budget planning
  Asset Book Parameters:
  - Book Name and Description
  - Set of Books / Ledger
  - Fiscal Year
  - Depreciation Calendar
  - Prorate Calendar
  - Retirement Convention
  - Current Fiscal Year
  - Last Depreciation Period
  Depreciation Methods:
  - STL: Straight Line
  - DDB: Double Declining Balance
  - SYD: Sum of Years Digits
  - ACRS: Accelerated Cost Recovery
  - MACRS: Modified ACRS
  - UNIT_PROD: Units of Production
  - NBV_RSL: Net Book Value Remaining Life
  Asset Categories:
  - Category Name
  - Capitalize Threshold
  - Life in Months
  - Depreciation Method
  - GL Accounts per category:
    * Asset Cost Account
    * Accumulated Depreciation Account
    * Depreciation Expense Account
    * Proceeds of Sale Account
    * Net Book Value Retired Account
    2. ASSET LIFECYCLE (continued)
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Step 2: Enter Asset Details (continued)
    - Asset Number (auto/manual)
    - Asset Description
    - Asset Category
    - Location
    - Assigned To (Employee/Cost Center)
    - Tag Number
    - Serial Number
    - Manufacturer
    - Model Number
    - Warranty Number
    - Warranty Expiry
  Step 3: Enter Financial Details
    - Date Placed in Service
    - Cost
    - Salvage Value
    - Life in Months
    - Depreciation Method
    - Prorate Convention:
      HALF_YEAR|FULL_MONTH|
      ACTUAL_DAYS|MID_QUARTER
  Step 4: Assign to Asset Book
    - Corporate Book (required)
    - Tax Book (optional)
    - Copy depreciation rules
  Step 5: Add Asset
    - Asset number assigned
    - Depreciation schedule created
    - GL Cost account debited
  Mass Additions from AP:
  AP Invoice for capital item
  → Create Accounting in AP
  → Run Mass Additions Create program
  → Rows appear in FA_MASS_ADDITIONS
  → Review and prepare in Fixed Assets
  → Post Mass Additions program
  → Assets created in FA
  3. DEPRECIATION PROCESSING
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Monthly Depreciation Process:
  Step 1: Open Period in FA
    - Must open before running depreciation
    - Cannot skip periods
    - Calendar must match GL calendar
  Step 2: Run Depreciation
    - Program: Depreciation
    - Parameters:
      * Book Name
      * Period Name
      * Close Period: Yes/No
  Step 3: Review Depreciation Reports
    - Depreciation Projection Report
    - Depreciation Reserve Report
    - Asset Register Report
  Step 4: Create Journal Entries
    - Program: Create Journal Entries
    - Parameters:
      * Book Name
      * Period Name
      * GL Posting: Yes/No
  Step 5: Transfer to GL
    - Journal entries transferred
    - Posted to GL automatically
  Depreciation Accounting:
  Monthly Depreciation:
  DR: Depreciation Expense Account
  CR: Accumulated Depreciation Account
  4. ASSET RETIREMENT
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  Retirement Types:
  - Full Retirement: Retire entire asset
  - Partial Retirement: Retire portion
  - Mass Retirement: Retire multiple
  Retirement Accounting:
  On Full Retirement:
  DR: Accumulated Depreciation
  DR: Proceeds of Sale (if sold)
  CR: Asset Cost Account
  CR/DR: Gain/Loss on Disposal
  Retirement Process:
  Step 1: Enter Retirement
    - Retirement Date
    - Retirement Type: SALE|SCRAPPED|STOLEN
    - Units (full or partial)
    - Proceeds of Sale
    - Cost of Removal
  Step 2: Calculate Gain/Loss
    - Net Book Value = Cost - Accum Depr
    - Gain/Loss = Proceeds - NBV - Cost of Removal
  Step 3: Run Depreciation for Period
    - Depreciation calculated to retirement date
    - Prorate based on retirement convention
  Step 4: Create Journal Entries
    - Retirement entries created
    - Transfer to GL
  5. CIP ASSETS
  ─────────────────────────────────────
  CIP = Construction in Progress
  Purpose: Track costs before asset placed in service
  CIP Process:
  Create CIP Asset
  → Add costs over time
  → When complete: Capitalize CIP
  → CIP becomes depreciable asset
  → Depreciation starts from in-service date
  CIP Accounting:
  Adding CIP Costs:
  DR: CIP Asset Account
  CR: Accounts Payable
  Capitalizing CIP:
  DR: Asset Cost Account
  CR: CIP Asset Account
sqlExamples: [
  Example 28: Fixed Assets Register Query
  exampleName: Complete Fixed Asset Register
  difficultyLevel: INTERMEDIATE
  businessPurpose: |
    Query complete fixed asset register
    showing cost, accumulated depreciation,
    net book value and depreciation details
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      fab.asset_number,
      fab.description                  AS asset_description,
      fab.tag_number,
      fab.serial_number,
      fab.manufacturer_name,
      fab.model_number,
      -- Category
      fac.segment1                     AS major_category,
      fac.segment2                     AS minor_category,
      -- Location
      fl.location_id,
      fl.segment1                      AS building,
      fl.segment2                      AS floor,
      fl.segment3                      AS room,
      -- Assignment
      papf.full_name                   AS assigned_to,
      papf.employee_number,
      haou.name                        AS department,
      -- Financial details
      fb.date_placed_in_service,
      fb.life_in_months,
      ROUND(fb.life_in_months / 12, 1) AS life_in_years,
      fb.cost                          AS asset_cost,
      fb.salvage_value,
      (fb.cost - fb.salvage_value)     AS depreciable_cost,
      -- Depreciation
      fb.method_code                   AS depr_method,
      fb.prorate_convention_code,
      fb.depreciation_amount           AS period_depr,
      fb.ytd_depreciation,
      fb.deprn_reserve                 AS accum_depreciation,
      -- Net Book Value
      (fb.cost - fb.deprn_reserve)     AS net_book_value,
      -- NBV percentage
      ROUND(
        ((fb.cost - fb.deprn_reserve) /
         NULLIF(fb.cost, 0)) * 100, 2) AS nbv_pct,
      -- Remaining life
      ROUND(
        (fb.cost - fb.salvage_value -
         fb.deprn_reserve) /
        NULLIF(fb.depreciation_amount, 0)
      )                                AS remaining_periods,
      -- Book details
      fb.book_type_code,
      fb.period_name                   AS last_depr_period,
      -- GL Accounts
      fac.asset_cost_acct              AS cost_account,
      fac.deprn_expense_acct           AS depr_expense_account,
      fac.deprn_reserve_acct           AS accum_depr_account,
      -- Status
      fab.asset_type,
      CASE fab.asset_type
        WHEN 'CAPITALIZED' THEN 'Active'
        WHEN 'CIP' THEN 'CIP - Not Depreciating'
        WHEN 'EXPENSED' THEN 'Expensed'
        ELSE fab.asset_type
      END                              AS asset_status
    FROM
      fa_assets                      fab,
      fa_books                       fb,
      fa_categories_b                fac,
      fa_locations                   fl,
      fa_asset_history               fah,
      per_all_people_f               papf,
      hr_all_organization_units      haou
    WHERE
      fab.asset_id              = fb.asset_id
      AND fab.asset_category_id     = fac.category_id
      AND fab.asset_id              = fah.asset_id
      AND fah.location_id           = fl.location_id(+)
      AND fah.assigned_to           = papf.person_id(+)
      AND fah.department_id         = haou.organization_id(+)
            AND SYSDATE BETWEEN papf.effective_start_date(+)
              AND papf.effective_end_date(+)
      AND fah.date_ineffective        IS NULL
      AND fb.book_type_code           = :p_book_name
      AND fb.transaction_header_id_out IS NULL
      AND fab.asset_type              IN ('CAPITALIZED','CIP')
      AND fab.asset_number            = NVL(
                                          :p_asset_number,
                                          fab.asset_number)
    ORDER BY
      fac.segment1,
      fac.segment2,
      fab.asset_number
  Example 29: FA Depreciation Projection Query
  exampleName: Fixed Asset Depreciation Projection
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Project future depreciation for
    budget planning and financial
    forecasting purposes
  sqlCode: |
    SELECT
      fab.asset_number,
      fab.description,
      fac.segment1                     AS category,
      fb.book_type_code,
      fb.cost                          AS asset_cost,
      fb.deprn_reserve                 AS accum_depr_to_date,
      (fb.cost - fb.deprn_reserve)     AS current_nbv,
      fb.depreciation_amount           AS current_period_depr,
      fb.life_in_months,
      fb.method_code,
      -- Remaining depreciation months
      ROUND(
        (fb.cost - fb.salvage_value -
         fb.deprn_reserve) /
        NULLIF(fb.depreciation_amount, 0)
      )                                AS remaining_months,
      -- Projected depreciation by year
      ROUND(fb.depreciation_amount * 12)
                                       AS annual_depr_yr1,
      ROUND(
        CASE
          WHEN fb.method_code = 'STL'
          THEN fb.depreciation_amount * 12
          WHEN fb.method_code = 'DDB'
          THEN ((fb.cost - fb.deprn_reserve) -
                (fb.depreciation_amount * 12)) *
               (2 / (fb.life_in_months / 12))
          ELSE fb.depreciation_amount * 12
        END
      )                                AS annual_depr_yr2,
      -- Projected end date
      ADD_MONTHS(
        fb.date_placed_in_service,
        fb.life_in_months
      )                                AS fully_depreciated_date,
      -- Already fully depreciated check
      CASE
        WHEN (fb.cost - fb.deprn_reserve)
             <= fb.salvage_value
        THEN 'YES'
        ELSE 'NO'
      END                              AS fully_depreciated,
      -- Asset details
      fl.segment1                      AS location,
      haou.name                        AS department,
      papf.full_name                   AS assigned_to
    FROM
      fa_assets                      fab,
      fa_books                       fb,
      fa_categories_b                fac,
      fa_locations                   fl,
      fa_asset_history               fah,
      per_all_people_f               papf,
      hr_all_organization_units      haou
    WHERE
      fab.asset_id              = fb.asset_id
      AND fab.asset_category_id     = fac.category_id
      AND fab.asset_id              = fah.asset_id
      AND fah.location_id           = fl.location_id(+)
      AND fah.assigned_to           = papf.person_id(+)
      AND fah.department_id         = haou.organization_id(+)
      AND SYSDATE BETWEEN papf.effective_start_date(+)
              AND papf.effective_end_date(+)
      AND fah.date_ineffective      IS NULL
      AND fb.book_type_code         = :p_book_name
      AND fb.transaction_header_id_out IS NULL
      AND fab.asset_type            = 'CAPITALIZED'
      AND (fb.cost - fb.deprn_reserve)
                                    > fb.salvage_value
    ORDER BY
      fac.segment1,
      fab.asset_number
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION Z — ADVANCED PL/SQL PATTERNS
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Topic 14: Advanced PL/SQL for Oracle EBS
knowledgeId: PLSQL-ADV-001
moduleCode: TECH
topicName: Advanced PL/SQL Patterns for Oracle EBS
topicCategory: TECHNICAL
difficulty: EXPERT
learningObjectives: [
  Master bulk collect and forall patterns,
  Implement error handling best practices,
  Use autonomous transactions correctly,
  Implement dynamic SQL safely,
  Use pipelined functions for reporting,
  Implement parallel processing patterns,
  Use UTL_FILE for file processing,
  Implement DBMS_SCHEDULER jobs,
  Use advanced cursor techniques,
  Implement retry logic and recovery
]
plsqlExamples: [
  Example 30: Bulk Collect and FORALL Pattern
  exampleName: High Performance Bulk Processing
  plsqlType: BULK_COLLECT
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Process large volumes of AP invoices
    using bulk collect and FORALL for
    maximum performance in EBS
  plsqlCode: |
    CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE
      xxcust_bulk_invoice_processor(
        p_org_id        IN  NUMBER,
        p_start_date    IN  DATE,
        p_end_date      IN  DATE,
        p_batch_size    IN  NUMBER DEFAULT 1000,
        p_processed_cnt OUT NUMBER,
        p_error_cnt     OUT NUMBER
    ) AS
    /*
    || Procedure : xxcust_bulk_invoice_processor
    || Purpose   : High-performance bulk processing
    ||             of AP invoices using BULK COLLECT
    || Author    : IBM Consulting
    || Created   : 2025
    */
      -- Custom type definitions
      TYPE t_invoice_id_tab  IS TABLE OF NUMBER
        INDEX BY PLS_INTEGER;
      TYPE t_vendor_id_tab   IS TABLE OF NUMBER
        INDEX BY PLS_INTEGER;
      TYPE t_amount_tab      IS TABLE OF NUMBER
        INDEX BY PLS_INTEGER;
      TYPE t_status_tab      IS TABLE OF VARCHAR2(25)
        INDEX BY PLS_INTEGER;
      TYPE t_error_tab       IS TABLE OF VARCHAR2(4000)
        INDEX BY PLS_INTEGER;
      -- Collection variables
      l_invoice_ids    t_invoice_id_tab;
      l_vendor_ids     t_vendor_id_tab;
      l_amounts        t_amount_tab;
      l_statuses       t_status_tab;
      l_errors         t_error_tab;
      -- Control variables
      l_processed      NUMBER := 0;
      l_error_count    NUMBER := 0;
      l_batch_count    NUMBER := 0;
      -- Cursor for invoice processing
      CURSOR c_invoices IS
        SELECT aia.invoice_id,
               aia.vendor_id,
               aia.invoice_amount
        FROM   ap_invoices_all aia
        WHERE  aia.org_id = p_org_id
        AND    aia.invoice_date
               BETWEEN p_start_date
               AND p_end_date
        AND    aia.approval_status = 'NEEDS REAPPROVAL'
        AND    aia.cancelled_date IS NULL
        ORDER BY aia.invoice_id;
        BEGIN
      -- Initialize counters
      p_processed_cnt := 0;
      p_error_cnt     := 0;
      l_processed     := 0;
      l_error_count   := 0;
      OPEN c_invoices;
      LOOP
        -- Bulk fetch in batches
        FETCH c_invoices
          BULK COLLECT INTO
            l_invoice_ids,
            l_vendor_ids,
            l_amounts
          LIMIT p_batch_size;
        EXIT WHEN l_invoice_ids.COUNT = 0;
        l_batch_count := l_batch_count + 1;
        -- Initialize error collection
        l_errors.DELETE;
        -- Process each record in batch
        FOR i IN 1..l_invoice_ids.COUNT LOOP
          BEGIN
            -- Custom processing logic
            xxcust_ap_utils_pkg.validate_invoice(
              p_invoice_id => l_invoice_ids(i),
              p_vendor_id  => l_vendor_ids(i),
              p_amount     => l_amounts(i)
            );
            l_statuses(i) := 'PROCESSED';
            l_processed := l_processed + 1;
          EXCEPTION
            WHEN OTHERS THEN
              l_statuses(i) := 'ERROR';
              l_errors(i)   := SQLERRM;
              l_error_count := l_error_count + 1;
          END;
        END LOOP;
        -- Bulk update using FORALL
        FORALL i IN 1..l_invoice_ids.COUNT
          SAVE EXCEPTIONS
          UPDATE xxcust_invoice_process_log
          SET    process_status  = l_statuses(i),
                 process_date    = SYSDATE,
                 error_message   = l_errors(i)
          WHERE  invoice_id      = l_invoice_ids(i);
        -- Commit after each batch
        COMMIT;
        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE(
          'Batch ' || l_batch_count ||
          ' processed: ' || l_invoice_ids.COUNT ||
          ' records. Running total: ' || l_processed
        );
        -- Clear collections for next batch
        l_invoice_ids.DELETE;
        l_vendor_ids.DELETE;
        l_amounts.DELETE;
        l_statuses.DELETE;
        EXIT WHEN c_invoices%NOTFOUND;
      END LOOP;
      CLOSE c_invoices;
      -- Set output parameters
      p_processed_cnt := l_processed;
      p_error_cnt     := l_error_count;
      DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE(
        'Processing complete. ' ||
        'Processed: ' || l_processed ||
        ' Errors: ' || l_error_count
      );
    EXCEPTION
      WHEN OTHERS THEN
        IF c_invoices%ISOPEN THEN
          CLOSE c_invoices;
        END IF;
        ROLLBACK;
        p_error_cnt := p_error_cnt + 1;
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
          -20001,
          'Bulk processor failed: ' || SQLERRM
        );
    END xxcust_bulk_invoice_processor;
    /
  Example 31: Dynamic SQL Pattern
  exampleName: Safe Dynamic SQL for EBS Reports
  plsqlType: DYNAMIC_SQL
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Generate dynamic reports for any
    EBS module using safe bind variable
    dynamic SQL with injection prevention
  plsqlCode: |
    CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE
      xxcust_dynamic_report_pkg(
        p_module        IN  VARCHAR2,
        p_start_date    IN  DATE,
        p_end_date      IN  DATE,
        p_org_id        IN  NUMBER,
        p_extra_filter  IN  VARCHAR2 DEFAULT NULL,
        p_result        OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
    ) AS
    /*
    || Procedure : xxcust_dynamic_report_pkg
    || Purpose   : Safe dynamic SQL report generator
    || Security  : Uses bind variables to prevent injection
    */
      l_sql           VARCHAR2(32767);
      l_module_upper  VARCHAR2(10);
      l_valid_modules VARCHAR2(200) :=
        'AP,AR,GL,PO,INV,HRMS,CM,FA,iEXP';
    BEGIN
      -- Validate module input (injection prevention)
      l_module_upper := UPPER(TRIM(p_module));
      IF INSTR(',' || l_valid_modules || ',',
               ',' || l_module_upper || ',') = 0
      THEN
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
          -20001,
          'Invalid module: ' || p_module
        );
      END IF;
      -- Validate extra filter (whitelist approach)
      IF p_extra_filter IS NOT NULL THEN
        -- Only allow safe characters
        IF REGEXP_LIKE(p_extra_filter,
                       '[^A-Za-z0-9_\s=<>!,''.]') THEN
          RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
            -20002,
            'Invalid filter characters detected'
          );
        END IF;
      END IF;
      -- Build dynamic SQL based on module
      CASE l_module_upper
        WHEN 'AP' THEN
          l_sql :=
            'SELECT aia.invoice_num, ' ||
            '       aia.invoice_date, ' ||
            '       aia.invoice_amount, ' ||
            '       pv.vendor_name, ' ||
            '       aia.approval_status, ' ||
            '       aia.payment_status_flag ' ||
            'FROM   ap_invoices_all aia, ' ||
            '       po_vendors pv ' ||
            'WHERE  aia.vendor_id = pv.vendor_id ' ||
            'AND    aia.org_id = :p_org_id ' ||
            'AND    aia.invoice_date ' ||
            '       BETWEEN :p_start_date ' ||
            '       AND :p_end_date ' ||
            'AND    aia.cancelled_date IS NULL ';
        WHEN 'AR' THEN
          l_sql :=
            'SELECT rct.trx_number, ' ||
            '       rct.trx_date, ' ||
            '       rct.amount_due_original, ' ||
            '       rct.amount_due_remaining, ' ||
            '       hp.party_name AS customer_name, ' ||
            '       rct.status_trx ' ||
            'FROM   ra_customer_trx_all rct, ' ||
            '       hz_cust_accounts hca, ' ||
            '       hz_parties hp ' ||
            'WHERE  rct.bill_to_customer_id ' ||
            '       = hca.cust_account_id ' ||
            'AND    hca.party_id = hp.party_id ' ||
            'AND    rct.org_id = :p_org_id ' ||
            'AND    rct.trx_date ' ||
            '       BETWEEN :p_start_date ' ||
            '       AND :p_end_date ';
                WHEN 'GL' THEN
          l_sql :=
            'SELECT gjh.name AS journal_name, ' ||
            '       gjh.period_name, ' ||
            '       gjh.je_source, ' ||
            '       gjh.je_category, ' ||
            '       gjh.running_total_dr, ' ||
            '       gjh.running_total_cr, ' ||
            '       gjh.status, ' ||
            '       gjh.posted_date ' ||
            'FROM   gl_je_headers gjh ' ||
            'WHERE  gjh.ledger_id = :p_org_id ' ||
            'AND    gjh.default_effective_date ' ||
            '       BETWEEN :p_start_date ' ||
            '       AND :p_end_date ' ||
            'AND    gjh.status = ''P'' ';
        WHEN 'PO' THEN
          l_sql :=
            'SELECT pha.segment1 AS po_number, ' ||
            '       pha.creation_date, ' ||
            '       pha.authorization_status, ' ||
            '       pv.vendor_name, ' ||
            '       pha.currency_code, ' ||
            '       pha.approved_date ' ||
            'FROM   po_headers_all pha, ' ||
            '       po_vendors pv ' ||
            'WHERE  pha.vendor_id = pv.vendor_id ' ||
            'AND    pha.org_id = :p_org_id ' ||
            'AND    pha.creation_date ' ||
            '       BETWEEN :p_start_date ' ||
            '       AND :p_end_date ' ||
            'AND    pha.type_lookup_code = ''STANDARD'' ';
        WHEN 'INV' THEN
          l_sql :=
            'SELECT msib.segment1 AS item_number, ' ||
            '       msib.description, ' ||
            '       moq.subinventory_code, ' ||
            '       moq.primary_transaction_quantity ' ||
            '         AS on_hand_qty, ' ||
            '       cic.item_cost ' ||
            'FROM   mtl_onhand_quantities_detail moq, ' ||
            '       mtl_system_items_b msib, ' ||
            '       cst_item_costs cic ' ||
            'WHERE  moq.inventory_item_id ' ||
            '       = msib.inventory_item_id ' ||
            'AND    moq.organization_id ' ||
            '       = msib.organization_id ' ||
            'AND    cic.inventory_item_id ' ||
            '       = moq.inventory_item_id ' ||
            'AND    cic.organization_id ' ||
            '       = moq.organization_id ' ||
            'AND    cic.cost_type_id = 1 ' ||
            'AND    moq.organization_id = :p_org_id ' ||
            'AND    SYSDATE ' ||
            '       BETWEEN :p_start_date ' ||
            '       AND :p_end_date ' ||
            'AND    moq.primary_transaction_quantity > 0 ';
        WHEN 'HRMS' THEN
          l_sql :=
            'SELECT papf.employee_number, ' ||
            '       papf.full_name, ' ||
            '       haou.name AS department, ' ||
            '       pj.name AS job_name, ' ||
            '       paaf.employment_category, ' ||
            '       ppos.date_start AS hire_date ' ||
            'FROM   per_all_people_f papf, ' ||
            '       per_all_assignments_f paaf, ' ||
            '       hr_all_organization_units haou, ' ||
            '       per_jobs pj, ' ||
            '       per_periods_of_service ppos ' ||
            'WHERE  paaf.person_id = papf.person_id ' ||
            'AND    paaf.organization_id ' ||
            '       = haou.organization_id ' ||
            'AND    paaf.job_id = pj.job_id(+) ' ||
            'AND    ppos.person_id = papf.person_id ' ||
            'AND    :p_org_id = papf.business_group_id ' ||
            'AND    SYSDATE BETWEEN ' ||
            '       papf.effective_start_date ' ||
            '       AND papf.effective_end_date ' ||
            'AND    SYSDATE BETWEEN ' ||
            '       paaf.effective_start_date ' ||
            '       AND paaf.effective_end_date ' ||
            'AND    paaf.primary_flag = ''Y'' ' ||
            'AND    ppos.date_start ' ||
            '       BETWEEN :p_start_date ' ||
            '       AND :p_end_date ';
        ELSE
          RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
            -20003,
            'Module not configured: ' || p_module
          );
      END CASE;
      -- Append extra filter if provided
      IF p_extra_filter IS NOT NULL THEN
        l_sql := l_sql || ' AND ' || p_extra_filter;
      END IF;
      -- Add ORDER BY
      l_sql := l_sql || ' ORDER BY 1';
      -- Open ref cursor with bind variables
      OPEN p_result FOR l_sql
        USING p_org_id, p_start_date, p_end_date;
    EXCEPTION
      WHEN OTHERS THEN
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
          -20099,
          'Dynamic report error: ' || SQLERRM
        );
    END xxcust_dynamic_report_pkg;
    /
  Example 32: Pipelined Function for EBS Reporting
  exampleName: Pipelined Function for Invoice Analytics
  plsqlType: PIPELINED_FUNCTION
  difficultyLevel: EXPERT
  businessPurpose: |
    Create pipelined function to stream
    large AP invoice datasets for
    analytics and reporting without
    memory constraints
  plsqlCode: |
    -- Type definitions (in package spec)
    CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE xxcust_pipeline_pkg AS
      -- Row type for invoice analytics
      TYPE t_invoice_analytics_row IS RECORD (
        invoice_num        VARCHAR2(50),
        vendor_name        VARCHAR2(240),
        invoice_date       DATE,
        invoice_amount     NUMBER,
        amount_due         NUMBER,
        days_outstanding   NUMBER,
        aging_bucket       VARCHAR2(30),
        approval_status    VARCHAR2(25),
        payment_status     VARCHAR2(1),
        risk_score         NUMBER,
        risk_category      VARCHAR2(20)
      );
      -- Table type for pipelining
      TYPE t_invoice_analytics_tab IS TABLE OF
        t_invoice_analytics_row;
      -- Pipelined function declaration
      FUNCTION get_invoice_analytics(
        p_org_id     IN NUMBER,
        p_start_date IN DATE,
        p_end_date   IN DATE
      ) RETURN t_invoice_analytics_tab
        PIPELINED;
      -- Risk scoring function
      FUNCTION calculate_risk_score(
        p_invoice_amount   IN NUMBER,
        p_days_outstanding IN NUMBER,
        p_hold_count       IN NUMBER,
        p_vendor_history   IN NUMBER
      ) RETURN NUMBER;
    END xxcust_pipeline_pkg;
    /
    CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY
      xxcust_pipeline_pkg AS
            FUNCTION calculate_risk_score(
        p_invoice_amount   IN NUMBER,
        p_days_outstanding IN NUMBER,
        p_hold_count       IN NUMBER,
        p_vendor_history   IN NUMBER
      ) RETURN NUMBER IS
        l_score NUMBER := 0;
      BEGIN
        -- Amount-based risk scoring
        IF p_invoice_amount >= 1000000 THEN
          l_score := l_score + 40;
        ELSIF p_invoice_amount >= 100000 THEN
          l_score := l_score + 25;
        ELSIF p_invoice_amount >= 50000 THEN
          l_score := l_score + 15;
        ELSIF p_invoice_amount >= 10000 THEN
          l_score := l_score + 5;
        END IF;
        -- Days outstanding risk scoring
        IF p_days_outstanding >= 90 THEN
          l_score := l_score + 30;
        ELSIF p_days_outstanding >= 60 THEN
          l_score := l_score + 20;
        ELSIF p_days_outstanding >= 30 THEN
          l_score := l_score + 10;
        ELSIF p_days_outstanding >= 15 THEN
          l_score := l_score + 5;
        END IF;
        -- Hold count risk scoring
        IF p_hold_count >= 5 THEN
          l_score := l_score + 20;
        ELSIF p_hold_count >= 3 THEN
          l_score := l_score + 10;
        ELSIF p_hold_count >= 1 THEN
          l_score := l_score + 5;
        END IF;
        -- Vendor history risk scoring
        IF p_vendor_history >= 10 THEN
          l_score := l_score + 10;
        ELSIF p_vendor_history >= 5 THEN
          l_score := l_score + 5;
        END IF;
        RETURN LEAST(l_score, 100);
      END calculate_risk_score;
      FUNCTION get_invoice_analytics(
        p_org_id     IN NUMBER,
        p_start_date IN DATE,
        p_end_date   IN DATE
      ) RETURN t_invoice_analytics_tab
        PIPELINED IS
        -- Output row variable
        l_row t_invoice_analytics_row;
        -- Cursor for invoice data
        CURSOR c_invoices IS
          SELECT
            aia.invoice_num,
            aia.invoice_date,
            aia.invoice_amount,
            (aia.invoice_amount -
             NVL(aia.amount_paid,0))   AS amount_due,
            aia.approval_status,
            aia.payment_status_flag,
            pv.vendor_name,
            -- Days outstanding
            TRUNC(SYSDATE) -
            TRUNC(aia.invoice_date)    AS days_outstanding,
            -- Hold count
            (SELECT COUNT(*)
             FROM   ap_holds_all ah
             WHERE  ah.invoice_id = aia.invoice_id
             AND    ah.release_lookup_code IS NULL)
                                       AS hold_count,
            -- Vendor invoice history
            (SELECT COUNT(*)
             FROM   ap_invoices_all aia2
             WHERE  aia2.vendor_id = aia.vendor_id
             AND    aia2.org_id = aia.org_id
             AND    aia2.invoice_date
                    >= ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,-12)
             AND    aia2.cancelled_date IS NULL)
                                       AS vendor_history
          FROM
            ap_invoices_all  aia,
            po_vendors       pv
          WHERE
            aia.vendor_id       = pv.vendor_id
            AND aia.org_id          = p_org_id
            AND aia.invoice_date
                BETWEEN p_start_date AND p_end_date
            AND aia.cancelled_date  IS NULL
          ORDER BY
            aia.invoice_amount DESC;
      BEGIN
        FOR rec IN c_invoices LOOP
          -- Populate output row
          l_row.invoice_num      := rec.invoice_num;
          l_row.vendor_name      := rec.vendor_name;
          l_row.invoice_date     := rec.invoice_date;
          l_row.invoice_amount   := rec.invoice_amount;
          l_row.amount_due       := rec.amount_due;
          l_row.days_outstanding := rec.days_outstanding;
          l_row.approval_status  := rec.approval_status;
          l_row.payment_status   := rec.payment_status_flag;
          -- Calculate aging bucket
          l_row.aging_bucket :=
            CASE
              WHEN rec.days_outstanding <= 0
              THEN 'CURRENT'
              WHEN rec.days_outstanding <= 30
              THEN '1-30 DAYS'
              WHEN rec.days_outstanding <= 60
              THEN '31-60 DAYS'
              WHEN rec.days_outstanding <= 90
              THEN '61-90 DAYS'
              ELSE 'OVER 90 DAYS'
            END;
          -- Calculate risk score
          l_row.risk_score :=
            calculate_risk_score(
              p_invoice_amount   => rec.invoice_amount,
              p_days_outstanding => rec.days_outstanding,
              p_hold_count       => rec.hold_count,
              p_vendor_history   => rec.vendor_history
            );
          -- Assign risk category
          l_row.risk_category :=
            CASE
              WHEN l_row.risk_score >= 70
              THEN 'HIGH RISK'
              WHEN l_row.risk_score >= 40
              THEN 'MEDIUM RISK'
              WHEN l_row.risk_score >= 20
              THEN 'LOW RISK'
              ELSE 'MINIMAL RISK'
            END;
          -- Pipe the row
          PIPE ROW(l_row);
        END LOOP;
        RETURN;
      EXCEPTION
        WHEN OTHERS THEN
          RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
            -20001,
            'Pipeline error: ' || SQLERRM
          );
      END get_invoice_analytics;
    END xxcust_pipeline_pkg;
    /
    -- Usage: Query pipelined function like a table
    SELECT *
    FROM   TABLE(
             xxcust_pipeline_pkg.get_invoice_analytics(
               p_org_id     => 101,
               p_start_date => TRUNC(SYSDATE,'YYYY'),
               p_end_date   => SYSDATE
             )
           )
    WHERE  risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'
    ORDER BY risk_score DESC;
  Example 33: DBMS_SCHEDULER Job
  exampleName: Automated EBS Data Processing Job
  plsqlType: DBMS_SCHEDULER
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Create automated DBMS_SCHEDULER job
    for nightly EBS data processing
    with error notification and retry logic
  plsqlCode: |
    -- Create job program
    BEGIN
      DBMS_SCHEDULER.CREATE_PROGRAM(
        program_name        =>
          'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_AP_PROCESS_PRG',
        program_type        => 'STORED_PROCEDURE',
        program_action      =>
          'xxcust_nightly_process_pkg.run_ap_processing',
        number_of_arguments => 3,
        enabled             => FALSE,
        comments            =>
          'Nightly AP Invoice Processing Program'
      );
            -- Define program arguments
      DBMS_SCHEDULER.DEFINE_PROGRAM_ARGUMENT(
        program_name      =>
          'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_AP_PROCESS_PRG',
        argument_position => 1,
        argument_name     => 'P_ORG_ID',
        argument_type     => 'NUMBER',
        default_value     => '101'
      );
      DBMS_SCHEDULER.DEFINE_PROGRAM_ARGUMENT(
        program_name      =>
          'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_AP_PROCESS_PRG',
        argument_position => 2,
        argument_name     => 'P_START_DATE',
        argument_type     => 'VARCHAR2',
        default_value     => 'TRUNC(SYSDATE-1)'
      );
      DBMS_SCHEDULER.DEFINE_PROGRAM_ARGUMENT(
        program_name      =>
          'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_AP_PROCESS_PRG',
        argument_position => 3,
        argument_name     => 'P_END_DATE',
        argument_type     => 'VARCHAR2',
        default_value     => 'TRUNC(SYSDATE)'
      );
      -- Enable program
      DBMS_SCHEDULER.ENABLE(
        'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_AP_PROCESS_PRG'
      );
    END;
    /
    -- Create schedule
    BEGIN
      DBMS_SCHEDULER.CREATE_SCHEDULE(
        schedule_name   =>
          'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_SCHEDULE',
        start_date      =>
          SYSTIMESTAMP AT TIME ZONE 'US/Eastern',
        repeat_interval =>
          'FREQ=DAILY;BYHOUR=22;BYMINUTE=0;BYSECOND=0',
        end_date        => NULL,
        comments        =>
          'Daily at 10 PM Eastern Time'
      );
    END;
    /
    -- Create job
    BEGIN
      DBMS_SCHEDULER.CREATE_JOB(
        job_name        =>
          'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_AP_PROCESS_JOB',
        program_name    =>
          'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_AP_PROCESS_PRG',
        schedule_name   =>
          'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_SCHEDULE',
        enabled         => FALSE,
        auto_drop       => FALSE,
        restartable     => TRUE,
        comments        =>
          'Nightly AP Invoice Processing Job',
        job_class       => 'DEFAULT_JOB_CLASS'
      );
      -- Set job attributes
      DBMS_SCHEDULER.SET_ATTRIBUTE(
        name      =>
          'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_AP_PROCESS_JOB',
        attribute => 'MAX_FAILURES',
        value     => 3
      );
      DBMS_SCHEDULER.SET_ATTRIBUTE(
        name      =>
          'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_AP_PROCESS_JOB',
        attribute => 'MAX_RUN_DURATION',
        value     =>
          INTERVAL '4' HOUR
      );
      DBMS_SCHEDULER.SET_ATTRIBUTE(
        name      =>
          'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_AP_PROCESS_JOB',
        attribute => 'RESTARTABLE',
        value     => TRUE
      );
      -- Enable the job
      DBMS_SCHEDULER.ENABLE(
        'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_AP_PROCESS_JOB'
      );
    END;
    /
    -- Monitor job execution
    SELECT
      job_name,
      status,
      actual_start_date,
      run_duration,
      cpu_used,
      error#,
      additional_info
    FROM
      user_scheduler_job_run_details
    WHERE
      job_name = 'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_AP_PROCESS_JOB'
    ORDER BY
      actual_start_date DESC;
    -- Check job status
    SELECT
      job_name,
      state,
      last_start_date,
      last_run_duration,
      next_run_date,
      failure_count,
      max_failures,
      enabled
    FROM
      user_scheduler_jobs
    WHERE
      job_name = 'XXCUST_NIGHTLY_AP_PROCESS_JOB';
  Example 34: UTL_FILE for EBS File Processing
  exampleName: UTL_FILE Inbound Interface Processor
  plsqlType: UTL_FILE
  difficultyLevel: ADVANCED
  businessPurpose: |
    Process inbound interface files
    from external systems into EBS
    using UTL_FILE with full error
    handling and audit trail
  plsqlCode: |
    CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE
      xxcust_file_processor(
        p_directory    IN  VARCHAR2,
        p_filename     IN  VARCHAR2,
        p_org_id       IN  NUMBER,
        p_batch_id     OUT VARCHAR2,
        p_records_read OUT NUMBER,
        p_records_ok   OUT NUMBER,
        p_records_err  OUT NUMBER
    ) AS
    /*
    || Procedure : xxcust_file_processor
    || Purpose   : Process inbound CSV interface file
    || Format    : VENDOR_NUM|INVOICE_NUM|DATE|
    ||             AMOUNT|DESCRIPTION|ACCOUNT
    */
      -- File handling variables
      l_file_handle    UTL_FILE.FILE_TYPE;
      l_line           VARCHAR2(32767);
      l_line_num       NUMBER := 0;
      l_fields         APEX_APPLICATION_GLOBAL.VC_ARR2;
      -- Processing variables
      l_batch_id       VARCHAR2(50);
      l_vendor_id      NUMBER;
      l_vendor_site_id NUMBER;
      l_invoice_date   DATE;
      l_amount         NUMBER;
      l_ccid           NUMBER;
      l_error_msg      VARCHAR2(4000);
      l_records_read   NUMBER := 0;
      l_records_ok     NUMBER := 0;
      l_records_err    NUMBER := 0;
      -- Log file handle
      l_log_handle     UTL_FILE.FILE_TYPE;
      l_log_filename   VARCHAR2(100);
    BEGIN
      -- Generate batch ID
      l_batch_id := 'XXCUST_' ||
        TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'YYYYMMDD_HH24MISS');
      -- Open log file
      l_log_filename := REPLACE(
        p_filename, '.csv', '_' ||
        TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'YYYYMMDD_HH24MISS') ||
        '.log'
      );
      l_log_handle := UTL_FILE.FOPEN(
        location => p_directory,
        filename => l_log_filename,
        open_mode => 'W',
        max_linesize => 32767
      );
      UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(
        l_log_handle,
        'Processing started: ' ||
        TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD-MON-YYYY HH24:MI:SS')
      );
      UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(
        l_log_handle,
        'File: ' || p_filename ||
        ' | Batch: ' || l_batch_id
      );
      -- Open input file
      l_file_handle := UTL_FILE.FOPEN(
        location     => p_directory,
        filename     => p_filename,
        open_mode    => 'R',
        max_linesize => 32767
      );
      -- Process each line
      LOOP
        BEGIN
                    -- Read line
          UTL_FILE.GET_LINE(
            l_file_handle,
            l_line
          );
          l_line_num := l_line_num + 1;
          -- Skip header line
          IF l_line_num = 1 THEN
            UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(
              l_log_handle,
              'Header line skipped: ' || l_line
            );
            CONTINUE;
          END IF;
          -- Skip blank lines
          IF TRIM(l_line) IS NULL THEN
            CONTINUE;
          END IF;
          l_records_read := l_records_read + 1;
          l_error_msg    := NULL;
          BEGIN
            -- Parse CSV fields (pipe delimited)
            l_fields := APEX_UTIL.STRING_TO_TABLE(
              p_string    => l_line,
              p_separator => '|'
            );
            -- Validate field count
            IF l_fields.COUNT < 6 THEN
              RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
                -20001,
                'Invalid field count: ' ||
                l_fields.COUNT ||
                ' expected 6'
              );
            END IF;
            -- Get vendor ID
            BEGIN
              SELECT vendor_id
              INTO   l_vendor_id
              FROM   po_vendors
              WHERE  segment1 = TRIM(l_fields(1))
              AND    ROWNUM = 1;
            EXCEPTION
              WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
                RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
                  -20002,
                  'Vendor not found: ' ||
                  TRIM(l_fields(1))
                );
            END;
            -- Get vendor site ID
            BEGIN
              SELECT vendor_site_id
              INTO   l_vendor_site_id
              FROM   po_vendor_sites_all
              WHERE  vendor_id = l_vendor_id
              AND    org_id    = p_org_id
              AND    primary_pay_site_flag = 'Y'
              AND    ROWNUM = 1;
            EXCEPTION
              WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
                RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
                  -20003,
                  'Vendor site not found for: ' ||
                  TRIM(l_fields(1))
                );
            END;
            -- Parse date
            BEGIN
              l_invoice_date := TO_DATE(
                TRIM(l_fields(3)),
                'DD/MM/YYYY'
              );
            EXCEPTION
              WHEN OTHERS THEN
                RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
                  -20004,
                  'Invalid date format: ' ||
                  TRIM(l_fields(3)) ||
                  ' expected DD/MM/YYYY'
                );
            END;
            -- Parse amount
            BEGIN
              l_amount := TO_NUMBER(
                TRIM(l_fields(4)),
                '999999999.99'
              );
              IF l_amount <= 0 THEN
                RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
                  -20005,
                  'Amount must be positive: ' ||
                  TRIM(l_fields(4))
                );
              END IF;
            EXCEPTION
              WHEN VALUE_ERROR THEN
                RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
                  -20006,
                  'Invalid amount: ' ||
                  TRIM(l_fields(4))
                );
            END;
            -- Get CCID from account string
            BEGIN
              SELECT code_combination_id
              INTO   l_ccid
              FROM   gl_code_combinations
              WHERE  concatenated_segments =
                     TRIM(l_fields(6))
              AND    enabled_flag = 'Y'
              AND    ROWNUM = 1;
            EXCEPTION
              WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
                RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
                  -20007,
                  'Invalid GL account: ' ||
                  TRIM(l_fields(6))
                );
            END;
            -- Insert to staging table
            INSERT INTO xxcust_ap_inv_stg (
              batch_id,
              line_number,
              vendor_num,
              vendor_id,
              vendor_site_id,
              invoice_num,
              invoice_date,
              invoice_amount,
              description,
              account_string,
              ccid,
              org_id,
              status,
              creation_date,
              created_by
            ) VALUES (
              l_batch_id,
              l_line_num,
              TRIM(l_fields(1)),
              l_vendor_id,
              l_vendor_site_id,
              TRIM(l_fields(2)),
              l_invoice_date,
              l_amount,
              TRIM(l_fields(5)),
              TRIM(l_fields(6)),
              l_ccid,
              p_org_id,
              'VALIDATED',
              SYSDATE,
              FND_GLOBAL.USER_ID
            );
            l_records_ok := l_records_ok + 1;
            UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(
              l_log_handle,
              'Line ' || l_line_num ||
              ' OK: Invoice ' ||
              TRIM(l_fields(2))
            );
          EXCEPTION
            WHEN OTHERS THEN
              l_error_msg    := SQLERRM;
              l_records_err  := l_records_err + 1;
              -- Insert error record
              INSERT INTO xxcust_ap_inv_stg (
                batch_id,
                line_number,
                vendor_num,
                invoice_num,
                invoice_amount,
                org_id,
                status,
                error_message,
                creation_date,
                created_by
              ) VALUES (
                l_batch_id,
                l_line_num,
                TRIM(l_fields(1)),
                TRIM(l_fields(2)),
                l_amount,
                p_org_id,
                'ERROR',
                l_error_msg,
                SYSDATE,
                FND_GLOBAL.USER_ID
              );
              UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(
                l_log_handle,
                'Line ' || l_line_num ||
                ' ERROR: ' || l_error_msg
              );
          END;
        EXCEPTION
          WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
            -- End of file
            EXIT;
          WHEN UTL_FILE.INVALID_FILEHANDLE THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
              -20010,
              'Invalid file handle'
            );
          WHEN UTL_FILE.READ_ERROR THEN
            RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
              -20011,
              'File read error at line: ' ||
              l_line_num
            );
        END;
      END LOOP;
      -- Close input file
      UTL_FILE.FCLOSE(l_file_handle);
      -- Commit staging data
      COMMIT;
      -- Write summary to log
      UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(
        l_log_handle,
        '─────────────────────────────'
      );
      UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(
        l_log_handle,
        'Processing Summary:'
      );
      UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(
        l_log_handle,
        'Total Records Read : ' || l_records_read
      );
      UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(
        l_log_handle,
        'Records Processed  : ' || l_records_ok
      );
      UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(
        l_log_handle,
        'Records with Errors: ' || l_records_err
      );
      UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(
        l_log_handle,
        'Batch ID           : ' || l_batch_id
      );
            UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(
        l_log_handle,
        'Processing completed: ' ||
        TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD-MON-YYYY HH24:MI:SS')
      );
      -- Close log file
      UTL_FILE.FCLOSE(l_log_handle);
      -- Set output parameters
      p_batch_id     := l_batch_id;
      p_records_read := l_records_read;
      p_records_ok   := l_records_ok;
      p_records_err  := l_records_err;
      -- Send notification email
      xxcust_notify_pkg.send_process_notification(
        p_subject    => 'AP Invoice File Processing Complete',
        p_message    => 'Batch: ' || l_batch_id ||
                        CHR(10) ||
                        'Records Read: ' || l_records_read ||
                        CHR(10) ||
                        'Success: ' || l_records_ok ||
                        CHR(10) ||
                        'Errors: ' || l_records_err,
        p_recipient  => 'ap.team@company.com'
      );
    EXCEPTION
      WHEN UTL_FILE.INVALID_PATH THEN
        IF UTL_FILE.IS_OPEN(l_file_handle) THEN
          UTL_FILE.FCLOSE(l_file_handle);
        END IF;
        IF UTL_FILE.IS_OPEN(l_log_handle) THEN
          UTL_FILE.FCLOSE(l_log_handle);
        END IF;
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
          -20020,
          'Invalid directory: ' || p_directory
        );
      WHEN UTL_FILE.INVALID_FILENAME THEN
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
          -20021,
          'Invalid filename: ' || p_filename
        );
      WHEN OTHERS THEN
        IF UTL_FILE.IS_OPEN(l_file_handle) THEN
          UTL_FILE.FCLOSE(l_file_handle);
        END IF;
        IF UTL_FILE.IS_OPEN(l_log_handle) THEN
          UTL_FILE.FCLOSE(l_log_handle);
        END IF;
        ROLLBACK;
        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
          -20099,
          'File processor error: ' || SQLERRM
        );
    END xxcust_file_processor;
    /
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
SECTION AA — COMPLETE EBS TROUBLESHOOTING GUIDE
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
Topic 15: Complete EBS Troubleshooting Knowledge
knowledgeId: TROUBLE-001
moduleCode: TECH
topicName: Oracle EBS Complete Troubleshooting Guide
topicCategory: TROUBLESHOOTING
difficulty: EXPERT
learningObjectives: [
  Diagnose and resolve AP invoice issues,
  Troubleshoot GL posting failures,
  Resolve workflow stuck notifications,
  Fix concurrent program failures,
  Troubleshoot interface errors,
  Resolve period close issues,
  Debug custom PL/SQL code,
  Troubleshoot performance issues,
  Resolve HRMS DateTrack errors,
  Fix inventory transaction errors
]
troubleshootingGuide: |
  ═══════════════════════════════════════
  ISSUE 1: AP INVOICE STUCK ON HOLD
  ═══════════════════════════════════════
  Symptom:
  Invoice cannot be paid due to holds
  Diagnosis Query:
  SELECT
    ah.hold_lookup_code,
    ah.hold_date,
    ah.held_by,
    ah.hold_reason,
    ah.release_lookup_code,
    ah.release_date,
    ah.released_by,
    -- Hold details from lookup
    fl.meaning AS hold_meaning,
    fl.description AS hold_description
  FROM
    ap_holds_all ah,
    fnd_lookups fl
  WHERE
    ah.invoice_id = :p_invoice_id
    AND fl.lookup_type = 'HOLD CODE'
    AND fl.lookup_code = ah.hold_lookup_code
  ORDER BY
    ah.hold_date;
  Resolution Steps:
  ─────────────────
  1. QTY ORD Hold:
     - Check PO quantity vs invoice quantity
     - Either increase PO quantity or
       reduce invoice quantity
     - Navigate: PO > Purchase Orders >
       Shipments > Quantities
  2. PRICE Hold:
     - Check PO price vs invoice price
     - Update PO price or create price
       tolerance override
     - Navigate: AP > Invoice > Tolerances
  3. DIST VARIANCE Hold:
     - Distributions must equal header amount
     - Check: SELECT SUM(amount)
       FROM ap_invoice_distributions_all
       WHERE invoice_id = :p_invoice_id
       AND reversal_flag != 'Y'
     - Fix distribution amounts
  4. NO RATE Hold:
     - Missing exchange rate for currency
     - Navigate: GL > Setup > Currencies >
       Rates > Daily
     - Enter rate for invoice date
  5. INVALID ACCOUNT Hold:
     - GL account disabled or invalid
     - Check: SELECT enabled_flag
       FROM gl_code_combinations
       WHERE code_combination_id = :p_ccid
     - Enable account in COA
  6. FUTURE PERIOD Hold:
     - GL date in future period
     - Change GL date to open period
     - Or open the future period
  Release Hold Query:
  UPDATE ap_holds_all
  SET    release_lookup_code = 'MANUAL RELEASE',
         release_date        = SYSDATE,
         released_by         = FND_GLOBAL.USER_ID,
         last_update_date    = SYSDATE,
         last_updated_by     = FND_GLOBAL.USER_ID
  WHERE  invoice_id          = :p_invoice_id
  AND    hold_lookup_code     = :p_hold_code
  AND    release_lookup_code  IS NULL;
  ═══════════════════════════════════════
  ISSUE 2: GL JOURNAL NOT POSTING
  ═══════════════════════════════════════
  Symptom:
  Journal status remains UNPOSTED or
  posting program errors
  Diagnosis Queries:
  -- Check journal status
  SELECT
    gjh.name,
    gjh.status,
    gjh.posting_run_id,
    gjh.unreservation_packet_id,
    gjh.je_source,
    gjh.je_category,
    gjh.period_name,
    gjh.running_total_dr,
    gjh.running_total_cr,
    (gjh.running_total_dr -
     gjh.running_total_cr) AS variance
  FROM
    gl_je_headers gjh
  WHERE
    gjh.je_header_id = :p_je_header_id;
  -- Check for posting errors
  SELECT
    gpe.je_header_id,
    gpe.je_line_num,
    gpe.error_code,
    gpe.error_message,
    gpe.creation_date
  FROM
    gl_posting_interim gpe
  WHERE
    gpe.je_header_id = :p_je_header_id
  ORDER BY
    gpe.je_line_num;
    -- Check period status (continued)
  SELECT
    gps.period_name,
    gps.period_year,
    gps.period_num,
    gps.closing_status,
    gps.effective_period_num
  FROM
    gl_period_statuses gps,
    gl_ledgers gl
  WHERE
    gps.ledger_id     = gl.ledger_id
    AND gl.ledger_id      = :p_ledger_id
    AND gps.period_name   = :p_period_name
    AND gps.application_id = 101;
  -- Check suspense account setup
  SELECT
    gl.name AS ledger_name,
    gl.enable_suspense_flag,
    gl.suspense_ccid,
    gcc.concatenated_segments AS suspense_account
  FROM
    gl_ledgers gl,
    gl_code_combinations_kfv gcc
  WHERE
    gl.ledger_id = :p_ledger_id
    AND gl.suspense_ccid = gcc.code_combination_id(+);
  Resolution Steps:
  ─────────────────
  1. Period Closed:
     - Open period in GL calendar
     - Navigate: GL > Setup > Open/Close Periods
  2. Debit Credit Imbalance:
     - Fix journal lines to balance
     - SELECT SUM(entered_dr) - SUM(entered_cr)
       FROM gl_je_lines
       WHERE je_header_id = :p_je_header_id
  3. Invalid Account:
     - Enable account in COA setup
     - Navigate: GL > Setup > Accounts >
       Code Combinations
  4. Budget Control Failure:
     - Check budget vs actual
     - Override if authorized
     - Navigate: GL > Budgets > Enter Budgets
  5. Cross Validation Rule Failure:
     - Check CVR setup
     - Fix account combination
     - Navigate: GL > Setup > Validation >
       Cross-Validation Rules
  ═══════════════════════════════════════
  ISSUE 3: WORKFLOW STUCK NOTIFICATION
  ═══════════════════════════════════════
  Symptom:
  Approval notification not received or
  workflow stuck in ERROR status
  Diagnosis Queries:
  -- Find stuck workflow activities
  SELECT
    wias.item_type,
    wias.item_key,
    wias.activity_status,
    wias.activity_result,
    wa.display_name AS activity_name,
    wias.assigned_user,
    wias.begin_date,
    TRUNC(SYSDATE) - TRUNC(wias.begin_date)
                              AS days_stuck,
    wias.notification_id
  FROM
    wf_item_activity_statuses wias,
    wf_activities_vl wa
  WHERE
    wias.process_activity = wa.instance_id
    AND wias.activity_status IN (
          'NOTIFIED','ERROR','ACTIVE')
    AND wias.item_type = :p_item_type
    AND TRUNC(SYSDATE) -
        TRUNC(wias.begin_date) > 1
  ORDER BY
    wias.begin_date;
  -- Check notification details
  SELECT
    wn.notification_id,
    wn.status,
    wn.sent_date,
    wn.due_date,
    wn.recipient_role,
    wn.subject,
    wn.mail_status,
    wn.original_recipient,
    wn.from_role,
    wn.item_type,
    wn.item_key
  FROM
    wf_notifications wn
  WHERE
    wn.notification_id = :p_notification_id;
  -- Check workflow errors
  SELECT
    we.item_type,
    we.item_key,
    we.activity_label,
    we.error_name,
    we.error_message,
    SUBSTR(we.error_stack,1,500)
                              AS error_stack,
    we.error_date
  FROM
    wf_errors we
  WHERE
    we.item_type = :p_item_type
    AND we.item_key  = :p_item_key
  ORDER BY
    we.error_date DESC;
  Resolution Steps:
  ─────────────────
  1. Notification Not Received:
     -- Check role email address
     SELECT wr.name,
            wr.display_name,
            wr.email_address,
            wr.status
     FROM   wf_roles wr
     WHERE  wr.name = :p_role_name;
     -- Update email if missing
     UPDATE wf_local_roles
     SET    email_address = :p_email
     WHERE  name = :p_role_name;
     COMMIT;
     -- Resend notification
     BEGIN
       wf_notification.send(
         nid => :p_notification_id
       );
       COMMIT;
     END;
  2. Workflow in ERROR Status:
     -- Retry workflow activity
     BEGIN
       wf_engine.handleerror(
         itemtype => :p_item_type,
         itemkey  => :p_item_key,
         activity => :p_activity_label,
         command  => 'RETRY',
         result   => NULL
       );
       COMMIT;
     END;
  3. Reassign Notification:
     BEGIN
       wf_notification.transfer(
         nid      => :p_notification_id,
         new_role => :p_new_approver
       );
       COMMIT;
     END;
  4. Cancel and Restart Workflow:
     BEGIN
       -- Cancel existing process
       wf_engine.abortprocess(
         itemtype => :p_item_type,
         itemkey  => :p_item_key
       );
       -- Restart based on module
       -- AP: ap_workflow_pkg.start_workflow
       -- PO: po_approval_pkg.start_workflow
       COMMIT;
     END;
  ═══════════════════════════════════════
  ISSUE 4: CONCURRENT PROGRAM FAILURE
  ═══════════════════════════════════════
  Symptom:
  Concurrent program completes with
  ERROR or WARNING status
  Diagnosis Queries:
  -- Check concurrent request status
  SELECT
    fcr.request_id,
    fcr.concurrent_program_id,
    fcp.concurrent_program_name,
    fcpt.user_concurrent_program_name,
    fcr.status_code,
    fcr.phase_code,
    fcr.completion_text,
    fcr.actual_start_date,
    fcr.actual_completion_date,
    ROUND(
      (fcr.actual_completion_date -
       fcr.actual_start_date) * 24 * 60,
    2)                        AS runtime_minutes,
    fcr.requested_by,
    fu.user_name,
    fcr.argument_text
  FROM
    fnd_concurrent_requests    fcr,
    fnd_concurrent_programs    fcp,
    fnd_concurrent_programs_tl fcpt,
    fnd_user                   fu
  WHERE
    fcr.concurrent_program_id = fcp.concurrent_program_id
    AND fcp.concurrent_program_id
                              = fcpt.concurrent_program_id
    AND fcpt.language         = 'US'
    AND fcr.requested_by      = fu.user_id
    AND fcr.request_id        = :p_request_id;
    -- Check log file location (continued)
  SELECT
    fcr.request_id,
    fcr.logfile_name,
    fcr.outfile_name,
    fcr.logfile_node_name,
    fcr.outfile_node_name,
    fcr.status_code,
    fcr.completion_text
  FROM
    fnd_concurrent_requests fcr
  WHERE
    fcr.request_id = :p_request_id;
  -- Check running concurrent programs
  SELECT
    fcr.request_id,
    fcpt.user_concurrent_program_name,
    fcr.phase_code,
    fcr.status_code,
    fcr.actual_start_date,
    ROUND(
      (SYSDATE - fcr.actual_start_date) *
      24 * 60, 2)             AS running_minutes,
    fcr.requested_by,
    fu.user_name,
    fcr.argument_text
  FROM
    fnd_concurrent_requests    fcr,
    fnd_concurrent_programs_tl fcpt,
    fnd_user                   fu
  WHERE
    fcr.concurrent_program_id = fcpt.concurrent_program_id
    AND fcpt.language          = 'US'
    AND fcr.requested_by       = fu.user_id
    AND fcr.phase_code         = 'R'
    AND fcr.status_code        = 'R'
  ORDER BY
    fcr.actual_start_date;
  -- Check manager status
  SELECT
    fcm.concurrent_queue_name,
    fcm.manager_type,
    fcm.running_processes,
    fcm.max_processes,
    fcm.cache_size,
    fcm.diagnostic_level,
    fct.user_concurrent_queue_name
  FROM
    fnd_concurrent_queues      fcm,
    fnd_concurrent_queues_tl   fct
  WHERE
    fcm.concurrent_queue_id = fct.concurrent_queue_id
    AND fct.language         = 'US'
    AND fcm.running_processes > 0
  ORDER BY
    fcm.running_processes DESC;
  Resolution Steps:
  ─────────────────
  1. Program Errors with ORA- Error:
     - Review log file for ORA error
     - Common errors:
       ORA-01555: Snapshot too old
         → Increase UNDO_RETENTION
         → Run during off-peak hours
       ORA-04031: Shared pool memory
         → Flush shared pool
         → ALTER SYSTEM FLUSH SHARED_POOL
       ORA-01652: Temp space
         → Add temp tablespace
         → Clean up temp segments
  2. Program Pending Too Long:
     -- Check if manager has capacity
     SELECT fcm.running_processes,
            fcm.max_processes,
            (fcm.max_processes -
             fcm.running_processes) AS available
     FROM   fnd_concurrent_queues fcm
     WHERE  fcm.concurrent_queue_name =
            'STANDARD';
     -- Increase max processes if needed
     -- Navigate: System Administrator >
     -- Concurrent > Manager > Define
  3. Program in Running Status Too Long:
     -- Check for blocking locks
     SELECT s.sid,
            s.serial#,
            s.username,
            s.program,
            s.status,
            s.sql_id,
            l.type AS lock_type,
            l.mode_held
     FROM   v$session s,
            v$lock l
     WHERE  s.sid = l.sid
     AND    l.block > 0;
     -- Kill blocking session if needed
     ALTER SYSTEM KILL SESSION
       ':p_sid,:p_serial' IMMEDIATE;
  ═══════════════════════════════════════
  ISSUE 5: INTERFACE IMPORT FAILURES
  ═══════════════════════════════════════
  Symptom:
  Open interface import program
  completes but records remain
  in interface table with errors
  Diagnosis Queries:
  -- AP Invoice Interface Errors
  SELECT
    aii.invoice_id,
    aii.invoice_num,
    aii.vendor_id,
    aii.invoice_amount,
    aii.org_id,
    aii.source,
